## Objective

Freeze and validate the already-composed `07a–c` default persistent session-port path, then prove
the complete authorized transaction, terminal cleanup ledger, mutually exclusive race contract,
and fail-closed harness teardown on a real local helper/PTY/relay/tmux path. The implementation
baseline already selects the reviewed factory/helper/relay seams; this leaf must not reimplement
them or manufacture a product RED from their former absence.

This leaf introduces no new product capability, product protocol, identity source, handshake,
canonicalization rule, or deletion authority. Product code may change only when an observable
behavioral race test proves a defect in the composed path.

Only an independently reviewed `D_0_7D` result satisfies the process prerequisite consumed by
`D_0_1_SPLICE`. Reviews of `07a`, `07b`, or `07c` alone do not unblock the splice.

## Scope

- Consolidate the complementary GREEN default-factory evidence into one dedicated acceptance
  transaction with literal provider argv, exact prompt result, exact 24-byte snapshot, and exact
  observation DTO. Do not claim that this exact single transaction already exists.
- Add an observable replacement-identity attack against isolated outer fixture teardown and
  make the harness preserve any replacement harness workspace, harness tmux socket, or harness
  tmux server child without signalling, invoking a mutating tmux teardown command, or
  performing any path-deletion operation. This assertion does not prohibit the frozen product
  cleanup from retiring an exactly sealed port-owned pane/session through its retained shared-
  server connection.
- Keep the reviewed issuer/codec, PTY/verified-write endpoint, authenticated relay/tmux
  observation, revocation, and ordinary supervisor settlement in their frozen order. Change
  their production implementation only if a new race test demonstrates a real behavioral
  failure.
- Exercise the real-host cancel/exit/identity/short-write/terminal/relay/helper/control race
  matrix, including cancel intent followed by proof-response loss, helper death before the
  first PTY byte, relay disappearance during snapshot, and helper/control loss after a partial
  or full terminal write but before the sideband response.
- Prove every component-owned retained descriptor closes, every cleanup target is terminal
  `RETIRED` or `PRESERVED`, and ordinary completion waits for that ledger rather than an empty
  filesystem namespace.
- Prove the ordinary accepted path records the pre-`ACCEPT` consumed relay key `RETIRED` and,
  absent external removal, retains the relay socket pathname and runtime directory through
  settlement as terminal `PRESERVED` entries, with zero post-`V` product `unlink`/`rmdir`.
- Prove same-name namespace replacements survive; exact process and port-owned pane/session
  targets follow their sealed `RETIRED`/`PRESERVED` outcomes; permitted survivors are recorded;
  and the shared tmux server/socket plus unrelated sibling sessions always survive.
- Prove the new path is absent from one-shot execution and that no public adapter/service splice
  or API change entered the leaves.

## Non-scope

- No `agent_service`, Codex/Claude adapter, catalog, schema, session repository, public response,
  policy, migration, package, dependency, release manifest, lockfile, workflow, or release
  change. The only authorized CI metadata change is checkpoint 1's mechanical replacement of
  `ci/suites.json:test.gateway.inventorySha256` from
  `sha256:f6bf19a001a01341c9dad63ab55f21bfd7b68571565b061f42b4a0e097f3922e`
  (126 paths on `07556fe`) with
  `sha256:1dd0cc26a0f8c165e4ff536641acf470b7b6c7ad17342450027cdaa9fff2431d`
  (127 paths) after adding the dedicated test. Its include/exclude/argv/classification/skip
  contract stays byte-identical; `ci/suites-contract.json`, CI scripts, and workflows do not
  change. Hosted-CI provisioning for the external pinned source archive and custom runtime is a
  later release-enablement concern; this leaf makes no hosted or release claim.
- No live Codex/Claude call, provider network, Redis, PostgreSQL, shared MCP mutation, or
  promotion claim. The unchanged
  `gateway/vendor/tmux-agents/build-offline.sh` is a frozen source recipe only: this leaf
  reproduces its pinned image/hash/patch/configure/parser/version semantics but never invokes
  that pathname-based script. The authenticated candidate coordinator emits only the bounded
  USTAR input; the reviewer custody parent validates it independently and alone invokes the
  exact ownership-probe/build argvs plus their bounded inspect/wait/remove/reconciliation
  operations. The build container is not a test/runtime service and
  receives no Docker socket, credential, repository-write, bind mount, or provider mount.
- No change to any shared rule frozen in [`D/0/07`](07.md); a discovered contract defect returns
  to plan review rather than being decided in implementation.
- No production janitor, namespace-retention period, basename-reuse protocol, or deletion policy.
  The fixture owner and outer real-host teardown have no path-deletion authority. Only kernel
  disposal of their transient tmpfs/mount namespace may reclaim paths after preservation
  evidence; it remains outside product/session-port cleanup.
- No host-root `/fixture`, ambient `/tmp/never`, inherited user tmux socket/configuration,
  provider credential, shared service, or network use. A test fixture path is repository-owned
  and its interpreter is an explicitly configured absolute path.

## TDD RED

Focused acceptance suite:
`tests/gateway/process_supervisor_session_port.test.js`.

The first failing test is:

> `preserves replaced fixture identities without signalling tmux or deleting the replacements`

RED preparation may extract the existing relay-test teardown behind a test-only injected
operations seam, without changing its behavior. The test seals the original private workspace,
relay socket, and tmux-server identity; substitutes same-name identities; invokes teardown; and
asserts:

```js
assert.deepEqual(teardown, {
  outcome: "PRESERVED",
  workspaceReplacementSurvives: true,
  socketReplacementSurvives: true,
  serverReplacementSurvives: true,
  harnessSignalCalls: 0,
  harnessKillSessionCalls: 0,
  harnessKillServerCalls: 0,
  harnessTmuxMutationCalls: 0,
  harnessUnlinkCalls: 0,
  harnessRmdirCalls: 0,
  harnessRenameCalls: 0,
  harnessRecursiveRemoveCalls: 0,
});
```

The pre-GREEN PTY/relay harnesses are RED because they resolve cleanup by pathname, invoke
mutating tmux `kill-session`/`kill-server` commands, and recursively remove the workspace
without revalidating the seal. The RED uses injected recording operations and a private
temporary namespace: it sends no real signal, deletes no replacement, starts no provider/tmux
process, and records every actual call before the assertion fails. An absent helper or a
source-text assertion is not an acceptable RED.

Run the same oracle after complete setup, after a startup failure between server spawn and socket
readiness, and after a teardown operation reports failure. In every phase, a substituted
identity survives; evidence records both the original seal and the replacement's different
device/inode/start-token identity. The mismatch grants no retry, fallback mutation, or broader
cleanup authority.

The second mandatory RED closes the final-component check/use race:

> `preserves a final-entry replacement installed after the last teardown observation`

The injected operations seam pauses immediately after the harness's last identity observation
and immediately before the old cleanup would perform its first path effect. The test installs a
different final entry, resumes teardown, and requires this exact oracle:

```js
assert.deepEqual(finalEntryRace, {
  outcome: "PRESERVED",
  replacementSurvives: true,
  harnessUnlinkCalls: 0,
  harnessUnlinkatCalls: 0,
  harnessRmdirCalls: 0,
  harnessRenameCalls: 0,
  harnessRecursiveRemoveCalls: 0,
});
```

The pre-GREEN extracted teardown selects the replacement for one forbidden path effect. The
recording seam records that call while suppressing the effect, so the zero-call assertion fails
and the replacement survives without real mutation. A pre-call `lstat`/`fstatat` recheck,
retained parent dirfd, or matching device/inode observation cannot make this test GREEN: none
binds an `unlinkat(dirfd, basename, ...)` effect to the observed final object.

The authorized transaction is not the RED for this leaf. Existing tests separately prove the
`ready\nstatus\nack:status\n` 24-byte render and a combined default-helper write-sequence-1 /
snapshot-sequence-2 path, but do not yet prove those exact values in one transaction. The
dedicated suite consolidates them through the real configured helper, PTY, authenticated relay,
and ephemeral custom-tmux target; it is expected to become GREEN with fixture/test work against
unchanged product code. The deterministic repository fixture is direct-executed with this exact
shape:

```text
[
  <configured-absolute-python>,
  <absolute-repository-fixture>,
  "--literal",
  "$(touch /tmp/never)"
]
```

The test records the resolved first two absolute paths, proves the metacharacter argument remains
one literal argv element, and proves the namespace-private `/tmp/never` is absent. The helper
must use direct `execve`; no shell and no tmux `send-keys` are permitted. In transaction mode
the fixture writes `ready\n`, reads exactly `status\r`, then writes
`status\nack:status\n`. The exact public oracles remain:

```js
assert.deepEqual(Reflect.ownKeys(execution), [
  "mode", "bindingDigest", "supervisor", "reaper",
  "utilityIdentity", "completion", "cancel",
]);
assert.deepEqual(await issued.port.writePrompt({ prompt: "status" }), {
  sequence: 1,
  acceptedBytes: 6,
});
assert.deepEqual(await issued.port.snapshot({}), {
  sequence: 2,
  snapshot: "ready\nstatus\nack:status\n",
  snapshotBytes: 24,
  truncated: false,
});
assert.deepEqual(issued.observation, {
  sessionId: "ag-red-port-codex-coder",
  tmuxTarget: "ag-red-port-codex-coder",
  attachCommand: "tmux attach -t ag-red-port-codex-coder",
});
```

Record each transaction sub-assertion independently so one failure cannot suppress later
evidence. A transaction failure is a regression or a newly demonstrated product defect; it is
not evidence that composition was absent on the declared baseline.

## TDD GREEN

Replace pathname-authoritative teardown with one long-lived test-only Python fixture-owner
process shared by the PTY, relay, and dedicated acceptance suites through a bounded,
length-delimited test command channel over inherited private pipes. This channel is harness-
internal, accepts only fixed fixture operations, and does not alter `ASP1`, `ASR1`, or any
product/runtime protocol. The owner opens workspace/socket parent dirfds with
no-follow semantics, records realpath/device/inode/owner/mode, directly spawns
`tmux -f /dev/null -S <private-socket> -D`, and binds a Linux pidfd immediately around that
unreaped child using the pidfd-first, two-read direct-child binding modeled on
`_bind_root_process_handle` at `scripts/ci_gate.py:980-1014`. It holds those handles until
evidence and teardown finish.

The retained dirfds are evidence handles only; they authorize no path mutation. The fixture
owner and outer real-host teardown never call `unlink`, `unlinkat`, `rmdir`, rename, or recursive
removal on any success, mismatch, startup-failure, partial-setup, teardown-failure, or fallback
branch. There is therefore no last-observation-to-delete check/use interval. Signal delivery is
the sole destructive outer-harness operation: it targets only the directly spawned server
through its bound pidfd, never a re-resolved PID/PGID, after the pidfd proves that exact child is
still live. Retained fds may close. A mismatch produces terminal `PRESERVED` and performs no
mutation.

The GREEN outer harness does not use tmux `kill-session`, tmux `kill-server`, any other mutating
tmux teardown command, or any path-deletion primitive for real-host teardown. Every writable
fixture/build staging path is created on the transient unit's private tmpfs inside the isolated
mount namespace; the builder streams its verified output into that tmpfs rather than requiring a
host source/output staging directory. After preservation evidence is durably emitted, the owner
closes its retained fds and returns a closure confirmation that the wrapper appends to durable
evidence. Only then is kernel disposal of that tmpfs/mount namespace the sole path-reclamation
mechanism. That disposal is not a product or fixture-cleanup success and no acceptance assertion
depends on absence afterward.
These outer-harness restrictions do not change the frozen product rule: product cleanup may
retire an exactly sealed port-owned pane/session through the retained shared-server connection,
but may never `kill-server` or re-resolve the public target.

Add only the repository-fixture transaction mode and focused acceptance/race cases required by
this sheet. Keep the ordinary `createProcessSupervisor(...)` and execution result unchanged. If
all product-facing tests are already GREEN, the valid implementation is test/fixture/harness
only. If a mandatory behavioral race is RED, name the exact failure in the handoff and make the
smallest production change that fixes that test without changing the frozen parent contract.

## Trial 5 executable reviewer authority contract

Design Trial 4 is immutable KO evidence at
`2d9470baa63e27a08f3fe8b5e8bb0f4e176a3eab`. Its non-root tmpfs correction is
closed and remains normative. Trial 5 changes exactly the three mechanisms rejected by that
result: pre-release launch and held-inode custody, closed-schema independent evidence, and
daemon-owned Docker lifecycle/reconciliation. It adds no product behavior, candidate path, CI
path, deletion authority, release claim, or implementation authority.

| Trial 4 P1 | Trial 5 binding correction |
|---|---|
| P1-1 — no executable pre-release/cgroup identity, pathname-reopened output, unspecified `close_range`, and contradictory `--collect` | exact reviewer-only unit shim/control socket, `bwrap --block-fd 3`, `--ro-bind-fd 4`, byte-frozen fd-seal shim, no `--collect`, `--remain-after-exit`, and a complete launch/exit/cleanup state machine |
| P1-2 — D7C2 and reviewer authority were open prose | byte-frozen canonical protocol spec plus independent stdlib oracle/attack driver, exact schemas/order/caps, and mechanically decided nine-attack RED/GREEN |
| P1-3 — Docker client death could leave an unidentified/unbounded daemon container | exact parent-owned name/labels/cidfile, resource flags, bounded inspect/wait/TERM/KILL/remove state machine, race normalization, and fixed success/failure transcripts |

The only normative reviewer-authority version after Trial 5 is
`D007D_REVIEW_CUSTODY_V3`. The later Trial 4 launch, D7C2, and two-call Docker prose is retained
only as the historical attack fixture. The LF-exact `D007D_USTAR_PARSER_V2` and
`D007D_CONTAINER_PROGRAM_V2` blocks are incorporated unchanged into V3 and remain normative.
The independent parser oracle must still accept the one canonical archive and reject exactly
21/21 individually generated mutations: bad magic, invalid octal digit, base-256 size,
alternate octal terminator, bad checksum, wrong size, nonzero padding, duplicate, reorder, extra
member, PAX, GNU longname, symlink, hardlink, sparse type, third zero block, trailing byte,
header truncation, payload truncation, one zero block, and empty archive. A different rejection
count or a parser/oracle exception is failure, not partial evidence.

### Immutable extraction and provenance

An independent reviewer extracts the three marked Trial 6 artifacts from the exact independently
approved design commit with `git cat-file blob <approved-07d-blob>`, never from a candidate
checkout. A reviewer-owned LF-preserving extractor accepts exactly one begin/end marker pair,
removes the blank line and Markdown fence only, preserves the final LF inside the fence, and
writes with `O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW|O_CLOEXEC` below a newly created mode-`0700` directory.
`REVIEW_ROOT` is exactly
`/run/user/<reviewer-decimal-uid>/ao-d7/<24-lowerhex-nonce>` below the no-follow, reviewer-owned
mode-`0700` user runtime directory; `AUTHORITY_DIR` is its mode-`0700` child `a`. Existing roots,
symlinks, a UID mismatch, or a control path over 100 encoded bytes reject. The paths and modes
are exactly:

```text
<REVIEW_ROOT>/a/d007d-custody-spec-v4.json  0400
<REVIEW_ROOT>/a/d007d-custody-oracle-v4.py 0500
<REVIEW_ROOT>/a/d007d-fd-seal-v3.py        0500
```

The reviewer opens all three no-follow, records design commit/tree/blob plus file device/inode/
owner/mode/size/SHA-256, retains their fds, and rehashes them before and after every run. The
candidate receives none of these host paths. The fd-seal file is exposed only through a
reviewer-held fd and `bwrap --ro-bind-fd` at `/authority/d007d-fd-seal-v3.py`. Candidate code,
future candidate tests, candidate digests, and coordination messages cannot select or attest any
authority artifact. A missing digest, wrong byte, wrong mode, or unavailable required syscall is
`infrastructure_unavailable`, never an implementation fallback.

Trial 6 selects one executable authority: the extracted V4 oracle is the parent/launcher, unit
shim, parser/archive-sink owner, state/evidence owner, Docker recorder/reconciler, and attack
driver. There is no fourth candidate-implemented or reviewer-selected executable. Its only
production entry is `[PYTHON,AUTHORITY_PARENT,"run",MODE,ODB,CANDIDATE_SHA,CANDIDATE_TREE,
SOURCE_ARCHIVE,HOST_TOOLCHAIN,HOST_DEPS,DOCKER,EXPECTATION_LEDGER,REVIEW_BASE]`; an unlisted
argument, executable, transition, or inherited descriptor is rejection.

The normative protocol/state-machine recipe follows.

Mechanical extraction of the 5,480 LF-terminated JSON-fence bytes has SHA-256
`01970f6227ab9eb487515201d2811ae6efba9f07023b69884ce4a10384d49639`; the reviewer recomputes it and requires `json.load` plus the
independent semantic assertions below to pass.

### Closed D7C2/3 contract

Every frame header is exactly bytes `0..3 = D7C2`, byte `4 = 0x03`, byte `5 = type`, bytes
`6..7 = 0x0000`, and bytes `8..11 = unsigned big-endian payload length`. Types outside `1..4`,
zero-length JSON frames, length/cumulative/count overflow, short header/payload, invalid UTF-8,
duplicate JSON keys, noncanonical JSON, a frame after the mode's terminal frame, or any byte after
the required kernel EOF rejects the run. JSON bytes are canonical only when identical to
`json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=True, allow_nan=False, sort_keys=True,
separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")`; objects must be plain maps, numbers must be integers,
and booleans never satisfy an integer field.

The separate diagnostic socket accepts at most 16,777,216 arbitrary bytes and actual EOF. The
parent records only byte count and SHA-256 plus a bounded escaped reviewer diagnostic; it never
projects candidate bytes as authority. Reaching the cap before EOF, a retained writer, or a read
error rejects the run and enters unit cleanup.

Type `1` is `test-summary` and has exactly these keys, with no extension keys:

| Key | Exact value/type and bound |
|---|---|
| `candidateSha` | lowercase 40-hex equal to the parent-held candidate |
| `mode` | equal to the parent-selected value from the eleven-value frozen mode enum |
| `phase` | literal `test` |
| `tests`, `pass`, `fail`, `skipped`, `cancelled` | JSON integers `0..1000000`; `tests = pass + fail + skipped + cancelled = len(names)` |
| `names` | execution-order array of `1..512` distinct strings; each UTF-8 length `1..200`, no control byte, and grammar `[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9 _./:()'=-]*` |
| `namesSha256` | lowercase 64-hex SHA-256 of each name encoded as two-byte big-endian length then UTF-8 bytes, concatenated in array order |
| `tapBytes` | integer `1..2097152`, equal to the concatenated type-3 `data` UTF-8 byte length |
| `tapSha256` | lowercase 64-hex SHA-256 of those exact concatenated bytes |

The parent independently parses the concatenated TAP bytes, requires one terminal root plan and
one contiguous terminal summary, rejects bailout, duplicate/non-sequential id, plan/summary
mismatch, a second header, nonterminal plan/summary, and every trailing TAP record or byte, and
proves its totals and ordered root-result names equal type `1` and the reviewer-owned expectation
ledger. The only permitted bytes after `# todo 0` are either immediate EOF or one final Node
`# duration_ms <nonnegative-decimal>` line and EOF. Candidate-reported totals alone are never
evidence.

Type `2` is `fixture-ledger` and has exactly `candidateSha`, `entries`, `ledgerSha256`, `mode`,
and `phase`; the first two identity fields match type `1` and `phase` is `test`. `entries` has
`0..128` objects sorted strictly by `(kind,name)`, with no duplicate pair. Every entry has exactly
`dev`, `ino`, `kind`, `name`, `outcome`, and `startToken`: `dev` and `ino` are integers
`0..9223372036854775807`; `kind` is one of `workspace`, `relayKey`, `relaySocket`,
`runtimeDirectory`, `tmuxServer`, `tmuxSession`, `process`, `descriptor`; `name` is printable
ASCII length `1..128`; `outcome` is `RETIRED` or `PRESERVED`; and `startToken` is lowercase
64-hex or JSON `null` only for non-process/non-server filesystem entries. `ledgerSha256` is the
SHA-256 of the canonical JSON bytes of the `entries` array alone. The parent compares required
entries/outcomes with the reviewer-owned expectation ledger and its held identity observations.

Type `3` is `tap-chunk` and has exactly `candidateSha`, `channel`, `data`, `mode`, `phase`, and
`seq`. Identity fields match type `1`, `channel` is literal `tap`, `phase` is `test`, `seq` is an
integer starting at zero and increasing by one, and `data` is a nonempty valid UTF-8 string with
no NUL and encoded length at most 32768. There are `1..64` chunks. Candidate diagnostics use the
separate capped stderr socket and can never be a type-3 authority record.

Type `4` is raw USTAR, occurs exactly once only in `archive` phase, is `1..268435456` bytes, and
is immediately followed by kernel EOF. Before launch the parent creates exactly one anonymous
`memfd_create("d007d-archive-v3", MFD_CLOEXEC|MFD_ALLOW_SEALING)` fd, verifies size zero and no
pathname, and streams the bounded frame into it in at-most-65,536-byte writes. After EOF it
`fchmod(0400)`, validates through the unchanged independent parser by `pread`, applies and reads
back `F_SEAL_SEAL|F_SEAL_SHRINK|F_SEAL_GROW|F_SEAL_WRITE`, seeks the held fd to zero, and supplies
that fd directly as the sole stdin of exact Docker build operation `03`. No archive pathname,
host staging directory, bind mount, or second serialization exists. Archive phase accepts
exactly `[4, EOF]`; every test phase accepts
exactly `[3 x N, 1, 2, EOF]`. Bootstrap, unit, Docker-integration, focused, and full-CI test modes
all use the same order; an empty ledger is still a required type `2`. A second summary/ledger,
archive in a test phase, JSON in archive phase, missing frame, reordered frame, zero-length final
read without actual EOF, retained writer, or trailing frame rejects without `DONE`.

The reviewer-owned expectation ledger is canonical JSON with exactly `candidateSha`, `mode`,
`names`, `totals`, and `requiredLedger`. It is generated from the approved checkpoint request,
exclusive-created outside the candidate tree, hashed in that request's result, and supplied by
held fd; the candidate cannot name it. Candidate/mode/names use the exact type-1 types and bounds;
`totals` has exactly the five type-1 integer count keys and reconciles to names;
`requiredLedger` is a `0..128` strictly `(kind,name)`-sorted array whose entries have exactly
`kind`, `name`, and `outcome` with the type-2 enums/bounds. The frozen oracle validates this
schema before reading frame byte one, and each mode's request freezes the exact file hash.

`fixedMode` is the bare member of the eleven-value `MODE_ORDER`, never a CLI option. Under
`D007D_CANDIDATE_PROTOCOL=D7C2-v3`, both the authenticated unit-shim argv and the final candidate
argv carry that same bare byte string; `--bootstrap-probe`, `--cp1-unit-teardown`, and every other
dashed substitution are protocol mutants and reject. The parent also sets exactly
`D007D_CANDIDATE_SHA=<bindings.candidateSha>` inside the cleared bwrap environment. The D7C2
wrapper requires that value to be lowercase 40-hex and uses those exact bytes in every frame; it
never runs Git, opens `.git`, resolves `/odb` `HEAD`, or accepts a candidate identity from another
environment variable, pathname, report, or child. A missing or malformed binding rejects before
any frame. The candidate distinguishes phases only by the parent contract: absence of
`/inputs/tmux` means archive `[4,EOF]`; its presence means test `[3 x N,1,2,EOF]`. It receives no
phase flag, expectation bytes/path/fd/hash, or expected totals. Protocol-unset legacy invocations
retain their reviewed dashed argv and explicit worktree-local `git -C "$D007D_CANDIDATE_ROOT"
rev-parse HEAD` behavior; legacy derivation is unreachable under D7C2-v3 and is not a fallback.

Every test phase, including `bootstrap-probe`, `docker-integration`, and `full-ci`, emits at least
one type-3 TAP chunk followed by one type-1 summary and one type-2 ledger. Unit and `focused`
modes read the actual serial Node stdout into one bounded byte buffer before parsing: reject zero
bytes or more than 2,097,152 bytes; decode once with fatal UTF-8; require byte-for-byte UTF-8
round-trip equality; and apply the exact terminal root-TAP grammar above. Chunking walks decoded
Unicode scalar boundaries and chooses the largest nonempty prefix whose original UTF-8 bytes are
at most 32,768, producing at most 64 chunks. Concatenating each chunk's UTF-8 bytes must reproduce
the original buffer exactly; `tapBytes` and `tapSha256` bind that original buffer, never a decoded,
replacement-character, or re-encoded normalization. The three non-TAP
commands use exactly one synthetic result name: `bootstrap-probe: authenticated candidate
dispatch`, `docker-integration: held tmux verification`, or `full-ci: scripts/ci.sh`. Only the
completed child/check result chooses `ok` versus `not ok`; exit `0` derives `1/1/0/0/0`, ordinary
exit `1` derives `1/0/1/0/0`, and signal, timeout, malformed output, or any other exit suppresses
the terminal frames and rejects the domain. The canonical synthetic TAP bytes are exactly
`TAP version 13\n`, the derived `ok|not ok 1 - <name>\n`, `1..1\n`, then `# tests 1\n`,
`# pass <0|1>\n`, `# fail <0|1>\n`, `# cancelled 0\n`, `# skipped 0\n`, and `# todo 0\n`.
Nothing may turn a planned name, an expected total, or a missing child report into run evidence.

Checkpoint 1 freezes the complete candidate dispatch table below. It is implemented and tested in
the CP1 wrapper even though CP2/CP3 tests land only after their prerequisite checkpoint verdicts;
no later checkpoint may add, replace, or reinterpret a wrapper handler:

```text
CP1_TEARDOWN_PATTERN=^(preserves replaced fixture identities without signalling tmux or deleting the replacements|preserves a final-entry replacement installed after the last teardown observation)$
CP1_STREAM_PATTERN=^(serializes the exact three-member USTAR build input and raw binary output contract|rejects stream-build framing trailing bytes and noncanonical members)$
CP1_CUSTODY_PATTERN=^(rejects same-owner chmod restore across every read-only prerequisite|withholds evidence descriptor and paths from the candidate domain|validates build output by held inode after pathname replacement|kills a retained child and closes retained record descriptors|kills a TERM-resistant descendant at the outer deadline|revalidates held candidate and prerequisite bytes after a restore attempt|rejects malformed duplicate reordered padded and trailed USTAR before Docker|exposes only the declared mount map and rejects every prerequisite write|withholds Docker client socket and arbitrary daemon operations)$
CP2_UNIT_PATTERN=^authorized persistent session port writes one bounded prompt and returns the bounded current snapshot plus exact attach observation$
CP3_CANCEL_PATTERN=^returns SESSION_PORT_CANCELLED when snapshot cancel wins after B and before R$
CP3_ORDERING_PATTERN=^(returns SESSION_PORT_WRITE_ABORTED when cancel is requested first and its proof response is lost|returns SESSION_PORT_WRITE_ABORTED when the helper dies before F|returns SESSION_PORT_TERMINAL_CLOSED when the relay disappears during a snapshot)$
CP3_MATRIX_PATTERN=^d007d race matrix: [A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9 _./:()=-]*$
FOCUSED_FILES=[tests/gateway/process_supervisor_session_port.test.js,tests/gateway/process_supervisor_session_port_pty.test.js,tests/gateway/process_supervisor_session_port_relay.test.js]
```

| Bare mode | Frozen candidate handler / name source |
|---|---|
| `bootstrap-probe` | synthetic `bootstrap-probe: authenticated candidate dispatch` |
| `cp1-unit-custody` | one serial Node run with `CP1_CUSTODY_PATTERN` |
| `cp1-unit-teardown` | one serial Node run with `CP1_TEARDOWN_PATTERN` |
| `cp1-unit-stream` | one serial Node run with `CP1_STREAM_PATTERN` |
| `cp2-unit` | one serial Node run with `CP2_UNIT_PATTERN` |
| `cp3-unit-cancel` | one serial Node run with `CP3_CANCEL_PATTERN` |
| `cp3-unit-ordering` | one serial Node run with `CP3_ORDERING_PATTERN` |
| `cp3-unit-matrix` | one serial Node run with `CP3_MATRIX_PATTERN`; every remaining directed matrix test added by CP3 must have exactly that prefix, and the CP3 request freezes the nonempty ordered root-result set and totals produced by this fixed pattern |
| `docker-integration` | completed held-tmux verification, then synthetic `docker-integration: held tmux verification` |
| `focused` | one serial Node run over `FOCUSED_FILES` in that order, with actual root names/totals |
| `full-ci` | completed canonical CI report/allowlist check, then synthetic `full-ci: scripts/ci.sh` |

The four future handlers are dispatch capability, not advance implementation of CP2/CP3 tests.
At CP1 their fake-runner unit oracle must prove the exact bare mode reaches its own handler and
frames that same mode; it must not fabricate future test evidence or add those modes to the eleven
CP1 authority rows. Once CP1 is independently reviewed, CP2 may change only its two declared test/
fixture paths, and CP3 may not change the wrapper. A zero-match future handler, mismatched framed
mode, dashed alias, shared catch-all handler, or handler that reads expectation material rejects.

For every invocation the checkpoint request carries the complete canonical ledger bytes as one
ASCII JSON string, its decimal byte count, and SHA-256. The reviewer writes those exact bytes
with one `O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW|O_CLOEXEC` operation, no terminal or embedded LF, `fsync`s the
parent, `fchmod(0400)`, reopens no-follow, and requires regular file, reviewer uid, link count one,
exact bytes, and exact hash. A different serialization is not normalized. The V4 parent accepts
only that mode and hash-bound held file and never maps or forwards it into a candidate domain.

### Exact launch and FD state machine

The parent uses a new reviewer-only mode-`0700` control directory and binds one filesystem
`AF_UNIX/SOCK_SEQPACKET|SOCK_CLOEXEC` listener. It creates two
`socketpair(AF_UNIX, SOCK_STREAM|SOCK_CLOEXEC)` pairs for D7C2 and diagnostics and holds both
read ends. For test phase it also holds the already validated output inode. The control pathname is absolute ASCII, at most 100 encoded
bytes, exclusive-created below the held no-follow dirfd, and has mode `0600`; a pre-existing
entry or more than one accepted peer rejects. No candidate pathname is involved.

Before any child, the parent calls and verifies
`prctl(PR_SET_CHILD_SUBREAPER,1,0,0,0)`/`PR_GET_CHILD_SUBREAPER`, snapshots its direct children,
and requires `pidfd_open` plus start-token validation for every systemd, bwrap, Docker, wait,
inspect, kill, and remove client it creates. The service-manager child is never claimed as an
adopted direct child; its identity comes only from the authenticated unit MainPID/peer/cgroup
chain below.

The exact service entry is:

<!-- D007D_SYSTEMD_ARGV_V3_BEGIN -->

```text
[SYSTEMD_RUN,"--user","--wait","--remain-after-exit","--quiet",
 "--service-type=exec","--unit",UNIT,
 "--property","KillMode=control-group","--property","TimeoutStopSec=15s",
 "--property","TasksMax=128","--property","MemoryMax=2147483648",
 "--property","LimitCPU=600","--property","LimitAS=2147483648",
 "--property","LimitFSIZE=268435456","--property","LimitNOFILE=256",
 "--property","StandardInput=null","--property","StandardOutput=null",
 "--property","StandardError=null",
 ENV,"-i","LC_ALL=C","LANG=C","TZ=UTC",
 PYTHON,AUTHORITY_ORACLE,"unit-shim",
 CONTROL_SOCKET,MODE,CANDIDATE_SHA,NONCE]
```

<!-- D007D_SYSTEMD_ARGV_V3_END -->

The 602 LF-terminated bytes inside that text fence have SHA-256
`0c2161937b37aff5aa193c142d9efb96aa71551485fbfe7dadab1bbf58427d42`.

`ENV`, `PYTHON`, and `SYSTEMD_RUN` are separately held and authenticated absolute executables.
`UNIT` is exactly `d007d-v4-<first20(candidateSha)>-<24-lowerhex-nonce>.service`; the parent
rejects an existing unit or control path. `--collect` is forbidden. `--wait` deliberately keeps
the authenticated direct `systemd-run` child present, while `--remain-after-exit` keeps successful
unit properties queryable until the parent explicitly stops it. Failed units remain loaded until
the parent records and resets them.

The unit shim is the `unit-shim` mode of the byte-frozen oracle. It connects once, sends the
canonical exact-key object `{candidateSha,mode,nonce,pid,type:"HELLO"}`, and blocks. The parent accepts exactly one
peer and requires `SO_PEERCRED == (systemctl show MainPID, reviewer uid, reviewer gid)`, opens a
pidfd for that PID, requires its executable to be held `PYTHON` and its post-`env` NUL-delimited
argv to be exactly `[PYTHON,AUTHORITY_ORACLE,"unit-shim",CONTROL_SOCKET,MODE,CANDIDATE_SHA,NONCE]`,
authenticates its start token, requires its `/proc/PID/cgroup`
to equal the exact held `ControlGroup`, opens that cgroup directory and `cgroup.procs` no-follow,
and authenticates the still-live direct `systemd-run` child by pidfd/executable/argv/parent. Only
then does state advance `UNIT_SUBMITTED -> UNIT_BOUND`.

At `UNIT_BOUND`, the parent sends one `SCM_RIGHTS` message containing fds in exact semantic order:
D7C2-write, diagnostic-write, then held-output only in test phase, and always the held fd-seal
artifact. The message body is canonical JSON with the exact fd-role array. The trusted shim
requires its control socket to be fd `3` and the received rights to be the consecutive fd vector
starting at `4`. It then creates the only `pipe2(O_CLOEXEC)` release pipe, requires its fds to be
the next consecutive pair, and retains the write end as a fail-closed guardian. Its child branch
duplicates D7C2/diagnostic to `1/2`, release-read to `3`, optional output to `4`, and fd-seal to
`5`. In archive phase it closes the remaining D7C2 alias at fd `4`, then calls
`close_range(6, UINT_MAX, CLOSE_RANGE_UNSHARE)` before execing authenticated bwrap. Archive phase
has allowlist `{0,1,2,3,5}`; test phase has `{0,1,2,3,4,5}`. Every other open fd is a hard
failure.

The exact bwrap argv is below. `PHASE_OUTPUT` is the empty list in archive/unit phases and exactly
`["--ro-bind-fd","4","/inputs/tmux"]` in a build-bearing test phase; no other
conditional token exists. `ABSOLUTE_CANDIDATE_PROGRAM` is the authenticated
`/candidate/tests/gateway/run_process_supervisor_session_port_real_host.sh`, and its sole next
argument is the fixed selected mode.

`HOST_TOOLCHAIN` is a fresh reviewer-owned hermetic root, not host `/`: it contains only the
authenticated minimum Python/Node/npm/system utilities and runtime libraries plus empty
mountpoints `/candidate`, `/odb`, `/deps`, `/inputs`, `/authority`, `/work`, `/home`, `/tmp`,
`/proc`, and `/dev`. The parent rejects any other top-level entry, writable prerequisite, device,
socket, set-id bit, symlink escape, Docker client/socket, user bus, credential, or reviewer-root
path. `HOST_CANDIDATE`, `HOST_ODB`, `HOST_DEPS`, and `HOST_SOURCE` are respectively the detached
clean candidate materialization, its read-only bare object database, exact dependency tree, and
held pinned source archive; each no-follow identity/hash is recorded before substitution. Thus
the candidate namespace cannot resolve `REVIEW_ROOT` even though the trusted unit shim can.

<!-- D007D_BWRAP_ARGV_V3_BEGIN -->

```text
[BWRAP,"--unshare-user","--unshare-pid","--unshare-net","--unshare-ipc",
 "--unshare-uts","--unshare-cgroup","--die-with-parent","--new-session",
 "--uid","0","--gid","0","--cap-drop","ALL",
 "--ro-bind",HOST_TOOLCHAIN,"/","--ro-bind",HOST_CANDIDATE,"/candidate",
 "--ro-bind",HOST_ODB,"/odb","--ro-bind",HOST_DEPS,"/deps",
 "--ro-bind",HOST_SOURCE,"/inputs/tmux-3.6a.tar.gz",
 PHASE_OUTPUT,
 "--ro-bind-fd","5","/authority/d007d-fd-seal-v3.py",
 "--size","805306368","--tmpfs","/work",
 "--size","67108864","--tmpfs","/home",
 "--size","201326592","--tmpfs","/tmp",
 "--proc","/proc","--dev","/dev","--chdir","/candidate","--clearenv",
 "--setenv","PATH","/bin:/usr/bin","--setenv","LC_ALL","C",
 "--setenv","LANG","C","--setenv","TZ","UTC","--setenv","HOME","/home",
 "--setenv","TMPDIR","/tmp","--setenv","D007D_CANDIDATE_ROOT","/candidate",
 "--setenv","D007D_CANDIDATE_SHA",CANDIDATE_SHA,
 "--setenv","D007D_GATE_TOOLCHAIN_ROOT","/",
   "--setenv","D007D_GATEWAY_NODE_MODULES","/deps",
   "--setenv","D007D_TMUX_SOURCE_ARCHIVE","/inputs/tmux-3.6a.tar.gz",
   "--setenv","D007C_TEST_TMUX_PATH","/inputs",
   "--setenv","D007C_RUN_REAL_TMUX_PROBE","1",
   "--setenv","D007C_TMUX_SOCKET_NAME","d007c-control-probe",
   "--setenv","D007D_CANDIDATE_PROTOCOL","D7C2-v3","--block-fd","3",
 PYTHON,"/authority/d007d-fd-seal-v3.py","--",ABSOLUTE_CANDIDATE_PROGRAM,FIXED_MODE]
```

<!-- D007D_BWRAP_ARGV_V3_END -->

The 1,372 LF-terminated bytes inside that text fence have SHA-256
`75b099560fa13264c7b4428b3e08e81f93d0db0face17fa7d016e2666e7cef09`.

It never uses `--ro-bind` for the held output. Both phases mount the held fd-seal artifact from
fd 5; the authenticated hermetic root must contain an empty mode-`0555` `/authority` directory
and empty target file, and any other pre-existing entry there is rejected. The final shim resets
stdin, closes every fd from 3 upward, and execs the candidate.

The trusted unit shim reports the bwrap PID over the authenticated seqpacket and retains its own
bwrap pidfd. The parent independently pidfd-opens it and derives the held start token, proves its parent is the unit shim, executable and full NUL-delimited argv match,
and cgroup membership matches the held unit cgroup. Candidate code is still impossible because
bwrap is blocked reading fd 3. Only after all proofs and durable `BWRAP_BOUND` evidence does the
parent send the shim exactly canonical `{nonce,pid,type:"RELEASE"}`. The shim verifies the exact
object, writes exactly byte `0x52` (`R`) to its guarded release-write fd, closes it, and sends
canonical `{pid,type:"RELEASED"}`; only that acknowledgement enters `RELEASED`. Before this
transition, malformed control input or parent EOF makes the shim pidfd-SIGKILL bwrap, wait for
that exact child, and only then close release-write. Because bwrap 0.11.1 ignores the return of
its one-byte `--block-fd` read, EOF is never used as release. Unit-shim death is fail-closed by
the already-active bwrap `--die-with-parent` SIGKILL. No failure path closes release-write while
the bwrap pidfd is live. If the immediate post-fork `pidfd_open` itself fails, the frozen shim may
SIGKILL only its exact still-unreaped fork return before wait/exit; that PID cannot have been
reused, and the run is infrastructure failure. The reviewer parent never uses a numeric-PID
fallback.

The final shim bytes are:

<!-- D007D_FD_SEAL_V3_BEGIN -->

```python
import ctypes
import os
import sys

SYS_close_range = 436
CLOSE_RANGE_UNSHARE = 2


def fail(message):
    os.write(2, ("d007d-fd-seal-v3: " + message + "\n").encode("ascii"))
    raise SystemExit(70)


if sys.platform != "linux" or os.uname().machine != "x86_64":
    fail("unsupported platform")
if len(sys.argv) < 3 or sys.argv[1] != "--" or not sys.argv[2].startswith("/"):
    fail("invalid argv")
null_fd = os.open("/dev/null", os.O_RDONLY | os.O_CLOEXEC)
os.dup2(null_fd, 0, inheritable=True)
if null_fd > 2:
    os.close(null_fd)
libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)
if libc.syscall(SYS_close_range, 3, 0xFFFFFFFF, CLOSE_RANGE_UNSHARE) != 0:
    fail("close_range failed")
os.execve(sys.argv[2], sys.argv[2:], dict(os.environ))
```

<!-- D007D_FD_SEAL_V3_END -->

Mechanical extraction of the 740 LF-terminated Python-fence bytes has SHA-256
`136130354a4f9df53a1601397a8e27ba057d2260d5b547039b11a23db97b3e47`; it must compile. The authenticated Python path/version and libc syscall
ABI are recorded; any non-Linux/amd64 environment fails before launch.

After release the parent concurrently drains both bounded sockets and latches
`D7C2_EOF_OBSERVED` and `DIAGNOSTIC_EOF_OBSERVED`; observation never advances state. It first
accepts the authenticated shim wait as `SHIM_WAIT_ACCEPTED`, then synthesizes
`BOTH_EOF_ACCEPTED` only from `SHIM_REAPED`, and only then accepts two held-cgroup empty reads
250 ms apart as `CGROUP_EMPTY_TWICE`. A retained writer or descendant therefore prevents EOF
acceptance and reaches the same deadline.
The single monotonic run deadline is 900 seconds. On any fault or deadline the parent enters
`REJECTED`, sends exact `systemctl --user kill --kill-whom=all --signal=TERM UNIT`, waits at most
5 seconds while draining, then sends the same command with `KILL`, waits at most 5 seconds, and
requires two empty cgroup scans. Missing/already-dead is accepted only when the held pidfds are
terminal and both scans are empty; no numeric-PID fallback exists.

Before unit cleanup the parent records one exact `systemctl --user show UNIT` projection:
`Id,LoadState,ActiveState,SubState,Result,ExecMainCode,ExecMainStatus,MainPID,ControlGroup`; it
must bind the original identities and terminal status. It then runs bounded
`systemctl --user stop UNIT`, conditionally `systemctl --user reset-failed UNIT` only when
`Result != success`, waits for the direct systemd-run pidfd/status, and polls
`systemctl --user show UNIT -p LoadState --value` until not found. Two absent-unit and absent-
cgroup-path scans 250 ms apart are required. Only then may held-fd/Git reauthentication and the
runtime cleanup/evidence sequence below occur. Every failure transition
first leaves the shim's guarded release writer open while it kills/reaps bwrap, then closes
control and candidate write endpoints, drains/limits reads, reconciles unit/cgroup, records the
first fault, emits no `DONE`, and never releases a different run.

The parent accepts only this transition table; an event not listed for the current state takes
the single `REJECTED` cleanup path:

| From | Required event/proof | To |
|---|---|---|
| `NEW` | candidate/prerequisite/artifact held fds, Git tree, dirs, sockets, and limits exact | `AUTHENTICATED` |
| `AUTHENTICATED` | exact systemd-run child spawned and pidfd-bound | `UNIT_SUBMITTED` |
| `UNIT_SUBMITTED` | one authenticated shim peer equals unit MainPID/cgroup/direct-child chain | `UNIT_BOUND` |
| `UNIT_BOUND` | exact SCM_RIGHTS roles sent once | `FDS_SENT` |
| `FDS_SENT` | bwrap pidfd/exe/argv/parent/start-token/cgroup exact while fd 3 is unread | `BWRAP_BOUND` |
| `BWRAP_BOUND` | durable pre-release record, exact RELEASE command, guarded `R`, and RELEASED acknowledgement | `RELEASED` |
| `RELEASED` | `SHIM_WAIT_ACCEPTED`: exact shim/bwrap status, pidfd/start token, and closed control channel | `SHIM_REAPED` |
| `SHIM_REAPED` | `BOTH_EOF_ACCEPTED`: both observed EOF facts are latched | `STREAMS_EOF` |
| `STREAMS_EOF` | `CGROUP_EMPTY_TWICE`: two empty held-cgroup/adopted-child scans and terminal pidfds | `UNIT_RECORDED` |
| `UNIT_RECORDED` | exact terminal unit projection recorded; stop/reset operation succeeds | `UNIT_STOPPED` |
| `UNIT_STOPPED` | two absent unit/cgroup scans | `ABSENT` |
| `ABSENT` | held identities revalidate; exact reviewer-runtime allowlist unlinked without recursion | `RUNTIME_CLEANED` |
| `RUNTIME_CLEANED` | unlinked held evidence gets DONE, self-parse/hash/fdatasync and artifact receipt | `EVIDENCE_SYNCED` |
| `EVIDENCE_SYNCED` | held fds close and two REVIEW_ROOT-absent scans succeed | `DONE` |

Guarded release-read/write, D7C2/diagnostic endpoints, control peer, unit/bwrap/systemd-run pidfds,
cgroup dirfd, evidence/output/artifact/prerequisite fds, and output dirfd remain held until the
table row that consumes their final proof. In particular output fd 4 is not closed until bwrap
has exited, D7C2/diagnostic EOF and empty cgroup are proven, and post-test output validation has
completed. Early EOF is latched but cannot be accepted before `SHIM_WAIT_ACCEPTED`; terminal
proof without both observed EOF facts, or cgroup acceptance before `BOTH_EOF_ACCEPTED`, is
rejection.

Runtime cleanup uses only held dirfds and `unlinkat`/`rmdir`, never recursive deletion. The exact
allowlist is the control socket, authority three files and `a` directory, evidence/output names,
the two cidfiles and cid directory, empty Docker config/home directories, and `REVIEW_ROOT`;
an unknown entry is rejection. The parent first unlinks these names while retaining the evidence
and output inodes, removes the empty directories/root, then appends
`D007D_REVIEW_DONE_V3` to the unlinked evidence inode, self-parses/fdatasyncs it, obtains the
reviewer artifact receipt for its exact hash, closes every held fd, and proves `REVIEW_ROOT`
absent twice 250 ms apart. Failure uses the same bounded allowlist cleanup but emits no `DONE`.
No product, candidate, source, policy, or arbitrary path is deletion-authorized.

### Independent custody oracle and nine attacks

The following byte-frozen stdlib program is both the D7C2 canonical oracle and the reviewer
attack-driver core. Its `unit-shim` mode implements the authenticated handshake/SCM_RIGHTS and
exact fd normalization above; its `frames` mode validates an fd against the schemas/order; its
`attacks` mode runs the immutable nine-id matrix by invoking the reviewer parent, never the
future candidate Node test. The implementation checkpoint may add candidate tests, but their
result cannot decide these nine authority outcomes.

def reject(message):
    raise ValueError(message)


def canonical(value):
    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=True, allow_nan=False, sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")


def exact_vector(actual, expected, label):
    if type(actual) is not list or actual != expected:
        reject(label + " argv mismatch")
    return tuple(actual)


def docker_private_environment(home, docker_config):
    if (type(home) is not str or type(docker_config) is not str
            or not home.startswith("/") or not docker_config.startswith("/")
            or home == docker_config or "\0" in home or "\0" in docker_config):
        reject("docker private environment path")
    environment = dict(FROZEN_ENV)
    environment.update({"DOCKER_CONFIG": docker_config, "DOCKER_HOST": DOCKER_HOST,
        "HOME": home})
    if tuple(sorted(environment)) != DOCKER_ENVIRONMENT_KEYS:
        reject("docker private environment keys")
    return environment


def validate_docker_environment(environment):
    if type(environment) is not dict or tuple(sorted(environment)) != DOCKER_ENVIRONMENT_KEYS:
        reject("docker environment keys")
    expected = docker_private_environment(
        environment.get("HOME"), environment.get("DOCKER_CONFIG"))
    if environment != expected:
        reject("docker environment values")
    return dict(environment)


def docker_child_observation(argv, environment, started, ended, pidfd_live_value,
        start_token, returncode, stdout, stderr, no_pid_reresolution=True,
        spawn_mechanism="subprocess.Popen", stdout_measurement=None):
    exact_vector(argv, list(argv), "docker child")
    environment = validate_docker_environment(environment)
    if (type(started) is not int or type(ended) is not int or started < 0 or ended < started
            or type(pidfd_live_value) is not bool or not pidfd_live_value
            or type(start_token) is not str or not start_token.isdigit()
            or type(returncode) is not int or type(stdout) is not bytes or type(stderr) is not bytes
            or type(no_pid_reresolution) is not bool or not no_pid_reresolution
            or spawn_mechanism != "subprocess.Popen"):
        reject("docker child identity or terminal observation")
    if stdout_measurement is None:
        stdout_measurement = {
            "bytes": len(stdout), "sha256": hashlib.sha256(stdout).hexdigest()}
    if (type(stdout_measurement) is not dict
            or set(stdout_measurement) != {"bytes", "sha256"}
            or type(stdout_measurement["bytes"]) is not int
            or stdout_measurement["bytes"] < 0
            or not HEX64.fullmatch(stdout_measurement["sha256"])):
        reject("docker stdout measurement")
    terminal = {"returncode": returncode if returncode >= 0 else None,
        "signal": -returncode if returncode < 0 else None}
    classification = "CLIENT_EXITED" if returncode >= 0 else "CLIENT_SIGNALED"
    return {"argv": list(argv), "childPidfdLive": True, "childStartToken": start_token,
        "daemonResult": {"classification": classification, "returncode": returncode},
        "endedMonotonicNs": ended, "environment": environment,
        "noPidReresolution": True,
        "startedMonotonicNs": started, "stderrBytes": len(stderr),
        "stderrSha256": hashlib.sha256(stderr).hexdigest(),
        "stdoutBytes": stdout_measurement["bytes"],
        "stdoutSha256": stdout_measurement["sha256"], "terminalStatus": terminal}


def validate_docker_child_observation(observation, expected_argv, expected_environment):
    exact_keys(observation, DOCKER_CHILD_OBSERVATION_KEYS)
    exact_vector(observation["argv"], expected_argv, "observed docker child")
    if observation["environment"] != validate_docker_environment(expected_environment):
        reject("observed docker environment")
    rebuilt = docker_child_observation(observation["argv"], observation["environment"],
        observation["startedMonotonicNs"], observation["endedMonotonicNs"],
        observation["childPidfdLive"], observation["childStartToken"],
        observation["daemonResult"]["returncode"], b"", b"",
        observation["noPidReresolution"])
    for prefix in ("stdout", "stderr"):
        if (type(observation[prefix + "Bytes"]) is not int
                or observation[prefix + "Bytes"] < 0
                or not HEX64.fullmatch(observation[prefix + "Sha256"])):
            reject("docker child output observation")
        rebuilt[prefix + "Bytes"] = observation[prefix + "Bytes"]
        rebuilt[prefix + "Sha256"] = observation[prefix + "Sha256"]
    if (observation["terminalStatus"] != rebuilt["terminalStatus"]
            or observation["daemonResult"] != rebuilt["daemonResult"]):
        reject("docker child normalized result")
    return {key: observation[key] for key in DOCKER_CHILD_OBSERVATION_KEYS}


def docker_operation(sequence, token, argv, observation, result):
    if (type(sequence) is not int or not 1 <= sequence <= 10
            or token not in DOCKER_NORMAL_TOKENS or type(result) is not str or not result):
        reject("docker operation fields")
    return {"argv": list(argv), "observation": dict(observation), "result": result,
        "sequence": "%02d" % sequence, "token": token}


def validate_cidfile_metadata(value, role, name, run_id, candidate):
    exact_keys(value, DOCKER_CIDFILE_KEYS)
    if (value["role"] != role or value["name"] != name or value["runId"] != run_id
            or value["candidate"] != candidate
            or type(value["containerId"]) is not str
            or not HEX64.fullmatch(value["containerId"])
            or value["mode"] != 0o600 or value["size"] != 65 or value["nlink"] != 1
            or value["regular"] is not True or value["reviewerOwned"] is not True
            or value["terminalLf"] is not True
            or value["sha256"] != hashlib.sha256(
                (value["containerId"] + "\n").encode("ascii")).hexdigest()):
        reject("cidfile metadata")
    return dict(value)


def validate_held_output(value):
    exact_keys(value, DOCKER_HELD_OUTPUT_KEYS)
    if (value["direct"] is not True or value["reviewerOwned"] is not True
            or value["mode"] != 0o600 or value["nlink"] != 0
            or type(value["dev"]) is not int or value["dev"] < 0
            or type(value["ino"]) is not int or value["ino"] <= 0
            or type(value["bytes"]) is not int or value["bytes"] <= 0
            or not HEX64.fullmatch(value["sha256"])):
        reject("held output metadata")
    return dict(value)


def validate_docker_transaction(value, role, run_argv, environment, name, run_id, candidate):
    exact_keys(value, DOCKER_TRANSACTION_KEYS)
    if role not in DOCKER_ROLES or value["role"] != role:
        reject("docker transaction role")
    cidfile = validate_cidfile_metadata(
        value["cidfile"], role, name, run_id, candidate)
    cid = cidfile["containerId"]
    expected_argv = (
        docker_control_argv(run_argv[0], "inspect", name),
        docker_control_argv(run_argv[0], "scan", cid, run_id, role),
        list(run_argv),
        docker_control_argv(run_argv[0], "inspect", cid),
        docker_control_argv(run_argv[0], "wait", cid),
        docker_control_argv(run_argv[0], "inspect", cid),
        docker_control_argv(run_argv[0], "remove", cid),
        docker_control_argv(run_argv[0], "inspect", name),
        docker_control_argv(run_argv[0], "scan", cid, run_id, role),
        docker_control_argv(run_argv[0], "scan", cid, run_id, role),
    )
    operations = value["operations"]
    if type(operations) is not list or len(operations) != 10:
        reject("docker transaction count")
    validated = []
    expected_results = (
        "not-found", "empty", "client-exit-0", None, "exit-0", "exited-0",
        "removed", "not-found", "empty", "empty")
    for index, operation in enumerate(operations, 1):
        exact_keys(operation, DOCKER_OPERATION_KEYS)
        if (operation["sequence"] != "%02d" % index
                or operation["token"] != DOCKER_NORMAL_TOKENS[index - 1]
                or operation["argv"] != expected_argv[index - 1]
                or type(operation["result"]) is not str or not operation["result"]
                or (expected_results[index - 1] is not None
                    and operation["result"] != expected_results[index - 1])
                or (index == 4 and operation["result"] not in ("running", "exited"))):
            reject("docker transaction operation")
        validate_docker_child_observation(
            operation["observation"], expected_argv[index - 1], environment)
        returncode = operation["observation"]["daemonResult"]["returncode"]
        if ((index in (1, 8) and returncode == 0)
                or (index not in (1, 8) and returncode != 0)):
            reject("docker transaction terminal result")
        validated.append({key: operation[key] for key in DOCKER_OPERATION_KEYS})
    held_output = value["heldOutput"]
    if role == "build":
        held_output = validate_held_output(held_output)
        run_observation = operations[2]["observation"]
        if (held_output["bytes"] != run_observation["stdoutBytes"]
                or held_output["sha256"] != run_observation["stdoutSha256"]):
            reject("held output observation")
    elif held_output is not None:
        reject("probe held output")
    return {"cidfile": cidfile, "heldOutput": held_output,
        "operations": validated, "role": role}


def socket_custody_observation(channels):
    exact_keys(channels, ("d7c2", "diagnostic"))
    endpoints = []
    for channel in ("d7c2", "diagnostic"):
        pair = channels[channel]
        if type(pair) is not tuple or len(pair) != 2:
            reject("socket endpoint custody")
        for endpoint in pair:
            if type(endpoint) is not socket.socket or endpoint.fileno() < 0 \
                    or endpoint.family != socket.AF_UNIX \
                    or endpoint.type & 0xf != socket.SOCK_STREAM:
                reject("socket family or type")
            endpoints.append(endpoint.fileno())
    if len(set(endpoints)) != 4:
        reject("socket channel alias")
    for fd in endpoints:
        metadata = os.fstat(fd)
        if not stat.S_ISSOCK(metadata.st_mode) \
                or not fcntl.fcntl(fd, fcntl.F_GETFD) & fcntl.FD_CLOEXEC:
            reject("socket mechanism")
    return {"closeOnExec": True, "d7c2": SOCKET_MECHANISM,
        "diagnostic": SOCKET_MECHANISM, "distinctChannels": True,
        "heldParentChildEndpoints": True}


def authenticate_unit_snapshot(observations):
    exact_keys(observations, AUTHENTICATION_CONJUNCTS)
    if any(type(observations[key]) is not bool or not observations[key]
            for key in AUTHENTICATION_CONJUNCTS):
        reject("unit authentication conjunction")
    result = {key: True for key in AUTHENTICATION_CONJUNCTS}
    result["fullConjunction"] = True
    return result


def contract_bytes():
    return {name: zlib.decompress(base64.b85decode(payload))
        for name, payload in CONTRACT_PACKED.items()}


def contract_proof():
    proof = {}
    for name, payload in contract_bytes().items():
        key = CONTRACT_KEYS[name]
        digest = hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest()
        if digest != CONTRACT_DIGESTS[key] or not payload.endswith(b"\n"):
            reject("contract drift: " + name)
        proof[key] = {"bytes": len(payload), "sha256": digest}
    return proof


def validate_bindings(bindings):
    exact_keys(bindings, BINDING_KEYS)
    for key in BINDING_KEYS:
        if key == "outputPresent":
            if type(bindings[key]) is not bool:
                reject("binding outputPresent")
        elif type(bindings[key]) is not str or "\0" in bindings[key]:
            reject("binding " + key)
    if not HEX40.fullmatch(bindings["candidateSha"]):
        reject("binding candidate")
    if bindings["fixedMode"] not in MODES or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{24}", bindings["nonce"]):
        reject("binding mode or nonce")
    return bindings


def systemd_argv(bindings):
    b = validate_bindings(bindings)
    return [b["systemdRun"], "--user", "--wait", "--remain-after-exit", "--quiet",
        "--service-type=exec", "--unit", b["unit"], "--property", "KillMode=control-group",
        "--property", "TimeoutStopSec=15s", "--property", "TasksMax=128", "--property",
        "MemoryMax=2147483648", "--property", "LimitCPU=600", "--property",
        "LimitAS=2147483648", "--property", "LimitFSIZE=268435456", "--property",
        "LimitNOFILE=256", "--property", "StandardInput=null", "--property",
        "StandardOutput=null", "--property", "StandardError=null", b["env"], "-i",
        "LC_ALL=C", "LANG=C", "TZ=UTC", b["python"], b["authorityOracle"], "unit-shim",
        b["controlSocket"], b["fixedMode"], b["candidateSha"], b["nonce"]]


def bwrap_argv(bindings):
    b = validate_bindings(bindings)
    result = [b["bwrap"], "--unshare-user", "--unshare-pid", "--unshare-net",
        "--unshare-ipc", "--unshare-uts", "--unshare-cgroup", "--die-with-parent",
        "--new-session", "--uid", "0", "--gid", "0", "--cap-drop", "ALL", "--ro-bind",
        b["hostToolchain"], "/", "--ro-bind", b["hostCandidate"], "/candidate", "--ro-bind",
        b["hostOdb"], "/odb", "--ro-bind", b["hostDeps"], "/deps", "--ro-bind",
        b["hostSource"], "/inputs/tmux-3.6a.tar.gz"]
    if b["outputPresent"]:
        result += ["--ro-bind-fd", "4", "/inputs/tmux"]
    result += ["--ro-bind-fd", "5", "/authority/d007d-fd-seal-v3.py", "--size",
        "805306368", "--tmpfs", "/work", "--size", "67108864", "--tmpfs", "/home",
        "--size", "201326592", "--tmpfs", "/tmp", "--proc", "/proc", "--dev", "/dev",
        "--chdir", "/candidate", "--clearenv", "--setenv", "PATH", "/bin:/usr/bin",
        "--setenv", "LC_ALL", "C", "--setenv", "LANG", "C", "--setenv", "TZ", "UTC",
        "--setenv", "HOME", "/home", "--setenv", "TMPDIR", "/tmp", "--setenv",
        "D007D_CANDIDATE_ROOT", "/candidate", "--setenv", "D007D_CANDIDATE_SHA",
        b["candidateSha"], "--setenv", "D007D_GATE_TOOLCHAIN_ROOT", "/",
        "--setenv", "D007D_GATEWAY_NODE_MODULES", "/deps", "--setenv",
        "D007D_TMUX_SOURCE_ARCHIVE", "/inputs/tmux-3.6a.tar.gz", "--setenv",
        "D007C_TEST_TMUX_PATH", "/inputs", "--setenv",
        "D007C_RUN_REAL_TMUX_PROBE", "1", "--setenv", "D007C_TMUX_SOCKET_NAME",
        "d007c-control-probe", "--setenv",
        "D007D_CANDIDATE_PROTOCOL", "D7C2-v3", "--block-fd", "3", b["python"],
        "/authority/d007d-fd-seal-v3.py", "--", b["candidateProgram"], b["fixedMode"]]
    return result


def docker_limits(role, name, cidfile, run_id, candidate):
    return ["--name", name, "--cidfile", cidfile,
        "--label", "org.agents-orchestrator.d007d.run=" + run_id,
        "--label", "org.agents-orchestrator.d007d.role=" + role,
        "--label", "org.agents-orchestrator.d007d.candidate=" + candidate,
        "--memory", "2147483648", "--memory-swap", "2147483648", "--pids-limit", "128",
        "--cpus", "1", "--ulimit", "cpu=600:600", "--ulimit",
        "fsize=268435456:268435456", "--ulimit", "nofile=256:256", "--stop-timeout", "10",
        "--init"]


def docker_argv(docker, role, name, cidfile, run_id, candidate):
    if role not in ("ownership-probe", "build"):
        reject("docker role")
    user = "65534:65534" if role == "ownership-probe" else "1000:1000"
    result = [docker, "run"] + docker_limits(role, name, cidfile, run_id, candidate)
    result += ["--pull", "never", "--network", "none", "--platform", "linux/amd64",
        "--read-only", "--cap-drop", "ALL", "--security-opt", "no-new-privileges", "--user",
        user, "--tmpfs", "/build:rw,exec,nosuid,nodev,uid=1000,gid=1000,mode=0700,size=536870912",
        "--tmpfs", "/tmp:rw,exec,nosuid,nodev,uid=1000,gid=1000,mode=0700,size=67108864",
        "--env", "LC_ALL=C", "--env", "TZ=UTC", "--env", "HOME=/tmp", "--env",
        "D007D_SELECTED_UID=1000", "--env", "D007D_SELECTED_GID=1000"]
    if role == "build":
        result += ["--env", "SOURCE_SHA256=b6d8d9c76585db8ef5fa00d4931902fa4b8cbe8166f528f44fc403961a3f3759",
            "--env", "PATCH_SHA256=2526659ccfb17d3cc2a07171687379322e5a2caa79414e86ea4b6682aa98b2b3",
            "--env", "EXTENSION_SHA256=4d80a8610651a1dd304b9b0e9b45d6832e115d9827d5166f26b4f9dbcdec27e2",
            "-i", "node@sha256:0625f79a0c9f5005e31dba1761260b9f66ea8a3293e5f645eb4550a4c7dcdbb9",
            "sh", "-eu", "-c", contract_bytes()["CONTAINER_PROGRAM_V2"].decode("ascii")]
    else:
        result += ["node@sha256:0625f79a0c9f5005e31dba1761260b9f66ea8a3293e5f645eb4550a4c7dcdbb9",
            "sh", "-eu", "-c", "test ! -r /build; test ! -w /build; test ! -x /build; test ! -r /tmp; test ! -w /tmp; test ! -x /tmp; ! mkdir /build/x 2>/dev/null; ! mkdir /tmp/x 2>/dev/null"]
    return result


class LaunchMachine:
    def __init__(self):
        self.state = "NEW"
        self.observed = {"d7c2": False, "diagnostic": False}
        self.authentication = None
        self.socket_mechanisms = None
        self.release_count = 0
        self.released_after_authentication = False

    def bind_authentication(self, socket_mechanisms, authentication):
        if self.state != "UNIT_SUBMITTED" or self.authentication is not None:
            self.state = "REJECTED"
            reject("authentication snapshot order")
        exact_keys(socket_mechanisms, SOCKET_OBSERVATION_KEYS)
        exact_keys(authentication, AUTHENTICATION_OBSERVATION_KEYS)
        if authentication["fullConjunction"] is not True:
            self.state = "REJECTED"
            reject("authentication snapshot incomplete")
        self.socket_mechanisms = tuple(
            (key, socket_mechanisms[key]) for key in SOCKET_OBSERVATION_KEYS)
        self.authentication = tuple(
            (key, authentication[key]) for key in AUTHENTICATION_OBSERVATION_KEYS)

    def observe_eof(self, channel):
        if channel not in self.observed:
            reject("EOF channel")
        self.observed[channel] = True

    def advance(self, event):
        if event == "UNIT_IDENTITIES" and (self.state != "UNIT_SUBMITTED"
                or self.authentication is None):
            self.state = "REJECTED"
            reject("unit bound without authentication")
        if event == "RELEASE_ACK" and (self.state != "BWRAP_BOUND"
                or self.authentication is None or self.release_count != 0):
            self.state = "REJECTED"
            reject("release without authentication")
        if event == "BOTH_EOF_ACCEPTED" and (self.state != "SHIM_REAPED"
                or not all(self.observed.values())):
            self.state = "REJECTED"
            reject("EOF acceptance order")
        for source, accepted, target in LAUNCH_TRANSITIONS:
            if self.state == source and event == accepted:
                self.state = target
                if event == "RELEASE_ACK":
                    self.release_count = 1
                    self.released_after_authentication = True
                return target
        self.state = "REJECTED"
        reject("launch transition")


class DockerRecorder:
    RECONCILE = {
        "MISSING": ("I_name", "P1", "P2"),
        "ALREADY_EXITED": ("I_id", "WAIT", "RM_FORCE", "I_name", "P1", "P2"),
        "TERM_EXITED": ("I_id", "TERM", "WAIT", "I_id", "RM_FORCE", "I_name", "P1", "P2"),
        "KILL_EXITED": ("I_id", "TERM", "WAIT_TIMEOUT", "KILL", "WAIT", "I_id", "RM_FORCE", "I_name", "P1", "P2"),
    }

    def __init__(self):
        self.operations = []
        self.transactions = []
        self.contacted = False

    def accept(self, expected, actual, role, operation):
        exact_vector(actual, expected, "docker")
        self.contacted = True
        self.operations.append((role, operation, tuple(actual)))

    def transaction(self, role, value, run_argv, environment, name, run_id, candidate):
        if [item["role"] for item in self.transactions] != list(DOCKER_ROLES[:len(
                self.transactions)]) or role != DOCKER_ROLES[len(self.transactions)]:
            reject("docker transaction order")
        accepted = validate_docker_transaction(
            value, role, run_argv, environment, name, run_id, candidate)
        self.transactions.append(accepted)
        self.operations.extend((role, item["sequence"], tuple(item["argv"]))
            for item in accepted["operations"])

    def reconcile(self, actual):
        tokens = tuple(actual)
        matches = [branch for branch, expected in self.RECONCILE.items()
            if tokens == expected]
        if len(matches) != 1:
            reject("docker reconciliation")
        branch = matches[0]
        self.operations.append(("reconcile", branch, tokens))
        return branch

    @classmethod
    def branch_proof(cls):
        proof = {}
        for branch, tokens in cls.RECONCILE.items():
            recorder = cls()
            derived = recorder.reconcile(tokens)
            if derived != branch or len(tokens) not in (3, 6, 8, 10):
                reject("docker branch derivation")
            proof[branch] = {"count": len(tokens), "tokens": list(tokens)}
        return proof


class DeadlineBudget:
    ABSOLUTE_OFFSETS = {
        "ownership-probe": 60, "build": 760, "reconciliation": 860,
        "finalization": 900, "outer": 900}

    def __init__(self, t0, clock=time.monotonic):
        if type(t0) not in (int, float) or t0 < 0 or not callable(clock):
            reject("deadline origin")
        self.t0 = t0
        self.clock = clock
        self.active = None
        self.active_deadline = self.t0 + PARENT_LIMITS["outer"]
        self.entries = []

    def enter(self, phase):
        if phase not in ("ownership-probe", "build", "reconciliation", "finalization"):
            reject("deadline phase")
        now = self.clock()
        duration = PARENT_LIMITS[phase]
        absolute = self.t0 + self.ABSOLUTE_OFFSETS[phase]
        self.active = phase
        self.active_deadline = min(now + duration, absolute,
            self.t0 + PARENT_LIMITS["outer"])
        self.entries.append({"absolute": absolute, "deadline": self.active_deadline,
            "duration": duration, "phase": phase, "started": now})
        return self.remaining(duration)

    def remaining(self, operation=None):
        now = self.clock()
        deadline = min(self.active_deadline, self.t0 + PARENT_LIMITS["outer"])
        if operation is not None:
            if type(operation) not in (int, float) or operation <= 0:
                reject("operation deadline")
            deadline = min(deadline, now + operation)
        value = deadline - now
        if value <= 0:
            raise TimeoutError("parent deadline")
        return value

    def outer(self, operation=None):
        active, active_deadline = self.active, self.active_deadline
        self.active = "outer"
        self.active_deadline = self.t0 + PARENT_LIMITS["outer"]
        try:
            return self.remaining(operation)
        finally:
            self.active, self.active_deadline = active, active_deadline


def deadline_proof():
    values = {"operation": 10, "ownershipProbe": 60, "build": 700,
        "reconciliation": 100, "finalization": 40, "outer": 900}
    if values["ownershipProbe"] + values["build"] + values["reconciliation"] + values["finalization"] != 900:
        reject("deadline sum")
    boundaries = {"probe": min(60, 60), "build": min(60 + 700, 760),
        "reconcile": min(760 + 100, 860), "final": 900}
    if boundaries != {"probe": 60, "build": 760, "reconcile": 860, "final": 900}:
        reject("deadline boundaries")
    clock_value = [1000.0]
    budget = DeadlineBudget(clock_value[0], lambda: clock_value[0])
    observed = {}
    for phase, start in (("ownership-probe", 1000.0), ("build", 1060.0),
            ("reconciliation", 1760.0), ("finalization", 1860.0)):
        clock_value[0] = start
        observed[phase] = budget.enter(phase)
    if observed != {"ownership-probe": 60.0, "build": 700.0,
            "reconciliation": 100.0, "finalization": 40.0}:
        reject("deadline executable slices")
    return {"absoluteOffsets": dict(DeadlineBudget.ABSOLUTE_OFFSETS),
        "oneT0BeforeGitAndMaterialization": True, "operation": 10,
        "scanSeparationMilliseconds": 250, "slices": values,
        "timeline": list(budget.entries)}


def object_no_duplicates(pairs):
    result = {}
    for key, value in pairs:
        if key in result:
            reject("duplicate JSON key")
        result[key] = value
    return result


def json_payload(payload):
    if not payload or len(payload) > 65536:
        reject("JSON frame size")
    value = json.loads(payload.decode("utf-8"), object_pairs_hook=object_no_duplicates,
        parse_constant=lambda _value: reject("nonfinite JSON"))
    if canonical(value) != payload:
        reject("noncanonical JSON")
    return value


def exact_keys(value, keys):
    if type(value) is not dict or set(value) != set(keys):
        reject("wrong keys")


def uint(value, maximum):
    if type(value) is not int or not 0 <= value <= maximum:
        reject("invalid integer")


def identity(value, expected, phase):
    if value.get("candidateSha") != expected["candidateSha"]:
        reject("candidate mismatch")
    if value.get("mode") != expected["mode"] or value.get("mode") not in MODES:
        reject("mode mismatch")
    if value.get("phase") != phase:
        reject("phase mismatch")


def validate_expected(expected):
    exact_keys(expected, ("candidateSha", "mode", "names", "requiredLedger", "totals"))
    if type(expected["candidateSha"]) is not str or not HEX40.fullmatch(expected["candidateSha"]):
        reject("expectation candidate")
    if expected["mode"] not in MODES:
        reject("expectation mode")
    names = expected["names"]
    if type(names) is not list or not 1 <= len(names) <= 512:
        reject("expectation names")
    if any(type(name) is not str or not NAME.fullmatch(name)
            or not 1 <= len(name.encode()) <= 200 for name in names):
        reject("expectation name")
    if len(set(names)) != len(names):
        reject("expectation duplicate name")
    totals = expected["totals"]
    exact_keys(totals, ("cancelled", "fail", "pass", "skipped", "tests"))
    for value in totals.values():
        uint(value, 1000000)
    if totals["tests"] != len(names) or totals["tests"] != sum(totals[key] for key in
            ("pass", "fail", "skipped", "cancelled")):
        reject("expectation totals")
    required = expected["requiredLedger"]
    if type(required) is not list or len(required) > 128:
        reject("expectation ledger count")
    prior = None
    for entry in required:
        exact_keys(entry, ("kind", "name", "outcome"))
        if entry["kind"] not in ("workspace", "relayKey", "relaySocket", "runtimeDirectory",
                "tmuxServer", "tmuxSession", "process", "descriptor"):
            reject("expectation ledger kind")
        if (type(entry["name"]) is not str or not 1 <= len(entry["name"]) <= 128
                or any(not 0x20 <= ord(char) <= 0x7E for char in entry["name"])):
            reject("expectation ledger name")
        if entry["outcome"] not in ("RETIRED", "PRESERVED"):
            reject("expectation ledger outcome")
        pair = (entry["kind"], entry["name"])
        if prior is not None and pair <= prior:
            reject("expectation ledger order")
        prior = pair


def validate_chunk(value, expected, seq):
    exact_keys(value, ("candidateSha", "channel", "data", "mode", "phase", "seq"))
    identity(value, expected, "test")
    uint(value["seq"], 63)
    if value["channel"] != "tap" or value["seq"] != seq or type(value["data"]) is not str:
        reject("chunk identity")
    data = value["data"].encode("utf-8")
    if not data or len(data) > 32768 or b"\0" in data:
        reject("chunk data")
    return data


def names_digest(names):
    payload = bytearray()
    for name in names:
        encoded = name.encode("utf-8")
        payload.extend(len(encoded).to_bytes(2, "big"))
        payload.extend(encoded)
    return hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest()


def validate_tap(tap, names, totals):
    if not tap.endswith(b"\n") or b"\r" in tap or b"\0" in tap:
        reject("TAP framing")
    try:
        lines = tap.decode("utf-8", "strict").splitlines()
    except UnicodeDecodeError:
        reject("TAP encoding")
    if (not lines or lines[0] != "TAP version 13"
            or lines.count("TAP version 13") != 1
            or any("Bail out!" in line for line in lines)):
        reject("TAP header")
    result_pattern = re.compile(r"(not )?ok ([1-9][0-9]*) - ([A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9 _./:()'=-]*)(?: # (SKIP|TODO).*)?\Z")
    results = []
    result_indexes = []
    for index, line in enumerate(lines):
        match = result_pattern.fullmatch(line)
        if match:
            results.append(match.groups())
            result_indexes.append(index)
    if [int(item[1]) for item in results] != list(range(1, len(results) + 1)):
        reject("TAP result ids")
    if [item[2] for item in results] != names or any(item[3] == "TODO" for item in results):
        reject("TAP result names")
    footer = [f"1..{len(names)}", f"# tests {totals['tests']}",
        f"# pass {totals['pass']}", f"# fail {totals['fail']}",
        f"# cancelled {totals['cancelled']}", f"# skipped {totals['skipped']}",
        "# todo 0"]
    duration_indexes = [index for index, line in enumerate(lines)
        if line.startswith("# duration_ms ")]
    has_duration = bool(duration_indexes)
    if has_duration and (duration_indexes != [len(lines) - 1]
            or not re.fullmatch(r"# duration_ms [0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?", lines[-1])):
        reject("TAP duration")
    footer_end = len(lines) - int(has_duration)
    footer_start = footer_end - len(footer)
    if (footer_start <= 0 or lines[footer_start:footer_end] != footer
            or not result_indexes or result_indexes[-1] >= footer_start):
        reject("TAP terminal summary")
    plans = [index for index, line in enumerate(lines)
        if re.fullmatch(r"1\.\.[0-9]+", line)]
    if plans != [footer_start]:
        reject("TAP plan")
    for offset, key in enumerate(("tests", "pass", "fail", "cancelled", "skipped", "todo"), 1):
        matches = [index for index, line in enumerate(lines)
            if re.fullmatch(rf"# {key} [0-9]+", line)]
        if matches != [footer_start + offset]:
            reject("TAP summary multiplicity")
    passes = sum(item[0] is None and item[3] is None for item in results)
    skips = sum(item[0] is None and item[3] == "SKIP" for item in results)
    failures = sum(item[0] is not None for item in results)
    if passes != totals["pass"] or skips != totals["skipped"] or failures != totals["fail"] + totals["cancelled"]:
        reject("TAP result accounting")


def validate_summary(value, expected, tap):
    keys = ("cancelled", "candidateSha", "fail", "mode", "names", "namesSha256",
        "pass", "phase", "skipped", "tapBytes", "tapSha256", "tests")
    exact_keys(value, keys)
    identity(value, expected, "test")
    for key in ("tests", "pass", "fail", "skipped", "cancelled"):
        uint(value[key], 1000000)
    uint(value["tapBytes"], 2097152)
    names = value["names"]
    if type(names) is not list or not 1 <= len(names) <= 512:
        reject("names cardinality")
    for name in names:
        if type(name) is not str or not NAME.fullmatch(name) or not 1 <= len(name.encode()) <= 200:
            reject("test name")
    if len(set(names)) != len(names):
        reject("duplicate test name")
    if value["tests"] != len(names) or value["tests"] != sum(value[k] for k in
            ("pass", "fail", "skipped", "cancelled")):
        reject("summary arithmetic")
    if value["tapBytes"] != len(tap) or value["tapSha256"] != hashlib.sha256(tap).hexdigest():
        reject("TAP binding")
    if value["namesSha256"] != names_digest(names):
        reject("names binding")
    if names != expected["names"] or {k: value[k] for k in expected["totals"]} != expected["totals"]:
        reject("expectation mismatch")
    validate_tap(tap, names, {key: value[key] for key in
        ("tests", "pass", "fail", "cancelled", "skipped")})


def validate_ledger(value, expected):
    exact_keys(value, ("candidateSha", "entries", "ledgerSha256", "mode", "phase"))
    identity(value, expected, "test")
    entries = value["entries"]
    if type(entries) is not list or len(entries) > 128:
        reject("ledger cardinality")
    prior = None
    for entry in entries:
        exact_keys(entry, ("dev", "ino", "kind", "name", "outcome", "startToken"))
        uint(entry["dev"], 9223372036854775807)
        uint(entry["ino"], 9223372036854775807)
        if entry["kind"] not in ("workspace", "relayKey", "relaySocket", "runtimeDirectory",
                "tmuxServer", "tmuxSession", "process", "descriptor"):
            reject("ledger kind")
        if (type(entry["name"]) is not str or not 1 <= len(entry["name"]) <= 128
                or any(not 0x20 <= ord(char) <= 0x7E for char in entry["name"])):
            reject("ledger name")
        if entry["outcome"] not in ("RETIRED", "PRESERVED"):
            reject("ledger outcome")
