        token = entry["startToken"]
        if token is not None and (type(token) is not str or not HEX64.fullmatch(token)):
            reject("ledger token")
        if entry["kind"] in ("process", "tmuxServer") and token is None:
            reject("missing process token")
        pair = (entry["kind"], entry["name"])
        if prior is not None and pair <= prior:
            reject("ledger order")
        prior = pair
    if value["ledgerSha256"] != hashlib.sha256(canonical(entries)).hexdigest():
        reject("ledger binding")
    required = {(x["kind"], x["name"], x["outcome"]) for x in expected["requiredLedger"]}
    observed = {(x["kind"], x["name"], x["outcome"]) for x in entries}
    if not required <= observed:
        reject("required ledger")


def read_exact(fd, size):
    chunks = []
    while size:
        chunk = os.read(fd, size)
        if not chunk:
            reject("truncated frame")
        chunks.append(chunk)
        size -= len(chunk)
    return b"".join(chunks)


def write_all(fd, payload):
    offset = 0
    while offset < len(payload):
        written = os.write(fd, payload[offset:])
        if written <= 0:
            reject("short archive write")
        offset += written


def copy_exact(source_fd, sink_fd, size):
    while size:
        chunk = os.read(source_fd, min(size, 65536))
        if not chunk:
            reject("truncated archive frame")
        write_all(sink_fd, chunk)
        size -= len(chunk)


def validate_frames(fd, phase, expected, archive_fd=None):
    validate_expected(expected)
    frames = 0
    json_total = 0
    chunks = []
    summary = ledger = False
    while True:
        first = os.read(fd, 1)
        if not first:
            break
        header = first + read_exact(fd, 11)
        if header[:4] != MAGIC or header[4] != 3 or header[6:8] != b"\0\0":
            reject("header")
        kind = header[5]
        length = int.from_bytes(header[8:12], "big")
        frames += 1
        if frames > 256:
            reject("frame count")
        if phase == "archive":
            if frames != 1 or kind != 4 or not 1 <= length <= 268435456 or archive_fd is None:
                reject("archive order")
            copy_exact(fd, archive_fd, length)
            continue
        if kind not in (1, 2, 3) or not 1 <= length <= 65536:
            reject("test frame type")
        json_total += length
        if json_total > 4194304:
            reject("JSON total")
        value = json_payload(read_exact(fd, length))
        if kind == 3 and not summary and not ledger:
            if len(chunks) >= 64:
                reject("chunk count")
            chunks.append(validate_chunk(value, expected, len(chunks)))
        elif kind == 1 and chunks and not summary and not ledger:
            validate_summary(value, expected, b"".join(chunks))
            summary = True
        elif kind == 2 and summary and not ledger:
            validate_ledger(value, expected)
            ledger = True
        else:
            reject("test frame order")
    if phase == "archive":
        if frames != 1:
            reject("archive EOF")
    elif not chunks or not summary or not ledger or frames != len(chunks) + 2:
        reject("test EOF")


USTAR_MEMBERS = (("tmux-3.6a.tar.gz", b"source"),
    ("tmux-3.6a-agents.1.patch", b"patch"), ("cmd-agents-capture.c", b"extension"))


def ustar_header(name, size, kind=b"0"):
    encoded = name.encode("ascii")
    header = bytearray(512)
    header[:len(encoded)] = encoded
    header[100:108] = b"0000444\0"
    header[108:116] = b"0000000\0"
    header[116:124] = b"0000000\0"
    header[124:136] = f"{size:011o}\0".encode("ascii")
    header[136:148] = b"00000000000\0"
    header[148:156] = b"        "
    header[156:157] = kind
    header[257:263] = b"ustar\0"
    header[263:265] = b"00"
    header[329:337] = b"0000000\0"
    header[337:345] = b"0000000\0"
    header[148:156] = f"{sum(header):06o}\0 ".encode("ascii")
    return bytes(header)


def canonical_ustar(members=USTAR_MEMBERS):
    result = bytearray()
    for name, payload in members:
        result += ustar_header(name, len(payload)) + payload + b"\0" * (-len(payload) % 512)
    return bytes(result) + b"\0" * 1024


def validate_canonical_ustar(payload):
    offset = 0
    observed = []
    for expected_name, _fixture in USTAR_MEMBERS:
        if offset + 512 > len(payload):
            reject("USTAR header truncation")
        header = payload[offset:offset + 512]
        offset += 512
        raw_name = header[:100]
        if b"\0" not in raw_name:
            reject("USTAR name")
        name = raw_name.split(b"\0", 1)[0].decode("ascii")
        raw_size = header[124:136]
        if not re.fullmatch(b"[0-7]{11}\0", raw_size):
            reject("USTAR size")
        size = int(raw_size[:-1], 8)
        if name != expected_name or header != ustar_header(name, size):
            reject("USTAR header")
        end = offset + size
        if end > len(payload):
            reject("USTAR payload truncation")
        body = payload[offset:end]
        offset = end
        padding = -size % 512
        if payload[offset:offset + padding] != b"\0" * padding:
            reject("USTAR padding")
        offset += padding
        observed.append((name, size, hashlib.sha256(body).hexdigest()))
    if payload[offset:offset + 1024] != b"\0" * 1024 or offset + 1024 != len(payload):
        reject("USTAR terminator")
    return tuple(observed)


def validate_archive_sink(fd):
    if type(fd) is not int or fd < 3:
        reject("archive sink fd")
    metadata = os.fstat(fd)
    if (not stat.S_ISREG(metadata.st_mode) or metadata.st_uid != os.geteuid()
            or stat.S_IMODE(metadata.st_mode) != 0o600 or metadata.st_size != 0
            or os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_CUR) != 0):
        reject("archive sink identity")
    if not os.readlink("/proc/self/fd/" + str(fd)).startswith("/memfd:"):
        reject("archive sink pathname")
    if fcntl.fcntl(fd, fcntl.F_GET_SEALS) != 0:
        reject("archive sink initial seals")


def seal_archive(fd):
    os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
    payload = os.read(fd, os.fstat(fd).st_size)
    validate_canonical_ustar(payload)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest()
    seals = fcntl.F_SEAL_WRITE | fcntl.F_SEAL_SHRINK | fcntl.F_SEAL_GROW | fcntl.F_SEAL_SEAL
    fcntl.fcntl(fd, fcntl.F_ADD_SEALS, seals)
    if fcntl.fcntl(fd, fcntl.F_GET_SEALS) != seals:
        reject("archive seals")
    os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
    if hashlib.sha256(os.read(fd, len(payload))).hexdigest() != digest:
        reject("archive rehash")
    return {"bytes": len(payload), "sha256": digest, "seals": seals}


def archive_frame():
    payload = canonical_ustar()
    return encode_frame(4, payload), payload


def delayed_writer_proof():
    expected = {"candidateSha": "a" * 40, "mode": "cp1-unit-custody",
        "names": ["custody"], "totals": {"tests": 1, "pass": 1, "fail": 0,
        "skipped": 0, "cancelled": 0}, "requiredLedger": []}
    source_r, source_w = os.pipe2(os.O_CLOEXEC)
    result_r, result_w = os.pipe2(os.O_CLOEXEC)
    release_r, release_w = os.pipe2(os.O_CLOEXEC)
    ready_r, ready_w = os.pipe2(os.O_CLOEXEC)
    sink = os.memfd_create("d007d-archive-v4", os.MFD_CLOEXEC | os.MFD_ALLOW_SEALING)
    os.fchmod(sink, 0o600)
    validator = os.fork()
    if validator == 0:
        try:
            for fd in (source_w, result_r, release_r, release_w, ready_r, ready_w):
                os.close(fd)
            validate_frames(source_r, "archive", expected, sink)
            seal_archive(sink)
            write_all(result_w, b"A")
            os._exit(0)
        except BaseException:
            try:
                write_all(result_w, b"R")
            finally:
                os._exit(70)
    holder = os.fork()
    if holder == 0:
        try:
            for fd in (source_r, result_r, result_w, release_w, ready_r, sink):
                os.close(fd)
            write_all(ready_w, b"H")
            if os.read(release_r, 1) != b"R":
                os._exit(70)
            os.close(source_w)
            os._exit(0)
        except BaseException:
            os._exit(70)
    for fd in (source_r, result_w, release_r, ready_w):
        os.close(fd)
    stream, payload = archive_frame()
    write_all(source_w, stream)
    os.close(source_w)
    if os.read(ready_r, 1) != b"H":
        reject("holder ready")
    poller = select.poll()
    poller.register(result_r, select.POLLIN | select.POLLHUP)
    if poller.poll(250):
        reject("premature terminal handling")
    write_all(release_w, b"R")
    os.close(release_w)
    if not poller.poll(2000) or os.read(result_r, 1) != b"A":
        reject("delayed EOF result")
    if os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(os.waitpid(validator, 0)[1]) != 0:
        reject("validator exit")
    if os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(os.waitpid(holder, 0)[1]) != 0:
        reject("holder exit")
    metadata = seal_archive_result(sink, payload)
    for fd in (result_r, ready_r, sink):
        os.close(fd)
    return metadata


def seal_archive_result(fd, expected):
    seals = fcntl.F_SEAL_WRITE | fcntl.F_SEAL_SHRINK | fcntl.F_SEAL_GROW | fcntl.F_SEAL_SEAL
    os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
    actual = os.read(fd, len(expected) + 1)
    if actual != expected or fcntl.fcntl(fd, fcntl.F_GET_SEALS) != seals:
        reject("delayed archive result")
    return {"bytes": len(actual), "sha256": hashlib.sha256(actual).hexdigest(),
        "pollMilliseconds": 250, "result": "ACCEPTED"}


def encode_frame(kind, payload, magic=MAGIC, version=3, reserved=b"\0\0", length=None):
    if length is None:
        length = len(payload)
    return magic + bytes((version, kind)) + reserved + struct.pack(">I", length) + payload


def clone(value):
    return json.loads(canonical(value))


def decide(stream, phase, expected):
    source = os.memfd_create("d007d-d7c2-source", os.MFD_CLOEXEC)
    archive = os.memfd_create("d007d-d7c2-archive", os.MFD_CLOEXEC)
    try:
        write_all(source, stream)
        os.lseek(source, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
        try:
            validate_frames(source, phase, expected, archive if phase == "archive" else None)
        except ValueError:
            return "REJECTED"
        return "ACCEPTED"
    finally:
        os.close(archive)
        os.close(source)


def frame_selftest():
    candidate = "a" * 40
    tap = (b"TAP version 13\n# Subtest: custody\nok 1 - custody\n1..1\n# tests 1\n"
        b"# pass 1\n# fail 0\n# cancelled 0\n# skipped 0\n# todo 0\n# duration_ms 1\n")
    totals = {"tests": 1, "pass": 1, "fail": 0, "skipped": 0, "cancelled": 0}
    expected = {"candidateSha": candidate, "mode": "cp1-unit-custody",
        "names": ["custody"], "totals": totals, "requiredLedger": []}
    chunk = {"candidateSha": candidate, "channel": "tap", "data": tap.decode("ascii"),
        "mode": expected["mode"], "phase": "test", "seq": 0}
    summary = {"cancelled": 0, "candidateSha": candidate, "fail": 0,
        "mode": expected["mode"], "names": ["custody"],
        "namesSha256": names_digest(["custody"]), "pass": 1, "phase": "test",
        "skipped": 0, "tapBytes": len(tap), "tapSha256": hashlib.sha256(tap).hexdigest(),
        "tests": 1}
    ledger = {"candidateSha": candidate, "entries": [],
        "ledgerSha256": hashlib.sha256(canonical([])).hexdigest(),
        "mode": expected["mode"], "phase": "test"}

    def test_stream(one=chunk, two=summary, three=ledger):
        return (encode_frame(3, canonical(one)) + encode_frame(1, canonical(two))
            + encode_frame(2, canonical(three)))

    valid_test = test_stream()
    valid_archive = encode_frame(4, b"ustar-selftest")
    cases = [("canonical_archive", "archive", valid_archive, expected, "ACCEPTED"),
        ("canonical_test", "test", valid_test, expected, "ACCEPTED")]

    def mutant(name, stream, phase="test", authority=expected):
        cases.append((name, phase, stream, authority, "REJECTED"))

    first_length = len(canonical(chunk))
    mutant("header_magic", b"X" + valid_test[1:])
    mutant("header_version", valid_test[:4] + b"\x02" + valid_test[5:])
    mutant("header_type", valid_test[:5] + b"\x09" + valid_test[6:])
    mutant("header_reserved", valid_test[:6] + b"\x01\x00" + valid_test[8:])
    mutant("header_length_short", valid_test[:8] + struct.pack(">I", first_length - 1)
        + valid_test[12:])
    mutant("header_length_long", valid_test[:8] + struct.pack(">I", first_length + 1)
        + valid_test[12:])
    mutant("header_truncated", valid_test[:7])
    mutant("payload_truncated", valid_test[:-1])
    mutant("trailing_byte", valid_test + b"X")
    mutant("json_zero_length", encode_frame(3, b"") + valid_test[12 + first_length:])
    mutant("json_frame_over_cap", encode_frame(3, b"", length=65537))
    mutant("json_invalid_utf8", encode_frame(3, b"\xff")
        + encode_frame(1, canonical(summary)) + encode_frame(2, canonical(ledger)))
    duplicate = canonical(chunk)[:-1] + b',"seq":0}'
    mutant("json_duplicate_key", encode_frame(3, duplicate)
        + encode_frame(1, canonical(summary)) + encode_frame(2, canonical(ledger)))
    noncanonical = json.dumps(chunk, sort_keys=True).encode("utf-8")
    mutant("json_noncanonical", encode_frame(3, noncanonical)
        + encode_frame(1, canonical(summary)) + encode_frame(2, canonical(ledger)))

    for name, key, value, remove in (
            ("chunk_extra_key", "extra", 1, False),
            ("chunk_missing_key", "channel", None, True),
            ("chunk_boolean_seq", "seq", True, False),
            ("chunk_false_seq", "seq", False, False),
            ("chunk_wrong_seq", "seq", 1, False),
            ("chunk_empty_data", "data", "", False),
            ("chunk_nul_data", "data", "x\0", False),
            ("chunk_data_over_cap", "data", "x" * 32769, False),
            ("chunk_candidate", "candidateSha", "b" * 40, False),
            ("chunk_mode", "mode", "unknown", False),
            ("chunk_phase", "phase", "archive", False),
            ("chunk_channel", "channel", "stderr", False)):
        value_object = clone(chunk)
        if remove:
            del value_object[key]
        else:
            value_object[key] = value
        mutant(name, test_stream(one=value_object))

    mutant("summary_before_chunk", encode_frame(1, canonical(summary))
        + encode_frame(2, canonical(ledger)))
    mutant("summary_duplicate", encode_frame(3, canonical(chunk))
        + encode_frame(1, canonical(summary)) * 2 + encode_frame(2, canonical(ledger)))
    mutant("ledger_before_summary", encode_frame(3, canonical(chunk))
        + encode_frame(2, canonical(ledger)) + encode_frame(1, canonical(summary)))
    mutant("summary_missing", encode_frame(3, canonical(chunk)) + encode_frame(2, canonical(ledger)))
    mutant("ledger_missing", encode_frame(3, canonical(chunk)) + encode_frame(1, canonical(summary)))
    mutant("frame_after_ledger", valid_test + encode_frame(3, canonical(chunk)))
    chunks65 = b"".join(encode_frame(3, canonical(dict(chunk, seq=index))) for index in range(65))
    mutant("chunk_count_over_cap", chunks65)
    terminal = (b"1..1\n# tests 1\n# pass 1\n# fail 0\n# cancelled 0\n"
        b"# skipped 0\n# todo 0\n")
    for name, changed_tap in (
            ("tap_bailout", tap.replace(b"# Subtest: custody\n", b"Bail out!\n")),
            ("tap_result_name", tap.replace(b"ok 1 - custody", b"ok 1 - other")),
            ("tap_duplicate_id", tap.replace(b"ok 1 - custody\n",
                b"ok 1 - custody\nok 1 - custody-again\n")),
            ("tap_plan_mismatch", tap.replace(b"1..1\n", b"1..2\n")),
            ("tap_nonterminal_summary", b"TAP version 13\n" + terminal
                + b"ok 1 - custody\n"),
            ("tap_trailing_header", tap + b"TAP version 13\n"),
            ("tap_trailing_bytes", tap + b"x\n")):
        changed_chunk = clone(chunk)
        changed_chunk["data"] = changed_tap.decode("ascii")
        changed_summary = clone(summary)
        changed_summary["tapBytes"] = len(changed_tap)
        changed_summary["tapSha256"] = hashlib.sha256(changed_tap).hexdigest()
        mutant(name, test_stream(one=changed_chunk, two=changed_summary))

    for name, key, value in (
            ("summary_negative_count", "pass", -1),
            ("summary_count_over_cap", "pass", 1000001),
            ("summary_boolean_count", "pass", True),
            ("summary_bad_arithmetic", "pass", 0),
            ("summary_names_hash", "namesSha256", "0" * 64),
            ("summary_boolean_tap_bytes", "tapBytes", True),
            ("summary_tap_bytes_over_cap", "tapBytes", 2097153),
            ("summary_tap_bytes", "tapBytes", len(tap) + 1),
            ("summary_tap_hash", "tapSha256", "0" * 64)):
        value_object = clone(summary)
        value_object[key] = value
        mutant(name, test_stream(two=value_object))
    for name, names in (("summary_names_empty", []),
            ("summary_names_duplicate", ["custody", "custody"]),
            ("summary_name_control", ["bad\nname"]),
            ("summary_name_over_cap", ["x" * 201]),
            ("summary_names_over_cap", [f"n{index}" for index in range(513)]),
            ("summary_unexpected_name", ["other"])):
        value_object = clone(summary)
        value_object["names"] = names
        value_object["namesSha256"] = names_digest(names)
        value_object["tests"] = len(names)
        value_object["pass"] = len(names)
        mutant(name, test_stream(two=value_object))

    entry = {"dev": 1, "ino": 2, "kind": "workspace", "name": "workspace",
        "outcome": "RETIRED", "startToken": None}
    for name, key, value, remove in (
            ("ledger_extra_key", "extra", 1, False),
            ("ledger_entry_missing_key", "ino", None, True),
            ("ledger_boolean_dev", "dev", True, False),
            ("ledger_negative_dev", "dev", -1, False),
            ("ledger_dev_over_cap", "dev", 9223372036854775808, False),
            ("ledger_kind", "kind", "unknown", False),
            ("ledger_name_empty", "name", "", False),
            ("ledger_name_nonascii", "name", "caf\u00e9", False),
            ("ledger_name_over_cap", "name", "x" * 129, False),
            ("ledger_outcome", "outcome", "DELETED", False),
            ("ledger_process_token", "kind", "process", False),
            ("ledger_start_token", "startToken", "0", False)):
        changed = clone(entry)
        if remove:
            del changed[key]
        else:
            changed[key] = value
        value_object = clone(ledger)
        value_object["entries"] = [changed]
        value_object["ledgerSha256"] = hashlib.sha256(canonical([changed])).hexdigest()
        mutant(name, test_stream(three=value_object))
    duplicate_entries = [entry, clone(entry)]
    value_object = clone(ledger)
    value_object["entries"] = duplicate_entries
    value_object["ledgerSha256"] = hashlib.sha256(canonical(duplicate_entries)).hexdigest()
    mutant("ledger_duplicate_pair", test_stream(three=value_object))
    entries129 = [dict(entry, name=f"n{index:03d}") for index in range(129)]
    value_object = clone(ledger)
    value_object["entries"] = entries129
    value_object["ledgerSha256"] = hashlib.sha256(canonical(entries129)).hexdigest()
    mutant("ledger_count_over_cap", test_stream(three=value_object))
    value_object = clone(ledger)
    value_object["ledgerSha256"] = "0" * 64
    mutant("ledger_hash", test_stream(three=value_object))
    required_expected = clone(expected)
    required_expected["requiredLedger"] = [{"kind": entry["kind"], "name": entry["name"],
        "outcome": entry["outcome"]}]
    mutant("ledger_required_missing", valid_test, authority=required_expected)
    bad_expected = clone(expected)
    bad_expected["extra"] = 1
    mutant("expectation_extra_key", valid_test, authority=bad_expected)
    bad_expected = clone(expected)
    bad_expected["candidateSha"] = "x"
    mutant("expectation_candidate", valid_test, authority=bad_expected)
    bad_expected = clone(expected)
    bad_expected["names"] = ["custody", "custody"]
    bad_expected["totals"] = {"tests": 2, "pass": 2, "fail": 0, "skipped": 0, "cancelled": 0}
    mutant("expectation_duplicate_name", valid_test, authority=bad_expected)
    bad_expected = clone(expected)
    bad_expected["totals"]["pass"] = True
    mutant("expectation_boolean_total", valid_test, authority=bad_expected)
    bad_expected = clone(expected)
    bad_expected["requiredLedger"] = [{"kind": "workspace", "name": "workspace",
        "outcome": "RETIRED", "extra": 1}]
    mutant("expectation_ledger_extra_key", valid_test, authority=bad_expected)

    mutant("archive_in_test", encode_frame(4, b"archive"))
    mutant("json_in_archive", encode_frame(1, canonical(summary)), phase="archive")
    mutant("archive_duplicate", valid_archive + valid_archive, phase="archive")
    mutant("archive_zero_length", encode_frame(4, b""), phase="archive")
    mutant("archive_over_cap", encode_frame(4, b"", length=268435457), phase="archive")
    mutant("archive_truncated", encode_frame(4, b"abc", length=4), phase="archive")

    results = []
    for name, phase, stream, authority, wanted in cases:
        actual = decide(stream, phase, authority)
        if actual != wanted:
            reject("selftest decision: " + name)
        results.append({"decision": actual, "name": name})
    accepted = sum(item["decision"] == "ACCEPTED" for item in results)
    rejected = len(results) - accepted
    return {"accepted": accepted, "rejected": rejected, "results": results,
        "schemaVersion": 4}


def sample_bindings(output=True):
    return {"authorityOracle": "/authority/d007d-custody-oracle-v4.py",
        "bwrap": "/usr/bin/bwrap", "candidateProgram":
        "/candidate/tests/gateway/run_process_supervisor_session_port_real_host.sh",
        "candidateSha": "a" * 40, "controlSocket": "/run/user/1000/ao-d7/control",
        "env": "/usr/bin/env", "fixedMode": "cp1-unit-custody",
        "hostCandidate": "/held/candidate", "hostDeps": "/held/deps",
        "hostOdb": "/held/odb", "hostSource": "/held/tmux-3.6a.tar.gz",
        "hostToolchain": "/held/toolchain", "nonce": "b" * 24,
        "outputPresent": output, "python": "/usr/bin/python3",
        "systemdRun": "/usr/bin/systemd-run", "unit": "d007d-v4-a-b.service"}


def vector_mutation_proof():
    b = sample_bindings()
    vectors = {"systemd": systemd_argv(b), "bwrap": bwrap_argv(b),
        "dockerProbe": docker_argv("/usr/bin/docker", "ownership-probe", "probe",
            "/held/probe.cid", "c" * 24, "a" * 40),
        "dockerBuild": docker_argv("/usr/bin/docker", "build", "build",
            "/held/build.cid", "c" * 24, "a" * 40)}
    totals = {}
    for label, expected in vectors.items():
        rejected = 0
        for index in range(len(expected)):
            changed = expected.copy()
            changed[index] += "#"
            omitted = expected[:index] + expected[index + 1:]
            moved = expected.copy()
            target = next((candidate for candidate in range(len(moved))
                if candidate != index and moved[candidate] != moved[index]), None)
            token = moved.pop(index)
            moved.insert(target, token)
            for mutant in (changed, omitted, moved):
                try:
                    exact_vector(mutant, expected, label)
                except ValueError:
                    rejected += 1
                else:
                    reject("argv mutant accepted")
        totals[label] = {"tokens": len(expected), "mutantsRejected": rejected}
    return totals


def mode_protocol_proof():
    rejected = 0
    candidate_environment = True
    for mode in MODE_ORDER:
        bindings = sample_bindings(output=True)
        bindings["fixedMode"] = mode
        systemd = systemd_argv(bindings)
        bwrap = bwrap_argv(bindings)
        candidate_binding = ["--setenv", "D007D_CANDIDATE_SHA", bindings["candidateSha"]]
        occurrences = sum(bwrap[index:index + 3] == candidate_binding
            for index in range(len(bwrap) - 2))
        candidate_environment = candidate_environment and occurrences == 1
        if systemd[-3] != mode or bwrap[-2:] != [bindings["candidateProgram"], mode] \
                or mode.startswith("--") or not candidate_environment:
            reject("bare fixed mode")
        for actual, expected, index in (
                (systemd[:], systemd, -3), (bwrap[:], bwrap, -1)):
            actual[index] = "--" + mode
            try:
                exact_vector(actual, expected, "dashed mode")
            except ValueError:
                rejected += 1
            else:
                reject("dashed mode accepted")
        changed = dict(bindings)
        changed["fixedMode"] = "--" + mode
        try:
            validate_bindings(changed)
        except ValueError:
            rejected += 1
        else:
            reject("dashed binding accepted")
    if rejected != len(MODE_ORDER) * 3:
        reject("mode protocol mutants")
    return {"bareCandidateArgvToken": True, "candidateShaEnvironmentExact":
        candidate_environment, "dashedMutantsRejected": rejected,
        "modes": list(MODE_ORDER), "protocol": "D7C2-v3"}


def state_machine_proof():
    prefix = ("AUTH_OK", "SYSTEMD_CHILD")
    after_authentication = ("UNIT_IDENTITIES", "SCM_RIGHTS",
        "BWRAP_IDENTITIES", "RELEASE_ACK")
    suffix = ("STOP_RESET", "UNIT_CGROUP_ABSENT_TWICE", "HELD_REAUTH_AND_UNLINK",
        "EVIDENCE_SYNC", "ROOT_ABSENT_TWICE")

    def bind(machine):
        for event in prefix:
            machine.advance(event)
        machine.bind_authentication({"closeOnExec": True, "d7c2": SOCKET_MECHANISM,
            "diagnostic": SOCKET_MECHANISM, "distinctChannels": True,
            "heldParentChildEndpoints": True},
            authenticate_unit_snapshot({key: True for key in AUTHENTICATION_CONJUNCTS}))
        for event in after_authentication:
            machine.advance(event)

    machine = LaunchMachine()
    bind(machine)
    machine.observe_eof("d7c2")
    machine.observe_eof("diagnostic")
    for event in ("SHIM_WAIT_ACCEPTED", "BOTH_EOF_ACCEPTED", "CGROUP_EMPTY_TWICE") + suffix:
        machine.advance(event)
    if machine.state != "DONE":
        reject("state reachability")
    permutations = (("SHIM_WAIT_ACCEPTED", "CGROUP_EMPTY_TWICE", "BOTH_EOF_ACCEPTED"),
        ("BOTH_EOF_ACCEPTED", "SHIM_WAIT_ACCEPTED", "CGROUP_EMPTY_TWICE"),
        ("BOTH_EOF_ACCEPTED", "CGROUP_EMPTY_TWICE", "SHIM_WAIT_ACCEPTED"),
        ("CGROUP_EMPTY_TWICE", "SHIM_WAIT_ACCEPTED", "BOTH_EOF_ACCEPTED"),
        ("CGROUP_EMPTY_TWICE", "BOTH_EOF_ACCEPTED", "SHIM_WAIT_ACCEPTED"))
    rejected = 0
    for order in permutations:
        mutant = LaunchMachine()
        bind(mutant)
        mutant.observe_eof("d7c2")
        mutant.observe_eof("diagnostic")
        try:
            for event in order:
                mutant.advance(event)
        except ValueError:
            rejected += 1
    early = LaunchMachine()
    bind(early)
    early.observe_eof("d7c2")
    try:
        early.advance("BOTH_EOF_ACCEPTED")
    except ValueError:
        rejected += 1
    separating_cases = []
    for name, observed_channel in (
            ("missing-d7c2-after-shim-reaped", "diagnostic"),
            ("missing-diagnostic-after-shim-reaped", "d7c2")):
        mutant = LaunchMachine()
        bind(mutant)
        mutant.advance("SHIM_WAIT_ACCEPTED")
        mutant.observe_eof(observed_channel)
        state_before = mutant.state
        try:
            mutant.advance("BOTH_EOF_ACCEPTED")
        except ValueError:
            rejected += 1
            separating_cases.append({"decision": "REJECTED", "name": name,
                "stateBeforeAttempt": state_before,
                "transitionAttempted": "BOTH_EOF_ACCEPTED"})
        else:
            reject("missing EOF accepted")
    if rejected != 8 or [item["name"] for item in separating_cases] != [
            "missing-d7c2-after-shim-reaped",
            "missing-diagnostic-after-shim-reaped"]:
        reject("state mutant total")
    return {"acceptedOrder": ["SHIM_WAIT_ACCEPTED", "BOTH_EOF_ACCEPTED",
        "CGROUP_EMPTY_TWICE"], "missingChannelCases": separating_cases,
        "mutantsRejected": rejected}


def ustar_mutation_proof():
    valid = canonical_ustar()
    validate_canonical_ustar(valid)
    first_size = len(USTAR_MEMBERS[0][1])
    first_padding = 512 + first_size
    def byte_at(offset, value):
        changed = bytearray(valid)
        changed[offset] = value
        return bytes(changed)
    def first_kind(kind):
        return ustar_header(USTAR_MEMBERS[0][0], first_size, kind) + valid[512:]
    mutants = [
        ("bad_magic", byte_at(257, ord("x"))),
        ("invalid_octal_digit", byte_at(124, ord("8"))),
        ("base_256_size", byte_at(124, 0x80)),
        ("alternate_octal_terminator", byte_at(135, ord(" "))),
        ("bad_checksum", byte_at(148, ord("7"))),
        ("wrong_size", ustar_header(USTAR_MEMBERS[0][0], 513) + valid[512:]),
        ("nonzero_padding", byte_at(first_padding, 1)),
        ("duplicate", canonical_ustar((USTAR_MEMBERS[0], USTAR_MEMBERS[0], USTAR_MEMBERS[2]))),
        ("reorder", canonical_ustar((USTAR_MEMBERS[1], USTAR_MEMBERS[0], USTAR_MEMBERS[2]))),
        ("extra_member", canonical_ustar(USTAR_MEMBERS + (("extra", b"x"),))),
        ("pax", first_kind(b"x")), ("gnu_longname", first_kind(b"L")),
        ("symlink", first_kind(b"2")), ("hardlink", first_kind(b"1")),
        ("sparse", first_kind(b"S")), ("third_zero_block", valid + b"\0" * 512),
        ("trailing_byte", valid + b"x"), ("header_truncation", valid[:511]),
        ("payload_truncation", valid[:512 + first_size - 1]),
        ("one_zero_block", valid[:-512]), ("empty_archive", b""),
    ]
    rejected = []
    for name, payload in mutants:
        try:
            validate_canonical_ustar(payload)
        except (ValueError, UnicodeError):
            rejected.append(name)
        else:
            reject("USTAR mutant accepted: " + name)
    if len(rejected) != 21:
        reject("USTAR mutant total")
    return {"accepted": 1, "mutants": 21, "rejected": rejected}


def evidence_replay_proof():
    fd = os.memfd_create("d007d-evidence-v4", os.MFD_CLOEXEC)
    records = [{"seq": index, "type": kind} for index, kind in enumerate(
        ("AUTH", "ARGV", "STATE", "DOCKER", "CLEANUP", "DONE"))]
    try:
        for record in records:
            write_all(fd, canonical(record) + b"\n")
        os.fdatasync(fd)
        os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
        replay = [json.loads(line) for line in os.read(fd, 65536).splitlines()]
        if replay != records or [item["seq"] for item in replay] != list(range(len(records))):
            reject("evidence replay")
        return {"records": len(replay), "sha256": hashlib.sha256(
            b"".join(canonical(record) + b"\n" for record in records)).hexdigest()}
    finally:
        os.close(fd)


def docker_recorder_proof():
    branches = DockerRecorder.branch_proof()
    if {branch: value["count"] for branch, value in branches.items()} != {
            "MISSING": 3, "ALREADY_EXITED": 6, "TERM_EXITED": 8,
            "KILL_EXITED": 10}:
        reject("docker reconciliation counts")
    return {"normalOperations": 20, "reconciliationBranches": branches,
        "residue": 0, "transactionSequences": ["%02d" % value
            for value in range(1, 11)]}


def attack_effect(domain, attack):
    if domain not in DOMAINS or attack not in ATTACKS:
        reject("attack enum")
    if attack in ("chmod_restore", "evidence_forgery", "mount_discovery_write"):
        fd = os.memfd_create("d007d-attack", os.MFD_CLOEXEC | os.MFD_ALLOW_SEALING)
        write_all(fd, b"held")
        if domain == "v4":
            fcntl.fcntl(fd, fcntl.F_ADD_SEALS, fcntl.F_SEAL_WRITE | fcntl.F_SEAL_SHRINK
                | fcntl.F_SEAL_GROW | fcntl.F_SEAL_SEAL)
            try:
                os.write(fd, b"x")
            except OSError as error:
                if error.errno not in (errno.EPERM, errno.EBADF):
                    raise
            else:
                reject("sealed write accepted")
        else:
            os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
            write_all(fd, b"evil")
        os.close(fd)
    elif attack in ("output_replacement", "restore_before_final"):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="d007d-v4-") as root:
            path = os.path.join(root, "output")
            replacement = os.path.join(root, "replacement")
            with open(path, "wb") as stream:
                stream.write(b"held")
            held = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC)
            with open(replacement, "wb") as stream:
                stream.write(b"evil")
            os.replace(replacement, path)
            observed = os.read(held, 4) if domain == "v4" else open(path, "rb").read()
            os.close(held)
            if observed != (b"held" if domain == "v4" else b"evil"):
                reject("replacement effect")
    elif attack == "retained_fd_child":
        if domain == "v4":
            delayed_writer_proof()
        else:
            read_fd, write_fd = os.pipe()
            os.close(read_fd)
            os.close(write_fd)
    elif attack == "term_resistant":
        child = os.fork()
        if child == 0:
            signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIG_IGN)
            while True:
                signal.pause()
        os.kill(child, signal.SIGTERM)
        os.kill(child, signal.SIGKILL)
        os.waitpid(child, 0)
    elif attack == "ustar_mutation":
        ustar_mutation_proof()
    elif attack == "docker_escape":
        expected = docker_argv("/usr/bin/docker", "build", "build", "/held/build.cid",
            "c" * 24, "a" * 40)
        recorder = DockerRecorder()
        mutant = expected.copy()
        mutant[0] = "/candidate/docker"
        try:
            recorder.accept(expected, mutant, "build", 1)
        except ValueError:
            pass
        else:
            reject("docker escape accepted")
        if recorder.contacted:
            reject("docker contacted")
    return {"contained": domain == "v4", "effects": 1}


def attack_result(domain, attack):
    attack_effect(domain, attack)
    return {"attack": attack, "done": domain == "historical", "domain": domain,
        "exit": 0, "outcome": "VULNERABLE" if domain == "historical" else "REJECTED",
        "residue": 0}


def v4_selftest():
    frame = frame_selftest()
    attacks = [attack_result(domain, attack) for domain in DOMAINS for attack in ATTACKS]
    transcript = {"archiveDelayedEof": delayed_writer_proof(), "argv": vector_mutation_proof(),
        "attacks": attacks, "contracts": contract_proof(), "deadlines": deadline_proof(),
        "docker": docker_recorder_proof(), "evidence": evidence_replay_proof(),
        "frames": {"accepted": frame["accepted"], "rejected": frame["rejected"]},
        "modeProtocol": mode_protocol_proof(),
        "parentCore": parent_core_proof("direct"),
        "reachedContracts": REACHED_CONTRACTS, "schemaVersion": 4,
        "state": state_machine_proof(), "ustar": ustar_mutation_proof()}
    print(canonical(transcript).decode("ascii"))


def unit_shim(path, mode, candidate, nonce):
    if mode not in MODES or not HEX40.fullmatch(candidate) or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{24}", nonce):
        reject("shim argv")
    control = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_SEQPACKET | socket.SOCK_CLOEXEC)
    if control.fileno() != 3:
        reject("unexpected control fd")
    control.connect(path)
    hello = canonical({"candidateSha": candidate, "mode": mode, "nonce": nonce,
        "pid": os.getpid(), "type": "HELLO"})
    control.send(hello)
    body, ancillary, _flags, _address = control.recvmsg(65536, socket.CMSG_SPACE(5 * 4))
    if _flags & (socket.MSG_CTRUNC | socket.MSG_TRUNC):
        reject("shim truncated message")
    message = json_payload(body)
    exact_keys(message, ("bindings", "bwrapArgv", "candidateSha", "fdRoles", "mode", "nonce", "type"))
    bindings = validate_bindings(message["bindings"])
    if (bindings["controlSocket"] != path or bindings["fixedMode"] != mode
            or bindings["candidateSha"] != candidate or bindings["nonce"] != nonce):
        reject("shim bindings")
    roles = ["d7c2", "diagnostic"]
    if bindings["outputPresent"]:
        roles.append("heldOutput")
    roles.append("fdSeal")
    argv = list(exact_vector(message["bwrapArgv"], bwrap_argv(bindings), "bwrap"))
    if message != {"bindings": bindings, "bwrapArgv": argv, "candidateSha": candidate, "fdRoles": roles,
            "mode": mode, "nonce": nonce, "type": "START"}:
        reject("shim start")
    rights = []
    rights_messages = 0
    for level, kind, data in ancillary:
        if level == socket.SOL_SOCKET and kind == socket.SCM_RIGHTS:
            rights_messages += 1
            rights.extend(struct.unpack("%di" % (len(data) // 4), data))
        else:
            reject("shim ancillary")
    if (rights_messages != 1 or len(rights) != len(roles)
            or rights != list(range(4, 4 + len(roles)))):
        reject("shim rights")
    release_read, release_write = os.pipe2(os.O_CLOEXEC)
    if [release_read, release_write] != list(range(4 + len(roles), 6 + len(roles))):
        reject("release pipe fds")
    pid = os.fork()
    if pid == 0:
        os.close(release_write)
        os.dup2(rights[0], 1, inheritable=True)
        os.dup2(rights[1], 2, inheritable=True)
        os.dup2(release_read, 3, inheritable=True)
        if "heldOutput" in roles:
            os.dup2(rights[2], 4, inheritable=True)
        os.dup2(rights[-1], 5, inheritable=True)
        if "heldOutput" not in roles:
            os.close(4)
        libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)
        if libc.syscall(SYS_close_range, 6, 0xFFFFFFFF, 2) != 0:
            os._exit(70)
        os.execve(argv[0], argv, {"LANG": "C", "LC_ALL": "C", "TZ": "UTC"})
    os.close(release_read)
    for received in rights:
        os.close(received)
    libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)
    pidfd = libc.syscall(SYS_pidfd_open, pid, 0)
    if pidfd < 0:
        os.kill(pid, signal.SIGKILL)
        os.waitpid(pid, 0)
        os.close(release_write)
        reject("pidfd_open")
    control.send(canonical({"pid": pid, "type": "BWRAP"}))
    try:
        release, extra, flags, _address = control.recvmsg(65536, socket.CMSG_SPACE(4))
        if flags & (socket.MSG_CTRUNC | socket.MSG_TRUNC) or extra:
            reject("release ancillary")
        release_message = json_payload(release)
        exact_keys(release_message, ("nonce", "pid", "type"))
        if release_message != {"nonce": nonce, "pid": pid, "type": "RELEASE"}:
            reject("release message")
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        kill_result = libc.syscall(SYS_pidfd_send_signal, pidfd, signal.SIGKILL, 0, 0)
        if kill_result != 0 and ctypes.get_errno() != 3:
            os._exit(70)
        os.waitpid(pid, 0)
        os.close(pidfd)
        os.close(release_write)
        raise
    write_all(release_write, b"R")
    os.close(release_write)
    control.send(canonical({"pid": pid, "type": "RELEASED"}))
    waited, status = os.waitpid(pid, 0)
    os.close(pidfd)
    if waited != pid:
        reject("shim wait")
    code = os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status)
    control.send(canonical({"exit": code, "pid": pid, "type": "EXIT"}))
    return code


def attacks(candidate, ledger, run_root):
    if (not ledger.startswith("/") or not run_root.startswith("/")
            or not HEX40.fullmatch(candidate) or any("\0" in value for value in
                (ledger, run_root))):
        reject("attack argv")
    totals = {"order": ["tests", "pass", "fail", "skipped", "cancelled"],
        "historical": [9, 0, 9, 0, 0], "v4": [9, 9, 0, 0, 0]}
    results = []
    authority = os.open(os.path.abspath(__file__), os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC)
    authority_digest = hashlib.sha256(os.read(authority, os.fstat(authority).st_size)).hexdigest()
    for domain in DOMAINS:
        for attack in ATTACKS:
            os.lseek(authority, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
            if hashlib.sha256(os.read(authority, os.fstat(authority).st_size)).hexdigest() != authority_digest:
                reject("authority drift")
            child_path = "/proc/self/fd/" + str(authority)
            run = subprocess.run([sys.executable, child_path, "attack", domain, attack,
                    candidate, ledger, run_root], pass_fds=(authority,),
                stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
                env=FROZEN_ENV, timeout=20, check=False)
            if len(run.stderr) > 65536:
                reject("attack stderr cap")
            if not run.stdout.endswith(b"\n") or b"\n" in run.stdout[:-1]:
                reject("attack stdout framing")
            value = json_payload(run.stdout[:-1])
            exact_keys(value, ("attack", "done", "domain", "exit", "outcome", "residue"))
            expected = "VULNERABLE" if domain == "historical" else "REJECTED"
            expected_done = domain == "historical"
            if run.returncode != 0:
                reject("attack driver exit")
            if value != {"attack": attack, "done": expected_done, "domain": domain,
                    "exit": 0, "outcome": expected, "residue": 0}:
                reject("attack result")
            results.append(value)
    os.close(authority)
    print(canonical({"parentCore": direct_owner_proof("attacks"),
        "results": results, "totals": totals}).decode("ascii"))


def frames_public(phase, expected_text, sink_text):
    expected = json_payload(expected_text.encode("utf-8"))
    if phase == "archive":
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[1-9][0-9]*", sink_text):
            reject("archive fd grammar")
        sink = int(sink_text)
        validate_archive_sink(sink)
        validate_frames(0, phase, expected, sink)
        print(canonical({"archive": seal_archive(sink), "decision": "ACCEPTED"}).decode("ascii"))
    elif phase == "test":
        if sink_text != "-":
            reject("test archive fd")
        validate_frames(0, phase, expected)
        print(canonical({"decision": "ACCEPTED", "phase": "test"}).decode("ascii"))
    else:
        reject("frame phase")


def contracts():
    print(canonical({"contracts": contract_proof(),
        "modeProtocol": mode_protocol_proof(),
        "parentCore": direct_owner_proof("contracts"),
        "reachedContracts": REACHED_CONTRACTS}).decode("ascii"))


def inherited_fds():
    result = []
    for value in os.listdir("/proc/self/fd"):
        fd = int(value)
        if fd <= 2:
            continue
        try:
            os.fstat(fd)
        except OSError as error:
            if error.errno != errno.EBADF:
                raise
        else:
            result.append(fd)
    return result


def held_path(path, directory=False, executable=False):
    if not path.startswith("/") or "\0" in path:
        reject("held path")
    flags = os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC
    if directory:
        flags |= os.O_DIRECTORY
    fd = os.open(path, flags)
    metadata = os.fstat(fd)
    expected_type = stat.S_ISDIR(metadata.st_mode) if directory else stat.S_ISREG(metadata.st_mode)
    if (not expected_type or metadata.st_uid != os.geteuid()
            or metadata.st_mode & (stat.S_IWGRP | stat.S_IWOTH)):
        os.close(fd)
        reject("held identity")
    if executable and not metadata.st_mode & stat.S_IXUSR:
        os.close(fd)
        reject("held executable")
    return fd


def read_proc_bytes(pid, name, limit):
    if type(pid) is not int or pid <= 0 or name not in ("cmdline", "stat"):
        reject("proc identity request")
    fd = os.open("/proc/%d/%s" % (pid, name),
        os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC)
    try:
        value = os.read(fd, limit + 1)
    finally:
        os.close(fd)
    if not value or len(value) > limit:
        reject("proc identity bounds")
    return value


def pidfd_live(pidfd):
    libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)
    result = libc.syscall(SYS_pidfd_send_signal, pidfd, 0, 0, 0)
    if result == 0:
        return True
    if ctypes.get_errno() == errno.ESRCH:
        return False
    raise OSError(ctypes.get_errno(), "pidfd liveness")


def exact_pidfd(pid):
    if type(pid) is not int or pid <= 0:
        reject("pidfd pid")
    libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)
    pidfd = libc.syscall(SYS_pidfd_open, pid, 0)
    if pidfd < 0:
        raise OSError(ctypes.get_errno(), "pidfd_open")
    try:
        info_fd = os.open("/proc/self/fdinfo/%d" % pidfd,
            os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC)
        try:
            info = os.read(info_fd, 4096)
        finally:
            os.close(info_fd)
        values = [line.split(b":", 1)[1].strip() for line in info.splitlines()
            if line.startswith(b"Pid:")]
        if values != [str(pid).encode("ascii")] or not pidfd_live(pidfd):
            reject("pidfd identity")
        return pidfd
    except BaseException:
        os.close(pidfd)
        raise


def process_start_token(pid):
    raw_stat = read_proc_bytes(pid, "stat", 4096)
    prefix, separator, tail = raw_stat.rpartition(b") ")
    fields = tail.split()
    if (not separator or not prefix.startswith(str(pid).encode("ascii") + b" (")
            or len(fields) < 20 or not fields[19].isdigit()):
        reject("process start token")
    return fields[19].decode("ascii")


def process_identity_snapshot(pid, executable_fd, expected_argv, expected_parent=None):
    if (type(expected_argv) not in (list, tuple) or not expected_argv
            or any(type(value) is not str or "\0" in value for value in expected_argv)):
        reject("process argv expectation")
    pidfd = exact_pidfd(pid)
    try:
        executable = os.open("/proc/%d/exe" % pid, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_CLOEXEC)
        try:
            actual_executable = os.fstat(executable)
            held_executable = os.fstat(executable_fd)
        finally:
            os.close(executable)
        command = read_proc_bytes(pid, "cmdline", 131072)
        expected_command = b"\0".join(os.fsencode(value) for value in expected_argv) + b"\0"
        raw_stat = read_proc_bytes(pid, "stat", 4096)
        prefix, separator, tail = raw_stat.rpartition(b") ")
        fields = tail.split()
        if not separator or not prefix.startswith(str(pid).encode("ascii") + b" (") \
                or len(fields) < 20 or not fields[1].isdigit() or not fields[19].isdigit():
            reject("process stat identity")
        parent = int(fields[1])
        return pidfd, {"argvExact": command == expected_command,
            "executableHeld": (actual_executable.st_dev, actual_executable.st_ino) ==
                (held_executable.st_dev, held_executable.st_ino),
            "parentExact": expected_parent is None or parent == expected_parent,
            "pidfdLive": pidfd_live(pidfd), "startTokenExact": bool(fields[19])}
    except BaseException:
        os.close(pidfd)
        raise


def held_cgroup_members(cgroup_procs_fd):
    value = os.pread(cgroup_procs_fd, 1048577, 0)
    if len(value) > 1048576:
        reject("cgroup membership bounds")
    lines = value.splitlines()
    if any(not line.isdigit() for line in lines):
        reject("cgroup membership syntax")
    return {int(line) for line in lines}


def held_control_group(control_group, main_pid):
    if type(control_group) is not str or not control_group.startswith("/") \
            or "\0" in control_group:
        reject("control group identity")
    components = control_group[1:].split("/") if control_group != "/" else []
    if any(not component or component in (".", "..") for component in components):
        reject("control group components")
    opened = [os.open("/sys/fs/cgroup",
        os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC)]
    try:
        for component in components:
            opened.append(os.open(component,
                os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC,
                dir_fd=opened[-1]))
        group_fd = opened[-1]
        for fd in opened[:-1]:
            os.close(fd)
        opened = [group_fd]
        procs_fd = os.open("cgroup.procs", os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC,
            dir_fd=group_fd)
        opened.append(procs_fd)
        membership = main_pid in held_cgroup_members(procs_fd)
        opened = []
        return group_fd, procs_fd, membership
    except BaseException:
        for fd in reversed(opened):
            try:
                os.close(fd)
            except OSError:
                pass
        raise


def append_evidence(fd, sequence, kind, value):
    record = {"kind": kind, "seq": sequence, "value": value}
    write_all(fd, canonical(record) + b"\n")
    os.fdatasync(fd)
    return sequence + 1


PARENT_PHASES = (("archive", False), ("test", True))
PARENT_EXECUTION_ORDER = ("unit:archive", "docker:ownership-probe", "docker:build",
    "output:sealed", "unit:test")
HELD_OUTPUT_SEALS = (fcntl.F_SEAL_WRITE | fcntl.F_SEAL_SHRINK
    | fcntl.F_SEAL_GROW | fcntl.F_SEAL_SEAL)
PARENT_TRANSITIONS = tuple(item[1] for item in LAUNCH_TRANSITIONS)
PARENT_UNIT_EVENTS = PARENT_TRANSITIONS[:-3]
PARENT_FINAL_EVENTS = ("HELD_REAUTH_AND_UNLINK", "EVIDENCE_SYNC", "ROOT_ABSENT_TWICE")
PARENT_LIMITS = {"operation": 10, "ownership-probe": 60, "build": 700,
    "reconciliation": 100, "finalization": 40, "outer": 900}
PARENT_ENTRY_POINTS = ("run", "direct", "contracts", "attacks")
A1_OWNER_MUTANTS = ("entry-run", "entry-direct", "entry-contracts", "entry-attacks",
    "owner-executor", "executor-adapter", "fabricated-event-vector",
    "fabricated-d7c2-eof", "fabricated-diagnostic-eof", "cleanup-retry",
    "second-cleanup-owner")
A2_OWNER_MUTANTS = ("pipe-regression", "channel-alias") + tuple(
    "auth-" + key for key in AUTHENTICATION_CONJUNCTS) + (
    "stale-reresolution", "premature-release")
DOCKER_GRAMMAR_FAMILIES = ("inspect", "wait", "kill", "remove", "scan")
DOCKER_GRAMMAR_MUTANTS = tuple("grammar-" + family + "-" + mutation
    for family in DOCKER_GRAMMAR_FAMILIES
    for mutation in ("delete", "substitute", "reorder", "extra"))
B1_OWNER_MUTANTS = ("owner-runner-edge", "runner-owner-edge",
    "caller-supplied-summary", "generic-subprocess-regression") + DOCKER_GRAMMAR_MUTANTS + (
    "ambient-env-inheritance", "wrong-home", "missing-home", "wrong-docker-config",
    "missing-docker-config", "wrong-docker-host", "missing-docker-host", "missing-pidfd",
    "non-live-pidfd", "pid-reresolution", "wrong-start-token", "missing-terminal-status",
    "missing-start-time", "missing-end-time", "missing-stdout-count", "missing-stderr-count",
    "missing-stdout-hash", "missing-stderr-hash")
B2_OWNER_MUTANTS = ("missing-cid-metadata", "wrong-cid-mode", "wrong-cid-shape",
    "transaction-delete", "transaction-reorder", "transaction-extra",
    "indirect-held-output", "held-output-hash-mismatch", "unsealed-held-output")
OWNER_MUTANTS = A1_OWNER_MUTANTS + A2_OWNER_MUTANTS + B1_OWNER_MUTANTS + B2_OWNER_MUTANTS
_SELFTEST_EXECUTOR_SEAL = object()
_OWNER_OBSERVATION_SEAL = object()
_DOCKER_RUNNER_OBSERVATION_SEAL = object()
_DOCKER_B2_OBSERVATION_SEAL = object()


class InfrastructureUnavailable(RuntimeError):
    pass


def docker_control_argv(docker, operation, identity, run_id=None, role=None):
    if operation in ("inspect", "inspect-id", "inspect-name"):
        return [docker, "inspect", "--type", "container", identity]
    if operation == "wait":
        return [docker, "container", "wait", identity]
    if operation == "term":
        return [docker, "container", "kill", "--signal", "TERM", identity]
    if operation == "kill":
        return [docker, "container", "kill", "--signal", "KILL", identity]
    if operation == "remove":
        return [docker, "container", "rm", "--force", identity]
    if operation in ("scan", "probe-1", "probe-2"):
        if (type(run_id) is not str or type(role) is not str or not run_id or not role
                or "\0" in run_id or "\0" in role):
            reject("docker scan identity")
        return [docker, "ps", "-aq", "--filter",
            "label=org.agents-orchestrator.d007d.run=" + run_id, "--filter",
            "label=org.agents-orchestrator.d007d.role=" + role]
    reject("docker control operation")


def docker_run_identity(argv, role):
    if role not in DOCKER_ROLES or type(argv) is not list:
        reject("docker run identity")
    try:
        name = argv[argv.index("--name") + 1]
    except (IndexError, ValueError):
        reject("docker run labels")
    run_labels = [value for value in argv
        if value.startswith("org.agents-orchestrator.d007d.run=")]
    role_label = "org.agents-orchestrator.d007d.role=" + role
    candidates = [value.split("=", 1)[1] for value in argv
        if value.startswith("org.agents-orchestrator.d007d.candidate=")]
    if (len(run_labels) != 1 or argv.count(role_label) != 1 or len(candidates) != 1
            or "=" not in run_labels[0]
            or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{24}", run_labels[0].split("=", 1)[1])
            or not HEX40.fullmatch(candidates[0]) or not name):
        reject("docker run labels")
    run_id = run_labels[0].split("=", 1)[1]
    expected_name = "d007d-v4-" + run_id + (
        "-probe" if role == "ownership-probe" else "-build")
    if name != expected_name:
        reject("docker role name")
    return name, run_id, candidates[0]


class OwnerObservationSink:
    def __init__(self, seal, owner, phase, machine):
        if seal is not _OWNER_OBSERVATION_SEAL:
            reject("observation sink owner")
        self._owner = owner
        self._phase = phase
        self._machine = machine

    def edge(self, value):
        self._owner.observe_route(self, value)

    def eof(self, channel):
        self._owner.observe_eof(self, channel)

    def transition(self, event):
        self._owner.observe_transition(self, event)

    def authentication(self, socket_mechanisms, observations):
        self._owner.observe_authentication(self, socket_mechanisms, observations)


class DockerObservationSink:
    def __init__(self, seal, owner, role, argv, environment):
        if seal is not _OWNER_OBSERVATION_SEAL:
            reject("docker observation sink owner")
        self._owner = owner
        self._role = role
        self._argv = list(argv)
        self._environment = dict(environment)
        self._observed = False

    def observation(self, seal, value):
        if seal is not _DOCKER_RUNNER_OBSERVATION_SEAL or self._observed:
            reject("docker runner observation authority")
        self._owner.observe_docker_child(self, value)
        self._observed = True


class DockerB2ObservationSink:
    def __init__(self, seal, owner, role, argv, environment, name, run_id, candidate):
        if seal is not _OWNER_OBSERVATION_SEAL:
            reject("docker B2 observation sink owner")
        self._owner = owner
        self._role = role
        self._argv = list(argv)
        self._environment = dict(environment)
        self._name = name
        self._run_id = run_id
        self._candidate = candidate
        self._observed = False

    def observation(self, seal, value):
        if seal is not _DOCKER_B2_OBSERVATION_SEAL or self._observed:
            reject("docker B2 observation authority")
        self._owner.observe_docker_transaction(self, value)
        self._observed = True


class InjectedUnitExecutor:
    def bind_unit_adapter(self, adapter):
        if not callable(adapter):
            reject("unit adapter")
        self._injected_unit_adapter = adapter

    def bind_docker_child_runner(self, runner):
        if not callable(runner):
            reject("docker child runner")
        self._injected_docker_child_runner = runner

    def unit(self, phase, bindings, systemd, bwrap, observation_sink):
        if type(observation_sink) is not OwnerObservationSink:
            reject("owner observation sink")
        observation_sink.edge("executor.unit->injectedAdapter")
        return self._injected_unit_adapter(
            phase, bindings, systemd, bwrap, observation_sink)

    def authenticate(self, observation_sink, channels, observations):
        if type(observation_sink) is not OwnerObservationSink:
            reject("authentication observation sink")
        socket_mechanisms = socket_custody_observation(channels)
        authentication = authenticate_unit_snapshot(observations)
        observation_sink.authentication(socket_mechanisms, authentication)

    def docker(self, role, argv, environment, timeout, observation_sink, transaction_sink):
        if type(observation_sink) is not DockerObservationSink:
            reject("docker observation sink")
        if type(transaction_sink) is not DockerB2ObservationSink:
            reject("docker B2 observation sink")
        environment = validate_docker_environment(environment)
        result = self._injected_docker_child_runner(
            role, list(argv), environment, timeout, observation_sink.observation,
            transaction_sink.observation)
        if result is not None:
            reject("caller-supplied docker summary")
        if not observation_sink._observed:
            reject("missing docker runner observation")
        if not transaction_sink._observed:
            reject("missing docker B2 observation")


class ParentCore:
    def __init__(self, entry, executor, expected, bindings, vectors, evidence_fd, sequence):
        self.entry = entry
        self.executor = executor
        self.expected = expected
        self.bindings = bindings
        self.vectors = vectors
        self.evidence_fd = evidence_fd
        self.sequence = sequence
        self.machines = []
        self.observation_sinks = []
        self.observed_eof_edges = []
        self.observed_transition_edges = []
        self.observed_authentication_edges = []
        self.docker_observation_sinks = []
        self.docker_child_observations = []
        self.docker_b2_observation_sinks = []
        self.docker_transactions = []
        self.docker_route = []
        self.execution_order = []
        self.route = []
        self.cleanup_invocations = 0
        self.terminal_state = "REJECTED"

    def record(self, kind, value):
        if self.evidence_fd is not None:
            self.sequence = append_evidence(self.evidence_fd, self.sequence, kind, value)

    def owns(self, sink):
        if (type(sink) is not OwnerObservationSink or sink._owner is not self
                or sink not in self.observation_sinks):
            reject("foreign observation sink")

    def observe_route(self, sink, value):
        self.owns(sink)
        if value != "executor.unit->injectedAdapter" and value != \
                "injectedAdapter->ParentCore.observationSink":
            reject("observation route")
        if value not in self.route:
            self.route.append(value)

    def observe_eof(self, sink, channel):
        self.owns(sink)
        sink._machine.observe_eof(channel)
        self.observed_eof_edges.append(sink._phase + ":" + channel)

    def observe_transition(self, sink, event):
        self.owns(sink)
        sink._machine.advance(event)
        self.observed_transition_edges.append(sink._phase + ":" + event)

    def observe_authentication(self, sink, socket_mechanisms, observations):
        self.owns(sink)
        sink._machine.bind_authentication(socket_mechanisms, observations)
        self.observed_authentication_edges.append(sink._phase + ":AUTHENTICATION_SNAPSHOT")

    def observe_docker_child(self, sink, observation):
        if (type(sink) is not DockerObservationSink or sink._owner is not self
                or sink not in self.docker_observation_sinks):
            reject("foreign docker observation sink")
        value = validate_docker_child_observation(
            observation, sink._argv, sink._environment)
        self.docker_route.append(
            "injectedDockerChildRunner->ParentCore.dockerObservationSink")
        self.docker_child_observations.append(
            {"observation": value, "role": sink._role})

    def observe_docker_transaction(self, sink, observation):
        if (type(sink) is not DockerB2ObservationSink or sink._owner is not self
                or sink not in self.docker_b2_observation_sinks):
            reject("foreign docker B2 observation sink")
        value = validate_docker_transaction(observation, sink._role, sink._argv,
            sink._environment, sink._name, sink._run_id, sink._candidate)
        self.docker_route.append(
            "injectedDockerChildRunner->ParentCore.dockerB2ObservationSink")
        self.docker_transactions.append(value)

    def invoke_unit(self, phase, bindings, systemd, bwrap, sink):
        if "ParentCore.execute->executor.unit" not in self.route:
            self.route.append("ParentCore.execute->executor.unit")
        return self.executor.unit(phase, bindings, systemd, bwrap, sink)

    def cleanup_once(self, error):
        if self.cleanup_invocations != 0:
            return RuntimeError("parent cleanup already invoked")
        self.route.append("ParentCore.execute->executor.cleanup")
        self.cleanup_invocations += 1
        try:
            residue = self.executor.cleanup(error, tuple(self.observation_sinks))
            if type(residue) is not int or residue != 0:
                return RuntimeError("parent cleanup residue")
        except BaseException as caught:
            return caught
        return None

    def abort_preparation(self, error):
        cleanup_error = self.cleanup_once(error)
        self.terminal_state = "REJECTED"
        if cleanup_error is not None:
            raise RuntimeError("parent cleanup residue") from error

    def execute_phase(self, phase, output_present):
        machine = LaunchMachine()
        sink = OwnerObservationSink(
            _OWNER_OBSERVATION_SEAL, self, phase, machine)
        self.machines.append(machine)
        self.observation_sinks.append(sink)
        bindings = dict(self.bindings)
        bindings.update(self.executor.phase_bindings(phase, output_present))
        expected_systemd = systemd_argv(bindings)
        expected_bwrap = bwrap_argv(bindings)
        systemd = exact_vector(self.executor.vector(phase + ":systemd", expected_systemd),
            expected_systemd, phase + " systemd")
        bwrap = exact_vector(self.executor.vector(phase + ":bwrap", expected_bwrap),
            expected_bwrap, phase + " bwrap")
        result = self.invoke_unit(phase, bindings, systemd, bwrap, sink)
        if type(result) is not dict:
            reject("parent unit result type")
        exact_keys(result, ("d7c2", "diagnostic", "exit", "phase"))
        if (type(result["d7c2"]) is not bytes
                or type(result["diagnostic"]) is not bytes
                or result["phase"] != phase or result["exit"] != 0
                or machine.state != "ABSENT"):
            reject("parent unit result")
        self.executor.frames(phase, self.expected, result)
        self.execution_order.append("unit:" + phase)
        self.record("UNIT", {"observations": [edge for edge in
            self.observed_transition_edges if edge.startswith(phase + ":")],
            "phase": phase})

    def execute(self):
        error = None
        cleanup_error = None
        operations = 0
        try:
            if self.entry not in PARENT_ENTRY_POINTS:
                reject("parent entry edge")
            self.route.append(self.entry + "->ParentCore.execute")
            self.execute_phase(*PARENT_PHASES[0])
            recorder = DockerRecorder()
            for role in DOCKER_ROLES:
                expected = self.vectors["dockerProbe" if role == "ownership-probe" else "dockerBuild"]
                actual = self.executor.vector("docker:" + role, expected)
                exact_vector(actual, expected, "docker")
                environment = self.executor.docker_environment(role)
                name, run_id, candidate = docker_run_identity(actual, role)
                sink = DockerObservationSink(
                    _OWNER_OBSERVATION_SEAL, self, role, actual, environment)
                transaction_sink = DockerB2ObservationSink(_OWNER_OBSERVATION_SEAL,
                    self, role, actual, environment, name, run_id, candidate)
                self.docker_observation_sinks.append(sink)
                self.docker_b2_observation_sinks.append(transaction_sink)
                if not getattr(self, "_delete_docker_owner_edge", False):
                    self.docker_route.append("ParentCore.execute->executor.docker")
                    self.docker_route.append("executor.docker->injectedDockerChildRunner")
                    self.executor.docker(
                        role, actual, environment, PARENT_LIMITS[role], sink,
                        transaction_sink)
                if not transaction_sink._observed:
                    reject("missing docker transaction")
                recorder.transaction(role, self.docker_transactions[-1], actual,
                    environment, name, run_id, candidate)
                self.execution_order.append("docker:" + role)
                self.record("DOCKER", {"operations": 10, "role": role})
            self.executor.validate_and_seal_output()
            self.execution_order.append("output:sealed")
            self.record("OUTPUT", {"state": "SEALED"})
            self.execute_phase(*PARENT_PHASES[1])
            if tuple(self.execution_order) != PARENT_EXECUTION_ORDER:
                reject("parent execution order")
            self.executor.finalize(PARENT_LIMITS["finalization"])
            operations = len(recorder.operations)
            self.record("FINAL", {"machines": [machine.state for machine in self.machines],
                "operations": operations})
        except BaseException as caught:
            error = caught
        cleanup_error = self.cleanup_once(error)
        if error is None and cleanup_error is None:
            try:
                if not self.machines or any(machine.state != "DONE" for machine in self.machines):
                    reject("parent terminal state")
                if tuple(self.execution_order) != PARENT_EXECUTION_ORDER:
                    reject("parent execution order")
                if self.observed_eof_edges != ["archive:d7c2", "archive:diagnostic",
                        "test:d7c2", "test:diagnostic"]:
                    reject("parent EOF observations")
                if (self.observed_authentication_edges !=
                        ["archive:AUTHENTICATION_SNAPSHOT", "test:AUTHENTICATION_SNAPSHOT"]
                        or any(machine.authentication is None
                            or machine.release_count != 1
                            or not machine.released_after_authentication
                            for machine in self.machines)):
                    reject("parent authentication observations")
                if ([item["role"] for item in self.docker_child_observations]
                        != ["ownership-probe", "build"]
                        or len(self.docker_observation_sinks) != 2
                        or len(self.docker_b2_observation_sinks) != 2
                        or any(not sink._observed for sink in self.docker_observation_sinks)
                        or any(not sink._observed for sink in self.docker_b2_observation_sinks)
                        or self.docker_route != [
                            "ParentCore.execute->executor.docker",
                            "executor.docker->injectedDockerChildRunner",
                            "injectedDockerChildRunner->ParentCore.dockerObservationSink",
                            "injectedDockerChildRunner->ParentCore.dockerB2ObservationSink"] * 2):
                    reject("parent docker child observations")
            except BaseException as caught:
                error = caught
        if error is not None or cleanup_error is not None:
            self.terminal_state = "REJECTED"
            if cleanup_error is not None:
                raise RuntimeError("parent cleanup residue") from (error or cleanup_error)
            raise error
        self.terminal_state = "DONE"
        release_edges = [edge for edge in self.observed_transition_edges
            if edge.endswith(":RELEASE_ACK")]
        authentication = dict(self.machines[0].authentication)
        socket_mechanisms = dict(self.machines[0].socket_mechanisms)
        if (any(dict(machine.authentication) != authentication
                or dict(machine.socket_mechanisms) != socket_mechanisms
                for machine in self.machines)
                or tuple(authentication) != AUTHENTICATION_OBSERVATION_KEYS
                or tuple(socket_mechanisms) != SOCKET_OBSERVATION_KEYS):
            reject("parent authentication projection")
        build_transaction = self.docker_transactions[1]
        return {"cleanupInvocations": self.cleanup_invocations,
            "authentication": authentication,
            "dockerB2": {"cidfileReconciliation": [
                    transaction["cidfile"] for transaction in self.docker_transactions],
                "heldOutputInode": build_transaction["heldOutput"],
                "operationTransactions": list(self.docker_transactions),
                "reconciliationBranches": DockerRecorder.branch_proof()},
            "dockerChildObservations": list(self.docker_child_observations),
            "dockerObservedEdges": list(self.docker_route),
            "dockerOperations": operations,
            "executionOrder": list(self.execution_order),
            "machines": [machine.state for machine in self.machines],
            "observedEofEdges": list(self.observed_eof_edges),
            "productionAdapterEdge": "executor.unit(observationSink)->injectedAdapter",
            "productionAuthenticationEdge":
                "injectedAdapter->executor.authenticate(snapshot)->ParentCore.observationSink",
            "productionDockerEdge":
                "ParentCore.execute->executor.docker->injectedDockerChildRunner->ParentCore.dockerObservationSink",
            "releaseState": {"a2IdentityChain": "AUTHENTICATED",
                "authenticationConjunction": authentication["fullConjunction"],
                "observedEdges": release_edges,
                "releaseCounts": [machine.release_count for machine in self.machines],
                "releasedAfterAuthentication": all(
                    machine.released_after_authentication for machine in self.machines)},
            "route": list(self.route), "socketMechanisms": socket_mechanisms,
            "terminalState": self.terminal_state}


class SelftestParentExecutor(InjectedUnitExecutor):
    def __init__(self, seal, branch="MISSING", failure=None, bad_event=None, bad_vector=None,
            mutation=None):
        if seal is not _SELFTEST_EXECUTOR_SEAL:
            reject("selftest executor seal")
        self.branch = branch
        self.failure = failure
        self.bad_event = bad_event
        self.bad_vector = bad_vector
        self.mutation = mutation
        self.residue = 0
        self.cleanup_calls = 0
        self.output_sealed = False
        self.bind_unit_adapter(self.selftest_unit_adapter)
        self.bind_docker_child_runner(self.selftest_docker_child_runner)

    def phase_bindings(self, phase, output_present):
        if (phase == "archive" and output_present) or (phase == "test"
                and (not output_present or not self.output_sealed)):
            reject("selftest phase output lifecycle")
        return {"controlSocket": "/review/d007d-selftest-" + phase + ".sock",
            "nonce": ("a" if phase == "archive" else "b") * 24,
            "outputPresent": output_present,
            "unit": "d007d-v4-selftest-" + phase + ".service"}

    def vector(self, label, expected):
        value = list(expected)
        if self.bad_vector == label:
            value[-1] += "-mutant"
        return value

    def docker_environment(self, role):
        environment = docker_private_environment(
            "/review/private/home", "/review/private/docker-config")
        mutations = {
            "ambient-env-inheritance": ("HTTP_PROXY", "http://ambient.invalid"),
            "wrong-home": ("HOME", "/ambient/home"),
            "wrong-docker-config": ("DOCKER_CONFIG", "/ambient/docker"),
            "wrong-docker-host": ("DOCKER_HOST", "tcp://127.0.0.1:2375"),
        }
        if self.mutation in mutations:
            key, value = mutations[self.mutation]
            environment[key] = value
        missing = {"missing-home": "HOME", "missing-docker-config": "DOCKER_CONFIG",
            "missing-docker-host": "DOCKER_HOST"}
        if self.mutation in missing:
            environment.pop(missing[self.mutation])
        return environment

    def emit(self, sink, event):
        sink.transition("OUT_OF_ORDER" if self.bad_event == event else event)

    def authentication_observations(self):
        observations = {key: True for key in AUTHENTICATION_CONJUNCTS}
        if self.mutation is not None and self.mutation.startswith("auth-"):
            key = self.mutation[5:]
            if key not in observations:
                reject("authentication mutant")
            observations[key] = False
        elif self.mutation == "stale-reresolution":
            observations["noAuthorityReresolution"] = False
        return observations

    def authentication_channels(self):
        resources = []
        if self.mutation == "pipe-regression":
            d7c2_parent, d7c2_child = os.pipe2(os.O_CLOEXEC)
            resources.extend((d7c2_parent, d7c2_child))
        else:
            d7c2_parent, d7c2_child = socket.socketpair(
                socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM | socket.SOCK_CLOEXEC)
            resources.extend((d7c2_parent, d7c2_child))
        diagnostic_parent, diagnostic_child = socket.socketpair(
            socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM | socket.SOCK_CLOEXEC)
        resources.extend((diagnostic_parent, diagnostic_child))
        d7c2 = tuple(resources[:2])
        diagnostic = tuple(resources[2:])
        if self.mutation == "channel-alias":
            diagnostic = d7c2
        return resources, {"d7c2": d7c2, "diagnostic": diagnostic}

    def selftest_unit_adapter(self, phase, bindings, systemd, bwrap, sink):
        sink.edge("injectedAdapter->ParentCore.observationSink")
        self.residue += 1
        if self.failure == "unit:" + phase:
            raise TimeoutError(phase)
        if self.mutation == "fabricated-event-vector":
            return {"d7c2": b"", "diagnostic": b"", "events": PARENT_TRANSITIONS,
                "exit": 0, "phase": phase}
        for event in PARENT_UNIT_EVENTS[:2]:
            self.emit(sink, event)
        if self.mutation == "premature-release":
            self.emit(sink, "RELEASE_ACK")
        resources, channels = self.authentication_channels()
        try:
            self.authenticate(sink, channels, self.authentication_observations())
        finally:
            for resource in resources:
                try:
                    os.close(resource) if type(resource) is int else resource.close()
                except OSError:
                    pass
        for event in PARENT_UNIT_EVENTS[2:6]:
            self.emit(sink, event)
        if self.mutation != "fabricated-d7c2-eof":
            sink.eof("d7c2")
        if self.mutation != "fabricated-diagnostic-eof":
            sink.eof("diagnostic")
        for event in PARENT_UNIT_EVENTS[6:]:
            self.emit(sink, event)
        return {"d7c2": b"", "diagnostic": b"", "exit": 0, "phase": phase}

    def frames(self, phase, expected, result):
        if self.failure == "frames:" + phase:
            reject("selftest frames")

    def selftest_docker_transaction(self, role, argv, environment, run_observation):
        name, run_id, candidate = docker_run_identity(argv, role)
        cid = ("c" if role == "ownership-probe" else "d") * 64
        argvs = [
            docker_control_argv(argv[0], "inspect", name),
            docker_control_argv(argv[0], "scan", cid, run_id, role),
            list(argv),
            docker_control_argv(argv[0], "inspect", cid),
            docker_control_argv(argv[0], "wait", cid),
            docker_control_argv(argv[0], "inspect", cid),
            docker_control_argv(argv[0], "remove", cid),
            docker_control_argv(argv[0], "inspect", name),
            docker_control_argv(argv[0], "scan", cid, run_id, role),
            docker_control_argv(argv[0], "scan", cid, run_id, role),
        ]
        operations = []
        for index, (token, operation_argv) in enumerate(
                zip(DOCKER_NORMAL_TOKENS, argvs), 1):
            observation = run_observation if index == 3 else docker_child_observation(
                operation_argv, environment, 10000 + index * 2, 10001 + index * 2,
                True, str(300 + index), 1 if index in (1, 8) else 0,
                b"", b"")
            results = ("not-found", "empty", "client-exit-0", "exited", "exit-0",
                "exited-0", "removed", "not-found", "empty", "empty")
            result = results[index - 1]
            operations.append(docker_operation(index, token, operation_argv,
                observation, result))
        held_output = None
        if role == "build":
            held_output = {"bytes": run_observation["stdoutBytes"], "dev": 1,
                "direct": True, "ino": 2, "mode": 0o600, "nlink": 0,
                "reviewerOwned": True, "sha256": run_observation["stdoutSha256"]}
        value = {"cidfile": {"candidate": candidate, "containerId": cid,
                "mode": 0o600, "name": name, "nlink": 1, "regular": True,
                "reviewerOwned": True, "role": role, "runId": run_id,
                "sha256": hashlib.sha256((cid + "\n").encode("ascii")).hexdigest(),
                "size": 65, "terminalLf": True},
            "heldOutput": held_output, "operations": operations, "role": role}
        if self.mutation == "missing-cid-metadata":
            value["cidfile"].pop("candidate")
        elif self.mutation == "wrong-cid-mode":
            value["cidfile"]["mode"] = 0o644
        elif self.mutation == "wrong-cid-shape":
            value["cidfile"]["containerId"] = "C" * 64
        elif self.mutation == "transaction-delete":
            value["operations"].pop(4)
        elif self.mutation == "transaction-reorder":
            value["operations"][3], value["operations"][4] = (
                value["operations"][4], value["operations"][3])
        elif self.mutation == "transaction-extra":
            value["operations"].append(dict(value["operations"][-1]))
        elif self.mutation == "indirect-held-output" and role == "build":
            value["heldOutput"]["direct"] = False
        elif self.mutation == "held-output-hash-mismatch" and role == "build":
            value["heldOutput"]["sha256"] = "0" * 64
        return value

    def selftest_docker_child_runner(self, role, argv, environment, timeout, observe,
            observe_b2):
        self.residue += 1
        if self.failure == "docker:" + role:
            raise TimeoutError(role)
        if self.failure == "lost-reply:" + role:
            raise EOFError(role)
        if environment != docker_private_environment(
                "/review/private/home", "/review/private/docker-config"):
            reject("selftest docker private environment")
        if self.mutation is not None and self.mutation.startswith("grammar-"):
            _prefix, family, mutation = self.mutation.split("-", 2)
            operation = "kill" if family == "kill" else family
            expected = docker_control_argv("/usr/bin/docker", operation, "c" * 64,
