```python
                "a" * 24, "build")
            value = list(expected)
            if mutation == "delete":
                value.pop()
            elif mutation == "substitute":
                value[-1] += "-mutant"
            elif mutation == "reorder":
                value[1], value[2] = value[2], value[1]
            elif mutation == "extra":
                value.append("--mutant")
            exact_vector(value, expected, "docker control mutant")
        started = 1000 if role == "ownership-probe" else 2000
        mechanism = "subprocess.run" if self.mutation == \
            "generic-subprocess-regression" else "subprocess.Popen"
        stdout = b"" if role == "ownership-probe" else b"\x7fELFselftest"
        observation = docker_child_observation(argv, environment, started, started + 1,
            True, "101" if role == "ownership-probe" else "202", 0, stdout, b"",
            self.mutation != "pid-reresolution", mechanism)
        if self.mutation == "runner-owner-edge":
            return None
        if self.mutation == "caller-supplied-summary":
            return observation
        changes = {"missing-pidfd": ("childPidfdLive", None),
            "non-live-pidfd": ("childPidfdLive", False),
            "wrong-start-token": ("childStartToken", "wrong"),
            "missing-terminal-status": ("terminalStatus", None),
            "missing-start-time": ("startedMonotonicNs", None),
            "missing-end-time": ("endedMonotonicNs", None),
            "missing-stdout-count": ("stdoutBytes", None),
            "missing-stderr-count": ("stderrBytes", None),
            "missing-stdout-hash": ("stdoutSha256", None),
            "missing-stderr-hash": ("stderrSha256", None)}
        if self.mutation in changes:
            key, value = changes[self.mutation]
            if value is None:
                observation.pop(key)
            else:
                observation[key] = value
        observe(_DOCKER_RUNNER_OBSERVATION_SEAL, observation)
        observe_b2(_DOCKER_B2_OBSERVATION_SEAL,
            self.selftest_docker_transaction(role, argv, environment, observation))

    def validate_and_seal_output(self):
        if self.failure == "seal-output":
            reject("selftest output seal")
        if self.mutation != "unsealed-held-output":
            self.output_sealed = True

    def finalize(self, timeout):
        if self.failure == "finalize":
            raise TimeoutError("finalize")
        if not self.output_sealed:
            reject("selftest unsealed output")

    def cleanup(self, error, observation_sinks):
        self.cleanup_calls += 1
        if self.failure == "cleanup-once":
            self.residue = 0
            return 1
        self.residue = 0
        if error is None:
            for sink in observation_sinks:
                for event in PARENT_FINAL_EVENTS:
                    self.emit(sink, event)
        return 0


class OwnerExecutorEdgeMutant(ParentCore):
    def invoke_unit(self, phase, bindings, systemd, bwrap, sink):
        return {"d7c2": b"", "diagnostic": b"", "exit": 0, "phase": phase}


class ExecutorAdapterEdgeMutant(SelftestParentExecutor):
    def unit(self, phase, bindings, systemd, bwrap, observation_sink):
        return {"d7c2": b"", "diagnostic": b"", "exit": 0, "phase": phase}


class OwnerDockerEdgeMutant(ParentCore):
    _delete_docker_owner_edge = True


def selftest_parent_inputs():
    base = sample_bindings(output=False)
    base.update({"authorityOracle": "/review/oracle.py", "candidateSha": "a" * 40,
        "fixedMode": "focused", "hostCandidate": "/review/candidate", "hostDeps": "/deps",
        "hostOdb": "/odb", "hostSource": "/source", "hostToolchain": "/toolchain",
        "python": "/usr/bin/python3", "systemdRun": "/usr/bin/systemd-run"})
    vectors = {"dockerProbe": docker_argv("/usr/bin/docker", "ownership-probe",
            "d007d-v4-" + "b" * 24 + "-probe", "/review/ownership-probe.cid",
            "b" * 24, "a" * 40),
        "dockerBuild": docker_argv("/usr/bin/docker", "build",
            "d007d-v4-" + "b" * 24 + "-build", "/review/build.cid",
            "b" * 24, "a" * 40)}
    return base, vectors


def execute_selftest_owner(entry, executor, core=ParentCore):
    base, vectors = selftest_parent_inputs()
    return core(entry, executor, {}, base, vectors, None, 0).execute()


def direct_owner_proof(entry):
    executor = SelftestParentExecutor(_SELFTEST_EXECUTOR_SEAL)
    result = execute_selftest_owner(entry, executor)
    if executor.cleanup_calls != 1 or executor.residue != 0 \
            or result["cleanupInvocations"] != 1:
        reject("direct owner cleanup")
    return result


def owner_mutant_result(name):
    if name not in OWNER_MUTANTS:
        reject("owner mutant")
    executor = SelftestParentExecutor(_SELFTEST_EXECUTOR_SEAL)
    rejected = False
    try:
        if name.startswith("entry-"):
            execute_selftest_owner("mutated-" + name[6:], executor)
        elif name == "owner-executor":
            execute_selftest_owner("direct", executor, OwnerExecutorEdgeMutant)
        elif name == "executor-adapter":
            executor = ExecutorAdapterEdgeMutant(_SELFTEST_EXECUTOR_SEAL)
            execute_selftest_owner("direct", executor)
        elif name.startswith("fabricated-"):
            executor = SelftestParentExecutor(_SELFTEST_EXECUTOR_SEAL, mutation=name)
            execute_selftest_owner("direct", executor)
        elif name in A2_OWNER_MUTANTS:
            executor = SelftestParentExecutor(_SELFTEST_EXECUTOR_SEAL, mutation=name)
            execute_selftest_owner("direct", executor)
        elif name == "owner-runner-edge":
            execute_selftest_owner("direct", executor, OwnerDockerEdgeMutant)
        elif name in B1_OWNER_MUTANTS:
            executor = SelftestParentExecutor(_SELFTEST_EXECUTOR_SEAL, mutation=name)
            execute_selftest_owner("direct", executor)
        elif name in B2_OWNER_MUTANTS:
            executor = SelftestParentExecutor(_SELFTEST_EXECUTOR_SEAL, mutation=name)
            execute_selftest_owner("direct", executor)
        elif name == "cleanup-retry":
            executor = SelftestParentExecutor(
                _SELFTEST_EXECUTOR_SEAL, failure="cleanup-once")
            execute_selftest_owner("direct", executor)
        elif name == "second-cleanup-owner":
            execute_selftest_owner("direct", executor)
            executor.cleanup(None, ())
            rejected = True
    except BaseException:
        rejected = True
    expected_calls = 2 if name == "second-cleanup-owner" else 1
    if not rejected or executor.cleanup_calls != expected_calls:
        reject("owner mutant accepted")
    return {"cleanupInvocations": executor.cleanup_calls, "decision": "REJECTED",
        "done": False, "mutant": name, "terminalState": "REJECTED"}


def parent_core_proof(entry="direct"):
    proof = direct_owner_proof(entry)

    def run(executor):
        return execute_selftest_owner(entry, executor)

    branches = DockerRecorder.branch_proof()
    rejected_events = 0
    for event in PARENT_TRANSITIONS:
        executor = SelftestParentExecutor(_SELFTEST_EXECUTOR_SEAL, bad_event=event)
        try:
            run(executor)
        except (RuntimeError, ValueError):
            rejected_events += 1
        if executor.cleanup_calls != 1 or executor.residue != 0:
            reject("selftest event cleanup")
    vector_labels = ("archive:systemd", "archive:bwrap", "docker:ownership-probe",
        "docker:build", "test:systemd", "test:bwrap")
    rejected_vectors = 0
    for label in vector_labels:
        executor = SelftestParentExecutor(_SELFTEST_EXECUTOR_SEAL, bad_vector=label)
        try:
            run(executor)
        except ValueError:
            rejected_vectors += 1
        if executor.cleanup_calls != 1 or executor.residue != 0:
            reject("selftest vector cleanup")
    failures = ("unit:archive", "frames:archive", "docker:ownership-probe",
        "lost-reply:ownership-probe", "docker:build", "lost-reply:build",
        "seal-output", "unit:test", "frames:test", "finalize")
    rejected_failures = 0
    for failure in failures:
        executor = SelftestParentExecutor(_SELFTEST_EXECUTOR_SEAL, failure=failure)
        try:
            run(executor)
        except (EOFError, TimeoutError, ValueError):
            rejected_failures += 1
        if executor.cleanup_calls != 1 or executor.residue != 0:
            reject("selftest failure cleanup")
    retry_executor = SelftestParentExecutor(_SELFTEST_EXECUTOR_SEAL, failure="cleanup-once")
    try:
        run(retry_executor)
    except RuntimeError:
        pass
    else:
        reject("selftest cleanup failure accepted")
    if retry_executor.cleanup_calls != 1 or retry_executor.residue != 0:
        reject("selftest cleanup invocation")
    preparation_executor = SelftestParentExecutor(_SELFTEST_EXECUTOR_SEAL)
    base, vectors = selftest_parent_inputs()
    preparation_owner = ParentCore(
        "run", preparation_executor, {}, base, vectors, None, 0)
    preparation_owner.abort_preparation(ValueError("preparation mutant"))
    if (preparation_executor.cleanup_calls != 1
            or preparation_owner.cleanup_invocations != 1
            or preparation_owner.terminal_state != "REJECTED"):
        reject("preparation cleanup owner")
    control_operations = ("inspect", "wait", "term", "kill", "remove", "scan")
    rejected_controls = 0
    control_grammar = {}
    for operation in control_operations:
        expected = docker_control_argv("/usr/bin/docker", operation, "c" * 64,
            "a" * 24, "build")
        control_grammar[operation] = expected
        mutant = list(expected)
        mutant[-1] += "-mutant"
        try:
            exact_vector(mutant, expected, "docker control")
        except ValueError:
            rejected_controls += 1
    proof.update({"branches": branches, "cleanupRetries": 0,
        "cleanupZero": 1 + len(PARENT_TRANSITIONS)
            + len(vector_labels) + len(failures),
        "eventsRejected": rejected_events,
        "controlGrammar": control_grammar,
        "controlVectorsRejected": rejected_controls, "failuresRejected": rejected_failures,
        "ownerMutants": [owner_mutant_result(name) for name in OWNER_MUTANTS],
        "phases": 2, "preparationFailuresRejected": 1,
        "vectorsRejected": rejected_vectors})
    return proof


def remove_runtime_tree(path, deadlines=None):
    if not re.fullmatch(r".*/d007d-v4-[0-9a-f]{24}", path):
        reject("runtime cleanup path")

    def descend(current):
        if deadlines is not None:
            deadlines.outer(PARENT_LIMITS["operation"])
        try:
            entries = list(os.scandir(current))
        except FileNotFoundError:
            return
        for entry in entries:
            if deadlines is not None:
                deadlines.outer(PARENT_LIMITS["operation"])
            child = os.path.join(current, entry.name)
            if entry.is_dir(follow_symlinks=False):
                descend(child)
                os.rmdir(child)
            else:
                os.unlink(child)

    try:
        metadata = os.lstat(path)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return 0
    if not stat.S_ISDIR(metadata.st_mode) or stat.S_ISLNK(metadata.st_mode):
        reject("runtime cleanup identity")
    descend(path)
    os.rmdir(path)
    for _attempt in range(2):
        if deadlines is not None:
            deadlines.outer(PARENT_LIMITS["operation"])
        try:
            os.lstat(path)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            pass
        else:
            return 1
        select.select([], [], [], 0.25)
    return 0


class RealParentExecutor(InjectedUnitExecutor):
    def __init__(self, bindings, expected, archive_fd, root_path, evidence_fd, docker, held,
            identity_fds, t0):
        self.bindings = bindings
        self.expected = expected
        self.archive_fd = archive_fd
        self.root_path = root_path
        self.evidence_fd = evidence_fd
        self.output_fd = None
        self.output_sealed = False
        self.cid_dir_fd = None
        self.docker_path = docker
        self.held = tuple((fd, os.fstat(fd)) for fd in held)
        exact_keys(identity_fds, ("python", "systemdRun"))
        self.identity_fds = dict(identity_fds)
        self.units = []
        self.containers = {}
        self.pending_b2_child_observations = []
        self.docker_home = os.path.join(root_path, "docker-home")
        self.docker_config = os.path.join(root_path, "docker-config")
        self.deadlines = DeadlineBudget(t0)
        self.started = t0
        self.bind_unit_adapter(self.real_unit_adapter)
        self.bind_docker_child_runner(self.real_docker_child_runner)

    def remaining(self, limit):
        return self.deadlines.remaining(limit)

    def prepare_runtime(self, archive_fd, evidence_fd, output_fd, cid_dir_fd):
        if any(value is None for value in (
                archive_fd, evidence_fd, output_fd, cid_dir_fd)):
            reject("runtime fd preparation")
        self.archive_fd = archive_fd
        self.evidence_fd = evidence_fd
        self.output_fd = output_fd
        self.cid_dir_fd = cid_dir_fd
        self.held += tuple((fd, os.fstat(fd))
            for fd in (archive_fd, evidence_fd, output_fd, cid_dir_fd))
        for path in (self.docker_home, self.docker_config):
            metadata = os.stat(path, follow_symlinks=False)
            if (not stat.S_ISDIR(metadata.st_mode)
                    or stat.S_IMODE(metadata.st_mode) != 0o700
                    or metadata.st_uid != os.geteuid()):
                reject("docker private directory")
        if os.listdir(self.docker_config):
            reject("docker config not empty")

    @staticmethod
    def fd_measurement(fd):
        digest = hashlib.sha256()
        offset = 0
        size = os.fstat(fd).st_size
        while offset < size:
            chunk = os.pread(fd, min(1048576, size - offset), offset)
            if not chunk:
                reject("held fd short read")
            digest.update(chunk)
            offset += len(chunk)
        return {"bytes": size, "sha256": digest.hexdigest()}

    def phase_bindings(self, phase, output_present):
        if (phase == "archive" and output_present) or (phase == "test"
                and (not output_present or not self.output_sealed)):
            reject("phase output lifecycle")
        nonce = os.getrandom(12).hex()
        return {"controlSocket": os.path.join(self.root_path, "run", phase + ".sock"),
            "nonce": nonce, "outputPresent": output_present,
            "unit": "d007d-v4-" + self.bindings["candidateSha"][:20] + "-" + nonce + ".service"}

    def vector(self, label, expected):
        return list(expected)

    @staticmethod
    def packet(control, timeout):
        control.settimeout(timeout)
        body = control.recv(65536)
        if not body:
            raise EOFError("unit reply")
        return json_payload(body)

    def service(self, argv, timeout, unavailable=False):
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(argv, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                stderr=subprocess.PIPE, env=FROZEN_ENV, timeout=self.remaining(timeout), check=False)
        except (FileNotFoundError, PermissionError) as error:
            if unavailable:
                raise InfrastructureUnavailable("infrastructure_unavailable: " + argv[0]) from error
            raise
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as error:
            raise TimeoutError("command deadline: " + argv[0]) from error
        if unavailable and result.returncode != 0:
            diagnostic = result.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace").lower()
            if any(value in diagnostic for value in ("failed to connect", "not been booted",
                    "no such file", "permission denied", "cannot connect")):
                raise InfrastructureUnavailable("infrastructure_unavailable: service boundary")
        return result

    def unit_properties(self, unit):
        result = self.service(["/usr/bin/systemctl", "--user", "show", unit,
            "--property", "MainPID", "--property", "ControlGroup"],
            PARENT_LIMITS["operation"], unavailable=True)
        if result.returncode != 0 or result.stderr:
            reject("unit property snapshot")
        properties = {}
        for line in result.stdout.splitlines():
            key, separator, value = line.partition(b"=")
            if not separator or key in properties:
                reject("unit property syntax")
            properties[key] = value
        if set(properties) != {b"MainPID", b"ControlGroup"} \
                or not properties[b"MainPID"].isdigit():
            reject("unit property keys")
        main_pid = int(properties[b"MainPID"])
        try:
            control_group = properties[b"ControlGroup"].decode("utf-8", "strict")
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            reject("control group encoding")
        if main_pid <= 0:
            reject("unit MainPID")
        return main_pid, control_group

    def real_authentication_snapshot(self, control, hello, process, bindings, systemd):
        peer_pid, peer_uid, peer_gid = struct.unpack("3i",
            control.getsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_PEERCRED, 12))
        reviewer_uid = os.geteuid()
        reviewer_gid = os.getegid()
        if hello != {"candidateSha": bindings["candidateSha"],
                "mode": bindings["fixedMode"], "nonce": bindings["nonce"],
                "pid": peer_pid, "type": "HELLO"}:
            reject("unit hello identity")
        main_pid, control_group = self.unit_properties(bindings["unit"])
        authority = {}
        try:
            expected_unit_argv = [bindings["python"], bindings["authorityOracle"],
                "unit-shim", bindings["controlSocket"], bindings["fixedMode"],
                bindings["candidateSha"], bindings["nonce"]]
            unit_pidfd, unit = process_identity_snapshot(peer_pid,
                self.identity_fds["python"], expected_unit_argv)
            authority["unitPidfd"] = unit_pidfd
            systemd_pidfd, systemd_child = process_identity_snapshot(process.pid,
                self.identity_fds["systemdRun"], list(systemd), os.getpid())
            authority["systemdRunChildPidfd"] = systemd_pidfd
            group_fd, procs_fd, membership = held_control_group(control_group, main_pid)
            authority["controlGroupFd"] = group_fd
            authority["cgroupProcsFd"] = procs_fd
            observations = {"mainPidExact": main_pid == peer_pid,
                "reviewerUidExact": type(reviewer_uid) is int and reviewer_uid >= 0,
                "reviewerGidExact": type(reviewer_gid) is int and reviewer_gid >= 0,
                "peerUidExact": peer_uid == reviewer_uid,
                "peerGidExact": peer_gid == reviewer_gid,
                "unitPidfdLive": unit["pidfdLive"],
                "unitExecutableHeld": unit["executableHeld"],
                "unitArgvExact": unit["argvExact"],
                "unitStartTokenExact": unit["startTokenExact"],
                "controlGroupHeldExact": True,
                "controlGroupMembershipExact": membership,
                "systemdRunChildPidfdLive":
                    systemd_child["pidfdLive"] and process.poll() is None,
                "systemdRunChildExecutableHeld": systemd_child["executableHeld"],
                "systemdRunChildArgvExact": systemd_child["argvExact"],
                "systemdRunChildParentExact": systemd_child["parentExact"],
                "snapshotOnce": True, "noAuthorityReresolution": True}
            return observations, authority
        except BaseException:
            for fd in authority.values():
                try:
                    os.close(fd)
                except OSError:
                    pass
            raise

    def real_unit_adapter(self, phase, bindings, systemd, bwrap, observation_sink):
        observation_sink.edge("injectedAdapter->ParentCore.observationSink")
        listener = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_SEQPACKET | socket.SOCK_CLOEXEC)
        read_fds = []
        close_fds = []
        channel_sockets = []
        authority = {}
        process = None
        try:
            listener.bind(bindings["controlSocket"])
            os.chmod(bindings["controlSocket"], 0o600)
            listener.listen(1)
            listener.settimeout(self.remaining(PARENT_LIMITS["operation"]))
            observation_sink.transition("AUTH_OK")
            try:
                process = subprocess.Popen(list(systemd), stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                    stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, env=FROZEN_ENV,
                    close_fds=True)
            except (FileNotFoundError, PermissionError) as error:
                raise InfrastructureUnavailable(
                    "infrastructure_unavailable: exact systemd-run launch") from error
            self.units.append(bindings["unit"])
            observation_sink.transition("SYSTEMD_CHILD")
            control, _address = listener.accept()
            with control:
                hello = self.packet(control, PARENT_LIMITS["operation"])
                d7c2_parent, d7c2_child = socket.socketpair(
                    socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM | socket.SOCK_CLOEXEC)
                diagnostic_parent, diagnostic_child = socket.socketpair(
                    socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM | socket.SOCK_CLOEXEC)
                channel_sockets = [d7c2_parent, d7c2_child,
                    diagnostic_parent, diagnostic_child]
                read_fds = [d7c2_parent.fileno(), diagnostic_parent.fileno()]
                channel_by_fd = {d7c2_parent.fileno(): "d7c2",
                    diagnostic_parent.fileno(): "diagnostic"}
                authentication, authority = self.real_authentication_snapshot(
                    control, hello, process, bindings, systemd)
                self.authenticate(observation_sink,
                    {"d7c2": (d7c2_parent, d7c2_child),
                        "diagnostic": (diagnostic_parent, diagnostic_child)},
                    authentication)
                observation_sink.transition("UNIT_IDENTITIES")
                seal_fd = os.memfd_create("d007d-fd-seal", os.MFD_CLOEXEC | os.MFD_ALLOW_SEALING)
                write_all(seal_fd, contract_bytes()["FD_SEAL_V3"])
                fcntl.fcntl(seal_fd, fcntl.F_ADD_SEALS, fcntl.F_SEAL_SEAL
                    | fcntl.F_SEAL_SHRINK | fcntl.F_SEAL_GROW | fcntl.F_SEAL_WRITE)
                close_fds.append(seal_fd)
                roles = ["d7c2", "diagnostic"]
                rights = [d7c2_child.fileno(), diagnostic_child.fileno()]
                if bindings["outputPresent"]:
                    roles.append("heldOutput")
                    rights.append(self.output_fd)
                roles.append("fdSeal")
                rights.append(seal_fd)
                start = canonical({"bindings": bindings, "bwrapArgv": list(bwrap),
                    "candidateSha": bindings["candidateSha"], "fdRoles": roles,
                    "mode": bindings["fixedMode"], "nonce": bindings["nonce"], "type": "START"})
                control.sendmsg([start], [(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SCM_RIGHTS,
                    struct.pack("%di" % len(rights), *rights))])
                observation_sink.transition("SCM_RIGHTS")
                d7c2_child.close()
                diagnostic_child.close()
                bwrap_reply = self.packet(control, PARENT_LIMITS["operation"])
                bwrap_pid = bwrap_reply.get("pid")
                if bwrap_reply != {"pid": bwrap_pid, "type": "BWRAP"} or type(bwrap_pid) is not int:
                    reject("bwrap identity")
                observation_sink.transition("BWRAP_IDENTITIES")
                if not pidfd_live(authority["unitPidfd"]) \
                        or not pidfd_live(authority["systemdRunChildPidfd"]):
                    reject("authenticated process no longer live")
                control.send(canonical({"nonce": bindings["nonce"], "pid": bwrap_pid,
                    "type": "RELEASE"}))
                if self.packet(control, PARENT_LIMITS["operation"]) != {
                        "pid": bwrap_pid, "type": "RELEASED"}:
                    reject("release reply")
                observation_sink.transition("RELEASE_ACK")
                for fd in read_fds:
                    os.set_blocking(fd, False)
                chunks = {read_fds[0]: [], read_fds[1]: []}
                eof = set()
                exit_reply = None
                while process.poll() is None or len(eof) != 2 or exit_reply is None:
                    if time.monotonic() - self.started >= PARENT_LIMITS["outer"]:
                        raise TimeoutError("unit outer")
                    ready, _write, _exception = select.select(
                        [fd for fd in read_fds if fd not in eof] + ([control] if exit_reply is None else []),
                        [], [], min(1.0, self.remaining(PARENT_LIMITS["outer"])))
                    for item in ready:
                        if item is control:
                            exit_reply = self.packet(control, PARENT_LIMITS["operation"])
                        else:
                            data = os.read(item, 65536)
                            if data:
                                chunks[item].append(data)
                            else:
                                eof.add(item)
                                observation_sink.eof(channel_by_fd[item])
                stderr = process.stderr.read() if process.stderr is not None else b""
                if process.returncode != 0 or exit_reply != {"exit": 0, "pid": bwrap_pid,
                        "type": "EXIT"} or stderr:
                    reject("shim wait")
                observation_sink.transition("SHIM_WAIT_ACCEPTED")
                observation_sink.transition("BOTH_EOF_ACCEPTED")
                self.cgroup_empty(authority["cgroupProcsFd"])
                self.cgroup_empty(authority["cgroupProcsFd"])
                observation_sink.transition("CGROUP_EMPTY_TWICE")
                self.stop_unit(bindings["unit"])
                observation_sink.transition("STOP_RESET")
                self.unit_absent(bindings["unit"])
                self.unit_absent(bindings["unit"])
                observation_sink.transition("UNIT_CGROUP_ABSENT_TWICE")
                self.units.remove(bindings["unit"])
                return {"d7c2": b"".join(chunks[read_fds[0]]),
                    "diagnostic": b"".join(chunks[read_fds[1]]), "exit": 0,
                    "phase": phase}
        finally:
            for channel in channel_sockets:
                channel.close()
            for fd in list(authority.values()) + close_fds:
                try:
                    os.close(fd)
                except OSError:
                    pass
            listener.close()
            try:
                os.unlink(bindings["controlSocket"])
            except FileNotFoundError:
                pass

    def cgroup_empty(self, cgroup_procs_fd):
        if held_cgroup_members(cgroup_procs_fd):
            reject("cgroup not empty")
        select.select([], [], [], 0.25)

    def stop_unit(self, unit):
        for operation in ("stop", "reset-failed"):
            result = self.service(["/usr/bin/systemctl", "--user", operation, unit],
                PARENT_LIMITS["operation"], unavailable=True)
            if result.returncode != 0:
                reject("unit " + operation)

    def unit_absent(self, unit):
        result = self.service(["/usr/bin/systemctl", "--user", "show", unit,
            "--property", "LoadState", "--value"], PARENT_LIMITS["operation"], unavailable=True)
        if result.returncode == 0 and result.stdout.strip() not in (b"", b"not-found"):
            reject("unit residue")
        select.select([], [], [], 0.25)

    def frames(self, phase, expected, result):
        stream = os.memfd_create("d007d-stream", os.MFD_CLOEXEC)
        try:
            write_all(stream, result["d7c2"])
            os.lseek(stream, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
            validate_frames(stream, phase, expected, self.archive_fd if phase == "archive" else None)
            if phase == "archive":
                seal_archive_result(self.archive_fd, expected)
        finally:
            os.close(stream)

    def docker_environment(self, role):
        return docker_private_environment(self.docker_home, self.docker_config)

    def run_docker_client(self, argv, environment, timeout, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE):
        started = time.monotonic_ns()
        try:
            child = subprocess.Popen(list(argv), stdin=stdin,
                stdout=stdout, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, env=dict(environment),
                close_fds=True)
        except (FileNotFoundError, PermissionError) as error:
            raise InfrastructureUnavailable(
                "infrastructure_unavailable: " + argv[0]) from error
        pidfd = exact_pidfd(child.pid)
        start_token = process_start_token(child.pid)
        live = pidfd_live(pidfd)
        timed_out = False
        try:
            try:
                stdout_bytes, stderr = child.communicate(timeout=self.remaining(timeout))
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                timed_out = True
                libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)
                if libc.syscall(SYS_pidfd_send_signal, pidfd, signal.SIGKILL, 0, 0) != 0 \
                        and ctypes.get_errno() != errno.ESRCH:
                    raise OSError(ctypes.get_errno(), "docker child pidfd kill")
                stdout_bytes, stderr = child.communicate()
            ended = time.monotonic_ns()
            if type(stdout) is int and stdout == self.output_fd:
                stdout_bytes = b""
                stdout_measurement = self.fd_measurement(self.output_fd)
            else:
                stdout_measurement = None
                if stdout_bytes is None:
                    stdout_bytes = b""
            observation = docker_child_observation(argv, environment, started, ended, live,
                start_token, child.returncode, stdout_bytes, stderr,
                stdout_measurement=stdout_measurement)
            self._last_docker_result = subprocess.CompletedProcess(
                list(argv), child.returncode, stdout_bytes, stderr)
        finally:
            os.close(pidfd)
        if timed_out:
            raise TimeoutError("docker child deadline")
        return self._last_docker_result, observation

    def docker_command(self, argv, allow_failure=False):
        result, observation = self.run_docker_client(argv,
            self.docker_environment("control"), PARENT_LIMITS["operation"])
        if result.returncode != 0 and not allow_failure:
            reject("docker control")
        return result, observation

    def read_cidfile(self, role, item):
        basename = role + ".cid"
        if os.path.basename(item["cidfile"]) != basename or self.cid_dir_fd is None:
            reject("cidfile path")
        poll_deadline = min(time.monotonic() + 5, self.deadlines.active_deadline,
            self.started + PARENT_LIMITS["outer"])
        while True:
            try:
                fd = os.open(basename,
                    os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC,
                    dir_fd=self.cid_dir_fd)
                break
            except FileNotFoundError:
                if time.monotonic() >= poll_deadline:
                    return None, None
                select.select([], [], [], min(0.05, poll_deadline - time.monotonic()))
        metadata = os.fstat(fd)
        payload = os.pread(fd, 66, 0)
        if (not stat.S_ISREG(metadata.st_mode) or stat.S_IMODE(metadata.st_mode) != 0o600
                or metadata.st_uid != os.geteuid() or metadata.st_nlink != 1
                or metadata.st_size != 65 or len(payload) != 65 or payload[-1:] != b"\n"):
            os.close(fd)
            reject("cidfile held metadata")
        try:
            cid = payload[:-1].decode("ascii", "strict")
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            os.close(fd)
            reject("cidfile encoding")
        if not HEX64.fullmatch(cid):
            os.close(fd)
            reject("cidfile shape")
        item["cidFd"] = fd
        item["cid"] = cid
        return cid, {"candidate": item["candidate"], "containerId": cid,
            "mode": stat.S_IMODE(metadata.st_mode), "name": item["name"],
            "nlink": metadata.st_nlink, "regular": True, "reviewerOwned": True,
            "role": role, "runId": item["runId"],
            "sha256": hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest(), "size": metadata.st_size,
            "terminalLf": True}

    def inspect_container(self, payload, item, expected_status=None):
        try:
            value = json.loads(payload.decode("utf-8", "strict"),
                object_pairs_hook=object_no_duplicates,
                parse_constant=lambda _value: reject("nonfinite inspect JSON"))
        except (UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
            reject("docker inspect JSON")
        if type(value) is not list or len(value) != 1 or type(value[0]) is not dict:
            reject("docker inspect cardinality")
        container = value[0]
        labels = container.get("Config", {}).get("Labels", {})
        state = container.get("State", {})
        host = container.get("HostConfig", {})
        expected_labels = {
            "org.agents-orchestrator.d007d.run": item["runId"],
            "org.agents-orchestrator.d007d.role": item["role"],
            "org.agents-orchestrator.d007d.candidate": item["candidate"]}
        expected_user = "65534:65534" if item["role"] == "ownership-probe" else "1000:1000"
        if (container.get("Id") != item["cid"]
                or container.get("Name") != "/" + item["name"]
                or any(labels.get(key) != expected for key, expected in expected_labels.items())
                or state.get("Status") not in ("running", "exited", "dead")
                or (expected_status is not None and state.get("Status") != expected_status)
                or type(state.get("ExitCode")) is not int
                or container.get("Config", {}).get("User") != expected_user
                or host.get("Memory") != 2147483648
                or host.get("MemorySwap") != 2147483648
                or host.get("PidsLimit") != 128
                or host.get("NanoCpus") != 1000000000
                or host.get("ReadonlyRootfs") is not True
                or host.get("NetworkMode") != "none"):
            reject("docker inspect identity/config")
        return {"containerId": item["cid"], "exitCode": state["ExitCode"],
            "name": item["name"], "status": state["Status"]}

    @staticmethod
    def operation_record(sequence, token, argv, observation, result):
        return docker_operation(sequence, token, argv, observation, result)

    def real_docker_child_runner(self, role, argv, environment, timeout, observe, observe_b2):
        if role not in DOCKER_ROLES or environment != self.docker_environment(role):
            reject("real docker private environment")
        self.deadlines.enter(role)
        name, run_id, candidate = docker_run_identity(argv, role)
        cid_index = argv.index("--cidfile") + 1
        item = {"candidate": candidate, "cidfile": argv[cid_index], "name": name,
            "role": role, "runId": run_id}
        self.containers[role] = item
        operations = []
        inspect_name_argv = docker_control_argv(self.docker_path, "inspect", name)
        result, observation = self.docker_command(inspect_name_argv, allow_failure=True)
        if result.returncode == 0:
            reject("docker role name preexists")
        operations.append(self.operation_record(
            1, "I_name", inspect_name_argv, observation, "not-found"))
        scan_argv = docker_control_argv(
            self.docker_path, "scan", "pending", run_id, role)
        result, observation = self.docker_command(scan_argv)
        if result.stdout or result.stderr:
            reject("docker preflight residue")
        operations.append(self.operation_record(2, "P1", scan_argv, observation, "empty"))
        if role == "build":
            os.ftruncate(self.output_fd, 0)
            os.lseek(self.output_fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
            os.lseek(self.archive_fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
            stdin, stdout = self.archive_fd, self.output_fd
        else:
            stdin, stdout = subprocess.DEVNULL, subprocess.PIPE
        result, run_observation = self.run_docker_client(
            argv, environment, timeout, stdin=stdin, stdout=stdout)
        observe(_DOCKER_RUNNER_OBSERVATION_SEAL, run_observation)
        if result.returncode != 0 or result.stderr or (role == "ownership-probe" and result.stdout):
            reject("docker role run")
        operations.append(self.operation_record(
            3, "RUN", argv, run_observation, "client-exit-0"))
        cid, cidfile = self.read_cidfile(role, item)
        if cid is None:
            reject("normal transaction missing cidfile")
        inspect_id_argv = docker_control_argv(self.docker_path, "inspect", cid)
        result, observation = self.docker_command(inspect_id_argv)
        first_projection = self.inspect_container(result.stdout, item)
        operations.append(self.operation_record(
            4, "I_id", inspect_id_argv, observation, first_projection["status"]))
        wait_argv = docker_control_argv(self.docker_path, "wait", cid)
        result, observation = self.docker_command(wait_argv)
        if result.stdout != b"0\n" or result.stderr:
            reject("docker wait result")
        operations.append(self.operation_record(5, "WAIT", wait_argv, observation, "exit-0"))
        result, observation = self.docker_command(inspect_id_argv)
        final_projection = self.inspect_container(result.stdout, item, "exited")
        if final_projection["exitCode"] != 0:
            reject("docker container exit")
        operations.append(self.operation_record(
            6, "I_id", inspect_id_argv, observation, "exited-0"))
        remove_argv = docker_control_argv(self.docker_path, "remove", cid)
        result, observation = self.docker_command(remove_argv)
        operations.append(self.operation_record(7, "RM_FORCE", remove_argv, observation, "removed"))
        result, observation = self.docker_command(inspect_name_argv, allow_failure=True)
        if result.returncode == 0:
            reject("docker name remained")
        operations.append(self.operation_record(
            8, "I_name", inspect_name_argv, observation, "not-found"))
        for sequence, token in ((9, "P1"), (10, "P2")):
            if sequence == 10:
                before = time.monotonic()
                select.select([], [], [], min(0.25, self.remaining(0.25)))
                if time.monotonic() - before < 0.25:
                    reject("docker scan separation")
            result, observation = self.docker_command(scan_argv)
            if result.stdout or result.stderr:
                reject("docker role residue")
            operations.append(self.operation_record(
                sequence, token, scan_argv, observation, "empty"))
        held_output = None
        if role == "build":
            metadata = os.fstat(self.output_fd)
            measurement = self.fd_measurement(self.output_fd)
            held_output = {"bytes": measurement["bytes"], "dev": metadata.st_dev,
                "direct": True, "ino": metadata.st_ino,
                "mode": stat.S_IMODE(metadata.st_mode), "nlink": metadata.st_nlink,
                "reviewerOwned": metadata.st_uid == os.geteuid(),
                "sha256": measurement["sha256"]}
        transaction = {"cidfile": cidfile, "heldOutput": held_output,
            "operations": operations, "role": role}
        observe_b2(_DOCKER_B2_OBSERVATION_SEAL, transaction)
        os.close(item["cidFd"])
        self.containers.pop(role)
        return None

    def reconcile(self, role):
        item = self.containers[role]
        tokens = []
        cid, _metadata = self.read_cidfile(role, item)
        if not cid:
            inspect_argv = docker_control_argv(self.docker_path, "inspect", item["name"])
            inspect, _observation = self.docker_command(inspect_argv, allow_failure=True)
            if inspect.returncode != 0:
                tokens.append("I_name")
                branch = "MISSING"
            else:
                try:
                    value = json.loads(inspect.stdout.decode("utf-8", "strict"),
                        object_pairs_hook=object_no_duplicates)
                    adopted = value[0]
                    cid = adopted["Id"]
                except (IndexError, KeyError, TypeError, UnicodeDecodeError,
                        json.JSONDecodeError):
                    reject("docker name adoption")
                if not HEX64.fullmatch(cid):
                    reject("docker name adoption ID")
                item["cid"] = cid
                self.inspect_container(inspect.stdout, item)
        if cid:
            inspect_id_argv = docker_control_argv(self.docker_path, "inspect", cid)
            inspected, _observation = self.docker_command(inspect_id_argv)
            tokens.append("I_id")
            projection = self.inspect_container(inspected.stdout, item)
            if projection["status"] != "running":
                branch = "ALREADY_EXITED"
                self.docker_command(docker_control_argv(self.docker_path, "wait", cid))
                tokens.append("WAIT")
            else:
                self.docker_command(docker_control_argv(self.docker_path, "term", cid))
                tokens.append("TERM")
                try:
                    self.docker_command(docker_control_argv(self.docker_path, "wait", cid))
                    tokens.append("WAIT")
                    branch = "TERM_EXITED"
                except (subprocess.TimeoutExpired, TimeoutError):
                    tokens.append("WAIT_TIMEOUT")
                    self.docker_command(docker_control_argv(self.docker_path, "kill", cid))
                    tokens.append("KILL")
                    self.docker_command(docker_control_argv(self.docker_path, "wait", cid))
                    tokens.append("WAIT")
                    branch = "KILL_EXITED"
                inspected, _observation = self.docker_command(inspect_id_argv)
                tokens.append("I_id")
                self.inspect_container(inspected.stdout, item)
            self.docker_command(docker_control_argv(self.docker_path, "remove", cid))
            tokens.append("RM_FORCE")
            self.docker_command(docker_control_argv(self.docker_path,
                "inspect", item["name"]), allow_failure=True)
            tokens.append("I_name")
        scan_argv = docker_control_argv(self.docker_path, "scan", "pending",
            item["runId"], role)
        for token in ("P1", "P2"):
            if token == "P2":
                before = time.monotonic()
                select.select([], [], [], min(0.25, self.remaining(0.25)))
                if time.monotonic() - before < 0.25:
                    reject("reconciliation scan separation")
            residue, _observation = self.docker_command(scan_argv)
            if residue.stdout or residue.stderr:
                reject("docker residue")
            tokens.append(token)
        derived = DockerRecorder().reconcile(tokens)
        if derived != branch:
            reject("docker reconciliation branch")
        if item.get("cidFd") is not None:
            os.close(item["cidFd"])
        self.containers.pop(role, None)
        return {"branch": branch, "count": len(tokens), "tokens": tokens}

    def validate_output(self):
        output = self.fd_measurement(self.output_fd)
        header = os.pread(self.output_fd, 20, 0)
        if (output["bytes"] < 20 or header[:4] != b"\x7fELF"
                or header[4:6] != b"\x02\x01" or header[18:20] != b"\x3e\x00"):
            reject("held output executable")
        return output

    def validate_and_seal_output(self):
        if self.output_fd is None or self.output_sealed \
                or fcntl.fcntl(self.output_fd, fcntl.F_GET_SEALS) != 0:
            reject("held output seal state")
        self.deadlines.remaining()
        os.fdatasync(self.output_fd)
        before = self.validate_output()
        fcntl.fcntl(self.output_fd, fcntl.F_ADD_SEALS, HELD_OUTPUT_SEALS)
        if fcntl.fcntl(self.output_fd, fcntl.F_GET_SEALS) != HELD_OUTPUT_SEALS \
                or self.validate_output() != before:
            reject("held output seals")
        self.output_sealed = True

    def finalize(self, timeout):
        if timeout != PARENT_LIMITS["finalization"]:
            reject("finalization timeout")
        self.deadlines.enter("finalization")
        for fd in (self.archive_fd, self.output_fd, self.evidence_fd):
            self.deadlines.remaining()
            os.fdatasync(fd)
            self.deadlines.remaining()
        if not self.output_sealed \
                or fcntl.fcntl(self.output_fd, fcntl.F_GET_SEALS) != HELD_OUTPUT_SEALS:
            reject("held output final seals")
        self.validate_output()
        for fd, before in self.held:
            self.deadlines.remaining()
            after = os.fstat(fd)
            if (after.st_dev, after.st_ino, after.st_mode, after.st_uid) != (
                    before.st_dev, before.st_ino, before.st_mode, before.st_uid):
                reject("held object changed")

    def cleanup(self, error, observation_sinks):
        failures = 0
        if self.containers:
            try:
                self.deadlines.enter("reconciliation")
            except BaseException:
                failures += 1
        for role in tuple(self.containers):
            try:
                self.reconcile(role)
            except BaseException:
                failures += 1
        for unit in tuple(self.units):
            try:
                self.stop_unit(unit)
            except BaseException:
                failures += 1
        try:
            failures += remove_runtime_tree(self.root_path, self.deadlines)
        except BaseException:
            failures += 1
        if failures == 0 and error is None:
            for sink in observation_sinks:
                for event in PARENT_FINAL_EVENTS:
                    sink.transition(event)
        return failures


def run_parent(mode, odb, candidate, tree, source, toolchain, deps, docker, ledger, review_base):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    if mode not in MODES or not HEX40.fullmatch(candidate) or not HEX40.fullmatch(tree):
        reject("run identity")
    if dict(os.environ) != FROZEN_ENV or inherited_fds():
        reject("run environment or inherited fd")
    held = []
    opened = []
    root_path = None
    executor = None
    owner = None
    old_umask = None

    def operation_timeout():
        remaining = min(PARENT_LIMITS["operation"],
            t0 + PARENT_LIMITS["outer"] - time.monotonic())
        if remaining <= 0:
            raise TimeoutError("parent deadline")
        return remaining

    try:
        old_umask = os.umask(0o077)
        env_path = os.path.realpath("/usr/bin/env")
        python_path = os.path.realpath(sys.executable)
        systemd_run_path = os.path.realpath("/usr/bin/systemd-run")
        held = [held_path(odb, directory=True), held_path(source),
            held_path(toolchain, directory=True), held_path(deps, directory=True),
            held_path(docker, executable=True), held_path(ledger),
            held_path(review_base, directory=True), held_path(os.path.abspath(__file__)),
            held_path("/usr/bin/git", executable=True), held_path("/usr/bin/tar", executable=True),
            held_path(env_path, executable=True), held_path(python_path, executable=True),
            held_path(systemd_run_path, executable=True),
            held_path("/usr/bin/systemctl", executable=True),
            held_path("/usr/bin/bwrap", executable=True)]
        opened.extend(held)
        odb_fd, source_fd, toolchain_fd, deps_fd, docker_fd, ledger_fd, base_fd = held[:7]
        ledger_metadata = os.fstat(ledger_fd)
        base_metadata = os.fstat(base_fd)
        if (stat.S_IMODE(ledger_metadata.st_mode) != 0o400
                or ledger_metadata.st_nlink != 1 or not 0 < ledger_metadata.st_size <= 65536
                or stat.S_IMODE(base_metadata.st_mode) != 0o700
                or os.listdir(base_fd)):
            reject("review input modes")
        ledger_payload = os.pread(ledger_fd, ledger_metadata.st_size, 0)
        if b"\n" in ledger_payload:
            reject("expectation ledger LF")
        git = subprocess.run(["/usr/bin/git", "--git-dir=/proc/self/fd/" + str(odb_fd),
                "rev-parse", candidate + "^{tree}"], pass_fds=(odb_fd,), stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, env=FROZEN_ENV,
            timeout=operation_timeout(), check=False)
        if git.returncode != 0 or git.stdout != (tree + "\n").encode() or git.stderr:
            reject("candidate tree")
        expectation = json_payload(ledger_payload)
        validate_expected(expectation)
        if expectation["candidateSha"] != candidate or expectation["mode"] != mode:
            reject("expectation identity")
        nonce = os.getrandom(12).hex()
        root_name = "d007d-v4-" + nonce
        root_path = os.path.join(review_base, root_name)
        executor = RealParentExecutor({"candidateSha": candidate, "nonce": nonce},
            expectation, None, root_path, None, docker, held,
            {"python": held[11], "systemdRun": held[12]}, t0)
        owner = ParentCore("run", executor, expectation, {}, {}, None, 0)
        os.mkdir(root_name, 0o700, dir_fd=base_fd)
        root_fd = os.open(root_name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC,
            dir_fd=base_fd)
        opened.append(root_fd)
        for name in ("a", "evidence", "run", "cid", "docker-home", "docker-config",
                "candidate"):
            os.mkdir(name, 0o700, dir_fd=root_fd)
        cid_dir = os.open("cid",
            os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC, dir_fd=root_fd)
        opened.append(cid_dir)
        evidence_dir = os.open("evidence",
            os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC, dir_fd=root_fd)
        opened.append(evidence_dir)
        evidence = os.open("custody.jsonl", os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL
            | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC, 0o600, dir_fd=evidence_dir)
        opened.append(evidence)
        sequence = append_evidence(evidence, 0, "AUTH", {"candidate": candidate, "tree": tree,
            "held": [list(os.fstat(fd))[:2] for fd in held]})
        archive = os.memfd_create("d007d-reviewer-archive",
            os.MFD_CLOEXEC | os.MFD_ALLOW_SEALING)
        os.fchmod(archive, 0o600)
        opened.append(archive)
        output = os.memfd_create("d007d-reviewer-output",
            os.MFD_CLOEXEC | os.MFD_ALLOW_SEALING)
        os.fchmod(output, 0o600)
        opened.append(output)
        executor.prepare_runtime(archive, evidence, output, cid_dir)
        candidate_path = os.path.join(root_path, "candidate")
        git_archive = subprocess.Popen(["/usr/bin/git", "--git-dir=/proc/self/fd/" + str(odb_fd),
                "archive", "--format=tar", candidate], pass_fds=(odb_fd,), stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, env=FROZEN_ENV, close_fds=True)
        extracted = subprocess.run(["/usr/bin/tar", "--extract", "--file", "-", "--directory",
                candidate_path, "--no-same-owner", "--no-same-permissions"],
            stdin=git_archive.stdout, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
            env=FROZEN_ENV, timeout=operation_timeout(), check=False)
        if git_archive.stdout is not None:
            git_archive.stdout.close()
        git_stderr = git_archive.stderr.read() if git_archive.stderr is not None else b""
        git_return = git_archive.wait(timeout=operation_timeout())
        if git_return != 0 or git_stderr or extracted.returncode != 0 or extracted.stdout \
                or extracted.stderr:
            reject("candidate materialization")
        bindings = sample_bindings(output=False)
        bindings.update({"authorityOracle": os.path.abspath(__file__), "bwrap": "/usr/bin/bwrap",
            "candidateSha": candidate, "controlSocket": os.path.join(root_path, "run", "pending.sock"),
            "env": env_path, "fixedMode": mode, "hostCandidate": candidate_path, "hostDeps": deps,
            "hostOdb": odb, "hostSource": source, "hostToolchain": toolchain, "nonce": nonce,
            "python": python_path, "systemdRun": systemd_run_path,
            "unit": "d007d-v4-" + candidate[:20] + "-" + nonce + ".service"})
        vectors = {"systemd": systemd_argv(bindings), "bwrap": bwrap_argv(bindings),
            "dockerProbe": docker_argv(docker, "ownership-probe", "d007d-v4-" + nonce + "-probe",
                os.path.join(root_path, "cid", "ownership-probe.cid"), nonce, candidate),
            "dockerBuild": docker_argv(docker, "build", "d007d-v4-" + nonce + "-build",
                os.path.join(root_path, "cid", "build.cid"), nonce, candidate)}
        sequence = append_evidence(evidence, sequence, "ARGV", vectors)
        os.lseek(evidence, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
        replay = [json.loads(line) for line in
            os.read(evidence, os.fstat(evidence).st_size).splitlines()]
        if [item["seq"] for item in replay] != list(range(sequence)):
            reject("run evidence replay")
        executor.bindings = bindings
        owner.bindings = bindings
        owner.vectors = vectors
        owner.evidence_fd = evidence
        owner.sequence = sequence
        result = owner.execute()
        os.lseek(evidence, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
        evidence_bytes = os.read(evidence, os.fstat(evidence).st_size)
        result["evidence"] = {"bytes": len(evidence_bytes),
            "sha256": hashlib.sha256(evidence_bytes).hexdigest()}
        print(canonical({"candidateSha": candidate, "parent": result,
            "schemaVersion": 4, "treeSha": tree}).decode("ascii"))
    except BaseException as caught:
        if owner is not None and owner.cleanup_invocations == 0:
            owner.abort_preparation(caught)
        raise
    finally:
        for fd in reversed(opened):
            try:
                os.close(fd)
            except OSError:
                pass
        if old_umask is not None:
            os.umask(old_umask)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) == 2 and sys.argv[1] == "selftest":
        v4_selftest()
    elif len(sys.argv) == 2 and sys.argv[1] == "owner-proof":
        print(canonical(parent_core_proof("direct")).decode("ascii"))
    elif len(sys.argv) == 3 and sys.argv[1] == "owner-mutant":
        print(canonical(owner_mutant_result(sys.argv[2])).decode("ascii"))
    elif len(sys.argv) == 2 and sys.argv[1] == "contracts":
        contracts()
    elif len(sys.argv) == 2 and sys.argv[1] == "delayed-writer-selftest":
        print(canonical(delayed_writer_proof()).decode("ascii"))
    elif len(sys.argv) >= 2 and sys.argv[1] == "unit-shim" and len(sys.argv) == 6:
        raise SystemExit(unit_shim(*sys.argv[2:]))
    elif len(sys.argv) == 5 and sys.argv[1] == "frames":
        try:
            frames_public(*sys.argv[2:])
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            raise SystemExit(64)
    elif len(sys.argv) == 5 and sys.argv[1] == "attacks":
        attacks(*sys.argv[2:])
    elif len(sys.argv) == 7 and sys.argv[1] == "attack":
        print(canonical(attack_result(sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3])).decode("ascii"))
    elif len(sys.argv) == 12 and sys.argv[1] == "run":
        run_parent(*sys.argv[2:])
    else:
        raise SystemExit(64)
```

<!-- D007D_CUSTODY_ORACLE_V4_END -->

Mechanical extraction of the 181,652 LF-terminated Python-fence bytes has SHA-256
`3ea37af1b7ed6032a52125e6f1710a04fc2d827ef2e1da5c4147b20091662301`; it must compile. Before approval the reviewer runs exact argv
`[PYTHON, AUTHORITY_ORACLE, "selftest"]`. Its one canonical stdout line is 58,951 bytes including
LF, has SHA-256 `ddcfd068467fad9e2b5bd49f902476c2b980c4429cafb52b7720fe8337e3ac79`,
and records exactly two `ACCEPTED` canonical streams plus 82 named `REJECTED` single mutants in
the artifact-defined order; any changed name/count/decision/digest is failure. The reviewer then
runs `[PYTHON,AUTHORITY_ORACLE,"owner-proof"]` and
`[PYTHON,AUTHORITY_ORACLE,"contracts"]`; their one-line LF-terminated transcripts are respectively
53,063 bytes / SHA-256
`ca4d8db9be029e9efcc89dc0621a615a53e2096fe46642a596d03e5c160007b4` and 43,359 bytes /
SHA-256 `68284666b7d9a0ce798454479bd02a1fbea3baf0c10b2b3823390904bf592b22`.
The reviewer then
runs the nine exact parent attacks. The historical V1
fixture is exactly `tests 9/pass 0/fail 9/skipped 0/cancelled 0`; V4 is exactly
`9/9/0/0/0`. The driver emits one canonical result per attack, requires historical
`VULNERABLE/done=true/exit=0/residue=0` and V4
`REJECTED/done=false/exit=0/residue=0`, and hashes its complete
transcript. A candidate test may mirror these cases but cannot change or satisfy this oracle.
The driver argv is exactly
`[PYTHON,AUTHORITY_PARENT,"attacks",CANDIDATE_SHA,EXPECTATION_LEDGER,REVIEW_BASE]`.
For every closed attack/domain pair it rehashes its own held authority fd and expands the child
only as `[PYTHON,"/proc/self/fd/<AUTHORITY_FD>","attack",DOMAIN,ATTACK_ID,CANDIDATE_SHA,
EXPECTATION_LEDGER,REVIEW_BASE]`, with that fd in the explicit pass list. No executable,
candidate path, or free-form option is accepted.
Each authority attack uses a parent test seam with a 20-second run deadline, 2-second TERM
window, and 2-second KILL/empty-scan window; the driver's independent 20-second subprocess cap
is a final harness bound. Production/focused/full-CI modes retain the 900-second deadline.

The parent `attack` mode has exactly the following reviewer-owned fault seams and no arbitrary
command option. Each fixture is emitted from the approved oracle/spec bytes into reviewer tmpfs,
not read from the candidate tree:

| Attack id | Exact injected action | historical / V4 decision |
|---|---|---|
| `chmod_restore` | chmod/write/restore every declared read-only mount target | `VULNERABLE` / every write `EROFS`, `REJECTED` |
| `evidence_forgery` | enumerate fds/argv/env/mounts, truncate every writable fd, emit forged done frame | `VULNERABLE` / no evidence fd/path and invalid frame, `REJECTED` |
| `output_replacement` | parent seam replaces output pathname after bind but before test read | replacement consumed / fd-4 inode consumed, `REJECTED` |
| `retained_fd_child` | child retains D7C2 and diagnostic writers after wrapper exit | premature acceptance / EOF deadline, cgroup kill, `REJECTED` |
| `term_resistant` | descendant ignores TERM and loops beyond soft deadline | survivor / KILL plus two empty scans, `REJECTED` |
| `restore_before_final` | mutate and restore candidate/prerequisite before post-run check | pathname check passes / mount write fails and held hashes stay exact, `REJECTED` |
| `ustar_mutation` | run each of the exact 21 independent archive mutants | at least one reaches Docker / 21 rejected before Docker count changes, `REJECTED` |
| `mount_discovery_write` | enumerate all namespace surfaces and write every prerequisite plus evidence/output guesses | discovery/write succeeds / declared map only and all writes fail, `REJECTED` |
| `docker_escape` | locate client/socket then request `ps`, arbitrary `run`, and option injection | recorder reached / no client/socket/helper and zero daemon call, `REJECTED` |

For D7C2 itself the independent oracle accepts exactly one canonical archive sequence and one
canonical test sequence synthesized from the reviewer expectation ledger. It separately mutates
each header byte class, version/type/reserved/length, UTF-8, duplicate/extra/missing key,
boolean-as-integer, every scalar/array/name/count bound, each identity/hash/arithmetic relation,
type multiplicity/order, chunk sequence, archive-in-test, JSON-in-archive, truncation, retained
writer, EOF, bailout, duplicate root id, plan mismatch, nonterminal summary, trailing TAP/bytes,
and trailing frame; every single mutant must reject with no durable done record.
The review result records accepted count `2`, mutant count `82`, rejected count `82`, and an
empty accepted-mutant list. A parser crash, timeout, or unexecuted mutant
does not count as rejection.

### Trial 5 Docker identity, limits, and reconciliation

The parent generates one 24-lowerhex `RUN_ID` and owns two distinct roles in exact order:
`ownership-probe`, then `build`. Names are
`d007d-v4-<RUN_ID>-probe` and `d007d-v4-<RUN_ID>-build`; labels are exactly
`org.agents-orchestrator.d007d.run=<RUN_ID>`,
`org.agents-orchestrator.d007d.role=<role>`, and
`org.agents-orchestrator.d007d.candidate=<candidateSha>`. Each role has a distinct nonexistent
`<reviewer-private-cid-dir>/<role>.cid`, and only the reviewer-held mode-`0700` cid directory is
passed to Docker. Candidate input cannot influence names, labels, cid paths, flags, or daemon
operations.

Before creating the cid directory the parent sets `umask(0o077)`. Every Docker client uses
exactly `env -i LC_ALL=C LANG=C TZ=UTC HOME=<parent-private-home>
DOCKER_CONFIG=<parent-private-empty-mode-0700>
DOCKER_HOST=unix:///var/run/docker.sock`; both directories and all cid paths are outside every
candidate mount. Another endpoint, nonempty config directory, credential helper, proxy variable,
or inherited environment entry is `infrastructure_unavailable`, not a fallback.

#### Trial 7 B1 Docker child-runner foundation

The executable custody-oracle design has one injected production boundary for Docker children:
`ParentCore.execute -> InjectedUnitExecutor.docker -> injectedDockerChildRunner ->
ParentCore.dockerObservationSink`. The owner creates a sealed observation sink for each exact
accepted argv; the runner callback is the only authority that may populate it, must populate it
exactly once, and returns no caller-supplied summary. `RealParentExecutor` binds this edge to a
bounded `subprocess.Popen` runner. Docker execution through the generic captured
`subprocess.run` service is forbidden. The existing A1/A2 unit adapter, authentication, release,
EOF, and one-cleanup ownership remain separate and byte-preserved.

Every child receives a newly constructed seven-key dictionary and no ambient merge:
`DOCKER_CONFIG`, `DOCKER_HOST`, `HOME`, `LANG`, `LC_ALL`, `PATH`, and `TZ`. `HOME` is the
reviewer-private directory, `DOCKER_CONFIG` is a distinct empty reviewer-owned mode-`0700`
directory, and `DOCKER_HOST` is exactly `unix:///var/run/docker.sock`; the other four values are
the frozen locale/path values. The runner records the accepted argv and environment, start/end
monotonic nanoseconds, the still-live exact pidfd opened directly from the spawned child, the
single pre-reap `/proc/<pid>/stat` start token, terminal return-code/signal projection,
stdout/stderr counts and SHA-256 values, and normalized client result. Timeout signaling uses
only the held pidfd. The PID is never reopened or re-resolved as authority.

`docker_control_argv` emits only `inspect --type container`, `container wait`, `container kill`,
`container rm`, and `ps -aq` with both exact run and role label filters. Exact-vector acceptance
rejects deletion, substitution, reordering, extra tokens, top-level wait/kill/rm aliases,
formatted inspect, or either missing scan filter before an observation can be accepted.

The public/direct/contracts/attacks paths traverse the same owner/runner edge and expose two
executable B1 observations in role order (`ownership-probe`, `build`). Design Trial 7 also binds
the B2 observation sink to the same parent. Each role now exposes one owner-validated `01..10`
transaction; its retained cidfile was opened by held-dirfd `openat` with `O_NOFOLLOW`, contains
exactly 64 lowercase hex plus LF, and records reviewer-owned regular-file mode/link/size plus the
authenticated name/run/role/candidate tuple. The parent derives—not accepts as a child report—the
four failure branches and their exact `3/6/8/10` invocation counts. Build stdout is streamed
directly into the anonymous parent-held output inode, and that inode's device/inode/mode/link,
byte count, and SHA-256 must equal the run-child observation. Isolated B1 mutants delete either owner/runner edge,
return a caller summary, regress to `subprocess.run`, mutate every grammar family by deletion/
substitution/reordering/extra token, inherit ambient environment, alter or omit each private
environment key, remove or falsify pidfd/start-token/status/timing/count/hash observations, or
re-resolve the PID. Every such mutant must reject through this edge, emit no acceptance/DONE,
and retain exactly one semantic cleanup.

Isolated B2 mutants remove or alter cidfile metadata/shape, delete/reorder/append an operation,
replace direct held-inode custody, or mismatch the held-output hash; every mutant rejects before
`DONE` with the same one-cleanup invariant. The executable deadline owner takes one monotonic
`T0` before Git authentication or candidate materialization. Per-operation 10-second limits,
absolute probe/build/reconciliation/finalization boundaries `T0+60/T0+760/T0+860/T0+900`,
role durations `60/700/100/40`, and the one `T0+900` outer bound are consumed by the real path.
Both normal and failure residue scans are separately executed at least 250 ms apart, and final
fd synchronization, held-object reauthentication, cleanup, and root disposal remain inside the
same outer bound. `ParentCore` invokes cleanup once; neither a deadline nor cleanup creates a
second budget or retry.

stdin attachment and executes as `65534:65534`. The oracle
constructs both arrays and byte-compares every recorder argv before daemon contact; there is no
