The probe accepts only daemon/run/wait exit `0` with zero stdout and zero stderr bytes. The build
accepts only matching daemon/run/wait exit `0`, a bounded stderr stream whose byte count/hash,
one exact `D007D_MOUNTS_V2` record, and one final `D007D_BUILD_V2` record are verified, and raw
binary stdout written directly to the already-held output inode; client status or a cidfile alone
can never satisfy either role.

Every Docker CLI subprocess has its own pidfd and a 10-second operation deadline except attached
`run`/`wait`; the ownership probe has a 60-second role deadline, the build has a 700-second role
deadline, one failure reconciliation has a 100-second cap, 40 seconds remain reserved for final
proof/sync, and all are slices of the one 900-second outer deadline. A role deadline includes its
preflight and normal ten-operation transcript; unused time is not added to a later slice. Client exit is
never container exit. Normal success for each role has exactly these ordered Docker operations:

`I_name` is exactly `[DOCKER,"inspect","--type","container",ROLE_NAME]`; `I_id` substitutes the
held 64-hex ID. `P` is exactly
`[DOCKER,"ps","-aq","--filter","label=org.agents-orchestrator.d007d.run=<RUN_ID>",
"--filter","label=org.agents-orchestrator.d007d.role=<role>"]`. `WAIT`, `TERM`, `KILL`, and
`RM_FORCE` are respectively `[DOCKER,"container","wait",ID]`,
`[DOCKER,"container","kill","--signal","TERM",ID]`, the same with `KILL`, and
`[DOCKER,"container","rm","--force",ID]`. Inspect stdout must be one JSON object in the one-
element array; `P` stdout is either empty or newline-terminated 64-hex IDs and rejects duplicate,
foreign, partial, or diagnostic text.

```text
01 I_name                            -> not-found
02 P                                 -> empty
03 run <exact role argv>             -> attached client started
04 I_id                              -> exact identity/config; running or already exited
05 WAIT                              -> daemon exit status
06 I_id                              -> exact exited status/code
07 RM_FORCE                          -> removed
08 I_name                            -> not-found
09 P                                 -> empty
10 P after 250 ms                    -> empty
```

The recorder therefore expects exactly 20 normal-success operations: probe `01..10`, then build
`01..10`. It records argv, start/end monotonic times, client pidfd status, cidfile held identity,
daemon ID, normalized inspect projection, stdout/stderr byte counts/hashes, and result. The
attached `run` and `container wait` clients may physically finish in either order; evidence
normalizes them only after both are terminal and requires their container exit codes agree.

The cidfile is opened after creation with `openat(O_RDONLY|O_NOFOLLOW|O_CLOEXEC)` below the held
dirfd, must be a reviewer-owned mode-`0600` regular one-link file containing exactly 64 lowercase
hex plus LF, and remains held. If cidfile creation races, the parent polls only that dirfd for at
most 5 seconds. If it is absent, the parent may issue exactly one inspect-by-name; it adopts the
returned ID only for cleanup when all three labels/name match, and enters reconciliation without
resuming the role; not-found takes the missing branch below. A cidfile ID and name-derived ID
mismatch is fatal.

On role timeout, client death, signal, malformed output, or any mismatch, the parent performs this
bounded state machine against the held ID, or the exact name only until an ID is authenticated:

1. `inspect` (10 s). Not-found proceeds to the two label scans; running/exited/dead proceeds.
2. If running, `container kill --signal TERM <id>` (10 s), then start
   `container wait <id>` with a 10-second deadline.
3. If wait does not return, `container kill --signal KILL <id>` (10 s) and a second wait bounded
   to 5 seconds. `not running` is accepted only when immediate inspect says exited/dead with the
   same ID/labels.
4. `container rm --force <id>` (10 s). `no such container` is accepted only after inspect-by-name
   is not-found and the label scan is empty.
5. Require inspect-by-name not-found plus two empty exact-label `ps -aq` scans 250 ms apart.

The recorder accepts exactly one of these ordered reconciliation transcripts; `I_id` and
`I_name` mean the fixed inspect argv by authenticated ID and exact name, `P1/P2` are the two
exact-label scans, and every listed token is one Docker client invocation:

| Branch | Exact ordered invocations | Count |
|---|---|---:|
| `MISSING` (including no cid plus not-found) | `I_name, P1, P2` | 3 |
| `ALREADY_EXITED` | `I_id, WAIT, RM_FORCE, I_name, P1, P2` | 6 |
| `TERM_EXITED` | `I_id, TERM, WAIT, I_id, RM_FORCE, I_name, P1, P2` | 8 |
| `KILL_EXITED` | `I_id, TERM, WAIT_TIMEOUT, KILL, WAIT, I_id, RM_FORCE, I_name, P1, P2` | 10 |

`WAIT_TIMEOUT` is still one recorded invocation and has no normalized exit value. `RM_FORCE`
returning no-such does not remove `I_name,P1,P2`; `TERM`/`KILL` returning not-running is accepted
only when the following `I_id` proves the same labeled ID already exited. No branch repeats,
reorders, or omits a token. Thus every branch has at most one TERM, one KILL, two waits, one
force-remove, three inspect calls, and two final scans. The worst-case `95.25` seconds (including
all three inspects, two label scans, and the 250 ms separation) fits the immutable 100-second reconciliation slice; reaching that
slice or the outer deadline kills any live client by pidfd and yields `DOCKER_REJECTED` after the
same daemon reconciliation, never a larger retry budget.
Missing-before-cid with an exited nonzero run client is `START_FAILED` only after the same two
empty scans. Already-exited uses inspect/wait/remove without TERM/KILL. A reused name, foreign
label, multiple label result, cid mismatch, operation timeout, or residual ID is
`DOCKER_REJECTED`, suppresses `DONE`, and cannot be normalized as cleanup success. The next role
never starts unless the prior role is `RECONCILED` with its exact expected result. Docker
reconciliation completes before test-domain launch and before evidence finalization.

### Rejected Trial 4 reviewer/candidate custody boundary (historical)

The following Trial 4 V2 launch/D7C2 mechanics are retained only to reproduce the independent
KO and are never executable authority after Trial 5. The embedded V2 USTAR parser remains
incorporated by the Trial 5 contract above.

Design Trial 3 is immutable KO evidence. Its same-UID, mode-bit-only launcher and inherited
evidence/output descriptors are not an implementation option. Trial 4 replaces that model with
`D007D_REVIEW_CUSTODY_V2`: a reviewer-extracted parent program that remains outside the
candidate filesystem and process domains. The candidate can neither name nor address the
reviewer evidence file, reviewer output directory, Docker client, Docker socket, systemd user
bus, custody program, or host materialization paths. Candidate output is untrusted input.

The Trial 4 design reviewer extracts the byte-exact independent USTAR parser and container
program named in this section, runs their syntax checks, recomputes their digests, and records
those digests in the immutable Trial 4 result. The custody parent itself is governed by the
exact bounded-helper behavior, argv, syscalls, state transitions, caps, and oracles in this
section. At a checkpoint the reviewer first authenticates and reviews the proposed custody
implementation against that contract, then copies it with no hardlinks into a new reviewer-
owned mode-`0500` directory outside every candidate/run root and records its blob and extracted
SHA-256 before use. The candidate neither chooses the accepted digest nor can address the
extracted file at runtime; candidate-produced checksums, tests, and records grant no authority.

### Threat model and authority table

The attacker controls every byte executed below `/candidate`, may be same-owner on the host,
may chmod and restore any pathname it can reach, may fork, retain inherited fds, ignore TERM,
forge candidate records, delay EOF, emit malformed archives, and attempt to restore bytes before
the final check. It may exploit `/proc/self/fd`, pathname replacement, archive-parser
differences, and every tool exposed inside its namespace. It does not control the independently
extracted custody parent, Linux kernel, reviewer-selected prerequisite roots, or reviewer-held
descriptors. A compromised host kernel, reviewer account, Docker daemon, pinned image, or
reviewer-supplied toolchain is outside this local candidate-isolation claim and is recorded as a
limitation, not silently accepted.

| Object/operation | Candidate authority | Reviewer-parent authority and required proof |
|---|---|---|
| candidate, bare ODB, toolchain, `node_modules`, source archive | read only through separate `bwrap --ro-bind` mounts | authenticate commit/tree/blobs and prerequisite inode/type/owner/digest before spawn; retain no-follow fds; re-read the held fds and re-resolve all identities after domain exit |
| candidate work, `HOME`, `TMPDIR`, tmux runtime | private tmpfs writes inside candidate mount namespace only | inspect bounded records; never accept candidate pathname evidence; namespace disposal is not an acceptance oracle |
| evidence | no fd, directory, basename, pathname, environment value, or mount | open with `O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_RDWR|O_CLOEXEC|O_NOFOLLOW`, mode `0600`, below reviewer-only mode-`0700` dirfd; retain file/dirfds, `fsync` directory, validate records, `fdatasync`, hash with `pread`, and compare held `fstat` before `DONE` |
| build output | no fd, directory, basename, pathname, or mount | open with `O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_RDWR|O_CLOEXEC|O_NOFOLLOW`, mode `0700`, below a different reviewer-only dirfd; retain file/dirfds and `fsync` directory; Docker stdout goes directly to held fd; parent `fdatasync`s, hashes, parses, and uses `execveat(fd, "", ..., AT_EMPTY_PATH)` |
| Docker | no client binary, socket, environment, host path, or helper endpoint | parent alone validates canonical USTAR, constructs fixed argv, invokes the authenticated client, owns stdin/stdout/stderr, enforces timeout, and checks named-container residue |
| candidate records/archive | write-only bounded D7C2 stdout; bounded diagnostic stderr | parent owns read ends, frame parsers, byte/count limits, ordering, EOF decision, and durable projection |
| signals/process cleanup | candidate may fork and ignore TERM only inside its PID/cgroup domain | parent owns transient unit, pidfd/process identity, deadline, TERM/KILL, pipe closure, child wait, unit/container residue checks, and final `DONE` decision |

Mode bits are never the security boundary. The same host UID may own a source inode, but the
candidate sees it only through a kernel read-only mount in a new user/mount namespace with all
capabilities dropped. No writable bind of a host pathname exists. The candidate receives only
stdin `/dev/null`, one bounded D7C2 stdout pipe, and one bounded diagnostic stderr pipe;
`close_range(3, UINT_MAX, CLOSE_RANGE_UNSHARE)` is applied in the child immediately before the
candidate wrapper exec. Evidence and output fds are opened with `O_CLOEXEC` and are never in the
child fd table.

### Exact reviewer entry argv and candidate execution domain

The reviewer invokes only this ten-position argv in an empty environment; the substitutions
retain the Trial 3 meaning, but evidence and run-root values are parent-only and never cross the
namespace boundary:

```text
env -i LC_ALL=C LANG=C TZ=UTC PATH=/usr/bin:/bin \
  /absolute/reviewer-owned/d007d-review-custody-v2 \
  <bootstrap-probe|cp1-unit-custody|cp1-unit-teardown|cp1-unit-stream|cp2-unit|cp3-unit-cancel|cp3-unit-ordering|cp3-unit-matrix|docker-integration|focused|full-ci> \
  /absolute/reviewer-only/candidate-object-db.git \
  <candidate-40-hex-sha> <candidate-40-hex-tree> \
  /absolute/reviewer-only/tmux-3.6a.tar.gz \
  /absolute/reviewer-only/toolchain \
  /absolute/reviewer-only/gateway-node-modules \
  /absolute/reviewer-only/docker-client \
  /absolute/reviewer-evidence/new-file.jsonl \
  /absolute/reviewer-tmpfs/new-run-root
```

The parent rejects any extra environment entry, argument, non-absolute path, symlink, existing
output, cross-device parent, non-regular input, writable prerequisite mount, wrong commit/tree/
blob/digest, alternates file, unsafe Git configuration, wrong owner/mode, or unavailable
primitive before candidate execution. It creates a no-hardlink detached materialization under
the reviewer run root, opens the root and every critical file no-follow, records device/inode/
mode/uid/gid/size plus Git blob, and never exposes the host pathname. The candidate namespace
starts from the authenticated hermetic read-only root and receives exactly these mounts:

Before its first spawn the parent calls
`prctl(PR_SET_CHILD_SUBREAPER, 1, 0, 0, 0)` and requires
`prctl(PR_GET_CHILD_SUBREAPER, &value, 0, 0, 0)` to return `value == 1`; it records the
pre-spawn direct-child set and opens a pidfd for every child it creates. Failure is
`infrastructure_unavailable`, never a fallback to numeric-PID cleanup.

```text
/                              ro-bind authenticated hermetic toolchain root
/candidate                    ro-bind authenticated materialization
/odb                          ro-bind authenticated bare object database
/deps                         ro-bind authenticated gateway node_modules
/inputs/tmux-3.6a.tar.gz      ro-bind authenticated held-source pathname
/work                         tmpfs, candidate-private
/home                         tmpfs, candidate-private
/tmp                          tmpfs, candidate-private
/proc                         private proc for the new PID namespace
/dev                          minimal bwrap dev
```

Build-bearing outer modes use two separate fresh candidate domains. The `archive` phase has the
map above and may emit one type-`4` D7C2 frame. After parent-side parse and Docker validation,
the `test` phase adds exactly one read-only mount,
`/inputs/tmux-3.6a-agents.1`, sourced by the parent from its still-held output inode; it cannot
emit type `4`. The parent validates the held output before and after that phase. No candidate
domain spans the Docker operation.

The hermetic root contains only reviewer-authenticated minimum executables/runtime libraries and
empty mountpoints `candidate`, `odb`, `deps`, `inputs`, `work`, `home`, `tmp`, `proc`, and `dev`;
the parent rejects another top-level entry, writable inode, device, socket, set-id bit, or
symlink escape. No host `/`, repository root, evidence/output parent, Docker client/socket, user
bus, credential, shared tmux path, or other device is mounted. The exact candidate-domain argv, after the

[SYSTEMD_RUN, "--user", "--wait", "--pipe", "--collect", "--quiet",

For a build-bearing `test` phase only, the parent inserts
`["--ro-bind", HOST_HELD_OUTPUT, "/inputs/tmux-3.6a-agents.1"]` immediately after the source

appears in the child argv after `bwrap` constructs the mount, environment, or visible `/proc`.
The `systemd-run` client and bwrap setup/supervisor remain outside the nested candidate PID
namespace. Candidate `/proc` is mounted only after namespace creation and contains PID 1 wrapper
plus its descendants; the parent requires the path-discovery attack to inspect every visible
cmdline, env, fd link, mountinfo entry, and namespace link and find no reviewer pathname.

`SYSTEMD_RUN` and `BWRAP` are reviewer-authenticated absolute executables. UID `0` is root only
inside the new user namespace and maps to the unprivileged reviewer UID outside; capability
drop plus the read-only mounts is the enforcement boundary. The parent requires Linux/amd64,
user/mount/PID/network/IPC/UTS/cgroup namespace creation, private `/proc`, `close_range`, pidfd,
`PR_SET_CHILD_SUBREAPER`, user-systemd accounting, and `O_TMPFILE`/held-fd operations to work as
probed. Darwin and every unsupported Linux host return terminal `infrastructure_unavailable`,
emit no `DONE`, and cannot be counted as skip, pass, or D/0/07d evidence. Darwin's existing
deterministic product seam tests still run in canonical CI, but this sheet claims no Darwin
real-host custody lane.

### Bounded pipes, parent records, and held-inode validation

Candidate stdout uses `D7C2`: 12-byte header
`44 37 43 32 02 <type> 00 00 <u32be-length>` followed by exactly that many bytes. JSON frame
length is `1..65536`, aggregate JSON is at most `4 MiB`, and at most `256` JSON frames are
accepted. Type `1` is a canonical UTF-8 TAP record, type `2` a canonical UTF-8 fixture ledger,
type `3` a bounded UTF-8 candidate diagnostic, and type `4` the single raw USTAR payload of at
most `256 MiB`. Unknown types/reserved bits, noncanonical UTF-8, duplicate JSON keys, non-
integer numbers/NaN, extra fields, invalid mode-specific order, a second type-`4` frame, or
trailing partial bytes fail. Candidate diagnostics are never authority. The parent projects
validated records as sorted-key JSON lines to its held evidence fd; candidate bytes are never
copied as already-serialized evidence. It copies type `4` directly into a reviewer-held scratch
fd and independently parses that fd before Docker.

The archive phase may emit type `3` diagnostics then exactly one type `4` frame and EOF. After
the parent validates/builds/holds the output, a separate fresh test-phase domain receives that
output only through a new read-only `/inputs/tmux-3.6a-agents.1` mount and may emit types `1..3`
but never type `4`. Unit modes emit types `1..3` only. A child or descendant retaining stdout or
stderr prevents EOF; at deadline the parent closes its endpoints, terminates the whole unit,
and emits no `DONE`. Diagnostic stderr is capped at `16 MiB` and never becomes evidence.

Immediately after unit start, the parent binds the exact `systemd-run` direct child with a pidfd,
resolves the unit's `ControlGroup` through the reviewer user manager, opens that exact cgroup and
its `cgroup.procs` no-follow, and retains the dirfd/file fd. A missing, reused, root-escaping, or
changed unit/cgroup identity aborts before candidate release.

After each candidate phase exits, the parent waits for both pipes to reach exact EOF, closes the
candidate-side descriptors, and queries the transient unit plus held `cgroup.procs`. It requires
inactive/dead or already-collected-success state, two consecutive empty held-cgroup reads, no
pidfd-live direct child, and two consecutive empty adopted-child scans. On the first failure or
the monotonic 900-second deadline it sends pidfd TERM to
the exact child and `systemctl --user kill --kill-whom=all --signal=TERM <unit>`, waits 500 ms,
then repeats with KILL, closes every pipe, waits at most two seconds, and rechecks twice. A
signal-resistant descendant, retained fd, live unit, PID-identity change, or cleanup timeout is
terminal failure. `systemctl reset-failed <unit>` occurs only after the zero-process proof and
is followed by an absent-unit check. No candidate record can suppress this sequence.

The parent then re-reads every pre-opened candidate/prerequisite fd with `pread`, recomputes its
digest/blob, compares its retained `fstat` identity, independently re-resolves candidate
HEAD/tree/clean state from the reviewer side, and rejects restore-before-final-check attacks.
It validates the evidence and output using their original held fds and identities, not their
pathnames. Version execution is an exact fork plus
`execveat(output_fd, "", ["tmux", "-V"], EMPTY_ENV, AT_EMPTY_PATH)` with pidfd-bound wait,
captured `tmux 3.6a-agents.1\n`, empty stderr, and zero exit; no pathname reopen is allowed. A
test-only parent seam replaces each pathname while the fd is open; the replacement must not
affect the hash/type/version/execute result, and it is never accepted as the output.
Only after parent-directory `fsync`, file `fdatasync`, held-inode SHA-256, evidence self-parse, candidate/unit/container residue
proof, and parent-owned reauthentication does the parent append
`D007D_REVIEW_DONE_V2`, `fdatasync` again, and close. Any failure suppresses `DONE`.

### Independent canonical USTAR parser

The reviewer parent does not use `tar`, the candidate coordinator, the pinned image, or a
candidate test to decide framing. `D007D_USTAR_PARSER_V2` parses 512-byte records from the held
archive fd and accepts exactly three regular members in this order:

```text
tmux-3.6a.tar.gz
tmux-3.6a-agents.1.patch
cmd-agents-capture.c
```

For each member it constructs an expected 512-byte header from the independently authenticated
name, payload length, and payload digest, then requires byte equality. Header offsets are
`name[0:100]`, `mode[100:108]`, `uid[108:116]`, `gid[116:124]`, `size[124:136]`,
`mtime[136:148]`, `checksum[148:156]`, `type[156]`, `linkname[157:257]`,
`magic[257:263]`, `version[263:265]`, `uname[265:297]`, `gname[297:329]`,
`devmajor[329:337]`, `devminor[337:345]`, `prefix[345:500]`, and zero pad
`[500:512]`. The exact fields are name plus NUL fill, `0000444\0`, `0000000\0`,
`0000000\0`, eleven lower-case octal size digits plus NUL, `00000000000\0`, six lower-case
octal checksum digits plus NUL-space, type `0`, zero linkname, `ustar\0`, `00`, zero uname/
gname/prefix/pad, and `0000000\0` for both device fields. Checksum is the unsigned sum of all
header bytes with `[148:156]` treated as eight spaces. Base-256, signed, overflow, alternate
terminators, or noncanonical octal is rejected.

The parser hashes each exact-size payload, requires the pinned source/patch/extension SHA-256,
requires every payload-padding byte to be zero, then requires exactly two all-zero 512-byte
blocks followed by immediate EOF. Empty/extra members, duplicate or reordered names, malformed
header/checksum/size/type/link/padding, a third zero block, or even one trailing byte fails
before Docker. The container may use `tar` only after this independent acceptance; container
parsing is defense in depth and makes no framing claim.

The following Python is the byte-exact parser core. `expected` is a parent-created tuple of
`(ascii_name, integer_size, lowercase_sha256)` derived from the three held prerequisite fds;
candidate data cannot create it.

<!-- D007D_USTAR_PARSER_V2_BEGIN -->

```python
import hashlib
import os
import stat

EXPECTED_NAMES = (
    "tmux-3.6a.tar.gz",
    "tmux-3.6a-agents.1.patch",
    "cmd-agents-capture.c",
)


def _pread_exact(fd, length, offset):
    chunks = []
    remaining = length
    while remaining:
        chunk = os.pread(fd, remaining, offset)
        if not chunk:
            raise ValueError("truncated USTAR")
        chunks.append(chunk)
        offset += len(chunk)
        remaining -= len(chunk)
    return b"".join(chunks), offset


def _canonical_header(name, size):
    encoded = name.encode("ascii")
    if not encoded or len(encoded) > 99 or b"\0" in encoded:
        raise ValueError("noncanonical USTAR name")
    if not isinstance(size, int) or size < 0 or size > 0o77777777777:
        raise ValueError("noncanonical USTAR size")
    header = bytearray(512)
    header[:len(encoded)] = encoded
    header[100:108] = b"0000444\0"
    header[108:116] = b"0000000\0"
    header[116:124] = b"0000000\0"
    header[124:136] = f"{size:011o}\0".encode("ascii")
    header[136:148] = b"00000000000\0"
    header[148:156] = b"        "
    header[156] = ord("0")
    header[257:263] = b"ustar\0"
    header[263:265] = b"00"
    header[329:337] = b"0000000\0"
    header[337:345] = b"0000000\0"
    checksum = f"{sum(header):06o}\0 ".encode("ascii")
    if len(checksum) != 8:
        raise ValueError("noncanonical USTAR checksum")
    header[148:156] = checksum
    return bytes(header)


def validate_ustar_v2(fd, expected):
    if tuple(item[0] for item in expected) != EXPECTED_NAMES:
        raise ValueError("wrong USTAR authority ledger")
    metadata = os.fstat(fd)
    if not stat.S_ISREG(metadata.st_mode) or metadata.st_size > 268435456:
        raise ValueError("invalid USTAR held object")
    offset = 0
    ledger = []
    for name, size, expected_digest in expected:
        header, offset = _pread_exact(fd, 512, offset)
        if header != _canonical_header(name, size):
            raise ValueError("noncanonical USTAR header")
        payload, offset = _pread_exact(fd, size, offset)
        digest = hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest()
        if digest != expected_digest:
            raise ValueError("USTAR payload authority mismatch")
        padding_length = (-size) % 512
        padding, offset = _pread_exact(fd, padding_length, offset)
        if padding != b"\0" * padding_length:
            raise ValueError("nonzero USTAR padding")
        ledger.append((name, size, digest))
    end_blocks, offset = _pread_exact(fd, 1024, offset)
    if end_blocks != b"\0" * 1024:
        raise ValueError("invalid USTAR terminator")
    if offset != metadata.st_size or os.pread(fd, 1, offset) != b"":
        raise ValueError("trailing USTAR byte")
    return tuple(ledger)
```

<!-- D007D_USTAR_PARSER_V2_END -->

Mechanical extraction of the LF-terminated bytes inside the Python fence has SHA-256
`77e20732a688b6db779002cc6728a6dc8d604e2112f505a15d7046657e09ef4a` and must compile with
the authenticated toolchain Python. The reviewer recomputes both facts rather than trusting
this author value.

### Mandatory Trial 4 authority RED/GREEN oracles

Checkpoint 1 adds reviewer-owned harness tests, separate from candidate tests, that run a
malicious candidate fixture in the real custody domain. Each RED first proves the rejected
Trial 3 model would accept the attack; GREEN must reject or contain it and emit no `DONE`:

| Attack | Exact GREEN oracle |
|---|---|
| same-owner `chmod u+w`, mutate, restore mode | every write to `/candidate`, `/odb`, toolchain-root `/bin` and `/usr`, `/deps`, and `/inputs/tmux-3.6a.tar.gz` fails `EROFS`; held hashes unchanged |
| inherited evidence fd truncation/forgery and evidence path discovery | no evidence fd/path/directory is present in env, mounts, argv, or `/proc/self/fd`; only D7C2 stdout is writable; forged/truncated frame fails |
| output rename/replacement while fd open | output path is undiscoverable and unmounted; reviewer replacement seam changes pathname inode but held-inode hash/type/version/exec remains bound to original |
| retained child or pipe fd after wrapper return | parent observes missing EOF, kills domain, proves zero processes/fds/unit residue, emits no `DONE` |
| timeout with TERM-ignoring descendant | exact unit receives TERM then KILL within the frozen bounds; two empty scans, no residue, no `DONE` |
| restore prerequisite/candidate bytes before final check | kernel write fails; independent held-fd and parent pathname reauthentication both remain mandatory and mutation makes the oracle fail |
| archive trailing byte, malformed header/checksum/size/padding, duplicate, reorder, or extra zero block | independent parser rejects before Docker invocation count changes from zero |
| evidence/output path discovery plus writes to every read-only prerequisite | discovery set is empty; every prerequisite write fails `EROFS`; candidate sees only declared mounts |
| Docker arbitrary operation (`ps`, `run`, socket open, or option injection) | no client/socket/helper is reachable in candidate domain; reviewer recorder observes only the fixed parent ownership-probe and build invocations |

The integration GREEN is authoritative only when every attack executes, its expected kernel or
parent rejection is recorded, all reviewer-held identities remain exact, and residue is zero.
A source-text assertion, candidate-authored recorder, skipped namespace probe, or cleanup after
the fact is not this evidence.

Trial 4 changes no other design ruling:

| Trial 3 P1 | Trial 4 closure |
|---|---|
| P1-1 — same-UID candidate controls materialization, evidence, output, timeout, and parser | kernel-read-only mounts in an isolated user/mount/PID/cgroup domain; D7C2-only candidate output; parent-only evidence/output held inodes; exact deadline/domain cleanup; independent USTAR parser; parent-side held-fd and Git reauthentication; the nine-attack RED/GREEN |
| P1-2 — non-root container cannot write root-owned mode-0700 tmpfs | exact matching `uid=<selected>,gid=<selected>,mode=0700` on both roots; non-root identity/mount/first-write/exec validation; deterministic other-identity denial; wrong/omitted-option recorder mutants |

The stream-only/no-bind/no-host-staging design, immutable legacy recipe, seven checkpoint-1
paths, 126/127 inventory digests, serial checkpoint reviews, cancel RED, zero-deletion outer
harness, bounded cleanup, status arithmetic, splice block, and release non-claims remain exact.

## Rejected Trial 3 launcher (historical attack fixture; never executable)

The following Trial 3 material is retained only to make the immutable KO mechanically
reproducible. It is not normative Trial 6 input, is never extracted by a checkpoint reviewer,
and grants no implementation authority. Names inside this historical block denote
`D007D_REVIEW_CUSTODY_V2`; every V4 command outside it invokes the extracted authority parent.

The implementation candidate does not attest its own provenance. Design Trial 3 freezes
`D007D_REVIEW_LAUNCHER_V1` below as reviewer-owned bytes outside every implementation
candidate. The independent Design Trial 3 reviewer must extract the bytes between the launcher
markers from this exact reviewed design commit, recompute their SHA-256, and record the digest
in its immutable `D_0_7D_DESIGN-3_result.md`. For every implementation checkpoint, a fresh
reviewer creates that launcher outside the candidate materialization, verifies it against the
Design Trial 3 result, and records the launcher path, digest, owner, mode, and extraction commit.
The candidate branch, wrapper, coordinator, tests, and evidence parser cannot create, replace,
approve, or hash-authorize the launcher. This independent action is the non-circular trust
anchor.

The launcher is invoked only by the following ten-position argv under the exact empty
environment. Angle-bracket values are reviewer-selected absolute paths or the frozen full
candidate identities; they are substitutions, not optional arguments:

```text
env -i LC_ALL=C LANG=C TZ=UTC PATH=/usr/bin:/bin \
  /absolute/reviewer-owned/d007d-review-launcher-v1 \
  <bootstrap-probe|cp1-unit-teardown|cp1-unit-stream|cp2-unit|cp3-unit-cancel|cp3-unit-ordering|cp3-unit-matrix|docker-integration|focused|full-ci> \
  /absolute/read-only/candidate-object-db.git \
  <candidate-40-hex-sha> <candidate-40-hex-tree> \
  /absolute/path/tmux-3.6a.tar.gz \
  /absolute/read-only/toolchain \
  /absolute/read-only/gateway-node-modules \
  /absolute/read-only/docker-client \
  /absolute/reviewer-evidence/new-file.jsonl \
  /absolute/reviewer-tmpfs/new-run-root
```

No other environment variable or input is accepted. The object database is a reviewer-created
`git clone --bare --no-hardlinks` whose candidate commit/tree were independently resolved before
it was mounted/read-protected; it is not the candidate worktree. The evidence file and run root
must not exist, the evidence parent is reviewer-owned mode `0700`, and the run-root parent must
report `tmpfs`. A non-absolute path, writable object database/toolchain/dependency input, wrong
argument count/mode/SHA/tree, existing output, symlink, non-regular input, unavailable command,
or failed check exits nonzero before candidate execution and is recorded by the reviewer as no
evidence, never as a skip or `DEFERRED` pass.

<!-- D007D_REVIEW_LAUNCHER_V1_BEGIN -->

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
umask 077
export GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0
test "$#" -eq 10
mode=$1; odb=$2; candidate=$3; expected_tree=$4; source_archive=$5
toolchain=$6; node_modules=$7; docker_bin=$8; evidence=$9; run_root=${10}
case "$mode" in
  bootstrap-probe|cp1-unit-teardown|cp1-unit-stream|cp2-unit|cp3-unit-cancel|cp3-unit-ordering|cp3-unit-matrix|docker-integration|focused|full-ci) ;;
  *) exit 64 ;;
esac
[[ "$candidate" =~ ^[0-9a-f]{40}$ ]]
[[ "$expected_tree" =~ ^[0-9a-f]{40}$ ]]
for value in "$odb" "$source_archive" "$toolchain" "$node_modules" \
  "$docker_bin" "$evidence" "$run_root"; do case "$value" in /*) ;; *) exit 64 ;; esac; done
test -d "$odb"; test ! -w "$odb"; test -d "$toolchain"; test ! -w "$toolchain"
test -d "$node_modules"; test ! -w "$node_modules"
test -z "$(find "$odb" "$toolchain" "$node_modules" -xdev -perm /222 -print -quit)"
test ! -s "$odb/objects/info/alternates"
test -z "$(git --git-dir="$odb" config --local --name-regexp \
  '^(filter\.|include\.|includeIf\.|core\.hooksPath$|core\.fsmonitor$|core\.sshCommand$)' || true)"
test ! -L "$docker_bin"; test -f "$docker_bin"; test -x "$docker_bin"; test ! -w "$docker_bin"
test ! -L "$source_archive"; test -f "$source_archive"; test ! -w "$source_archive"
test "$(stat -c '%F:%u' -- "$source_archive")" = "regular file:$(id -u)"
evidence_parent=$(dirname -- "$evidence"); run_parent=$(dirname -- "$run_root")
test -d "$evidence_parent"; test ! -L "$evidence_parent"
test "$(stat -c '%F:%a:%u' -- "$evidence_parent")" = "directory:700:$(id -u)"
test ! -e "$evidence"; test ! -e "$run_root"
test "$(stat -f -c %T -- "$run_parent")" = tmpfs
run_nonce=$(basename -- "$run_root"); [[ "$run_nonce" =~ ^[A-Za-z0-9._-]+$ ]]
mkdir -m 0700 -- "$run_root"
materialized=$run_root/candidate
test "$(git --git-dir="$odb" rev-parse --verify "$candidate^{commit}")" = "$candidate"
test "$(git --git-dir="$odb" rev-parse "$candidate^{tree}")" = "$expected_tree"
git clone --no-hardlinks --no-checkout -- "$odb" "$materialized" >/dev/null
git -C "$materialized" remote remove origin
git -C "$materialized" checkout --detach "$candidate" >/dev/null
test "$(git -C "$materialized" rev-parse HEAD)" = "$candidate"
test "$(git -C "$materialized" rev-parse 'HEAD^{tree}')" = "$expected_tree"
test -z "$(git -C "$materialized" symbolic-ref -q HEAD || true)"
test -z "$(git -C "$materialized" status --porcelain=v1 --untracked-files=all)"
wrapper=$materialized/tests/gateway/run_process_supervisor_session_port_real_host.sh
coordinator=$materialized/tests/gateway/process_supervisor_session_port_fixture_owner.js
manifest=$materialized/gateway/vendor/tmux-agents/manifest.json
patch=$materialized/gateway/vendor/tmux-agents/tmux-3.6a-agents.1.patch
extension=$materialized/gateway/vendor/tmux-agents/cmd-agents-capture.c
recipe=$materialized/gateway/vendor/tmux-agents/build-offline.sh
test -x "$wrapper"; test -f "$coordinator"; test -f "$manifest"
test -f "$patch"; test -f "$extension"; test -x "$recipe"
for repo_path in \
  tests/gateway/run_process_supervisor_session_port_real_host.sh \
  tests/gateway/process_supervisor_session_port_fixture_owner.js \
  gateway/vendor/tmux-agents/manifest.json \
  gateway/vendor/tmux-agents/tmux-3.6a-agents.1.patch \
  gateway/vendor/tmux-agents/cmd-agents-capture.c \
  gateway/vendor/tmux-agents/build-offline.sh scripts/ci_gate.py scripts/ci.sh; do
  blob=$(git -C "$materialized" rev-parse "$candidate:$repo_path")
  test "$(git -C "$materialized" hash-object "$materialized/$repo_path")" = "$blob"
done
chmod -R a-w -- "$materialized"
test -z "$(git -C "$materialized" status --porcelain=v1 --untracked-files=all)"
set -o noclobber; : >"$evidence"; set +o noclobber
exec 3>>"$evidence"
test "$(stat -c '%F:%a:%u:%h' -- "$evidence")" = "regular file:600:$(id -u):1"
source_hash=$(sha256sum -- "$source_archive" | cut -d ' ' -f1)
test "$source_hash" = b6d8d9c76585db8ef5fa00d4931902fa4b8cbe8166f528f44fc403961a3f3759
container_label="org.agents-orchestrator.d007d=$candidate.$run_nonce"
printf 'D007D_PROVENANCE_V1\tcandidate=%s\ttree=%s\tmode=%s\tlabel=%s\n' \
  "$candidate" "$expected_tree" "$mode" "$container_label" >&3
printf 'D007D_INPUT_V1\tsource=%s\tsource_sha256=%s\ttoolchain=%s\tnode_modules=%s\tdocker=%s\n' \
  "$(realpath -e -- "$source_archive")" "$source_hash" \
  "$(realpath -e -- "$toolchain")" "$(realpath -e -- "$node_modules")" \
  "$(realpath -e -- "$docker_bin")" >&3
for repo_path in \
  tests/gateway/run_process_supervisor_session_port_real_host.sh \
  tests/gateway/process_supervisor_session_port_fixture_owner.js \
  gateway/vendor/tmux-agents/manifest.json \
  gateway/vendor/tmux-agents/tmux-3.6a-agents.1.patch \
  gateway/vendor/tmux-agents/cmd-agents-capture.c \
  gateway/vendor/tmux-agents/build-offline.sh scripts/ci_gate.py scripts/ci.sh; do
  printf 'D007D_BLOB_V1\t%s\t%s\n' "$repo_path" \
    "$(git -C "$materialized" rev-parse "$candidate:$repo_path")" >&3
done
common_env=(env -i LC_ALL=C LANG=C TZ=UTC PATH=/usr/bin:/bin
  GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0
  HOME="$run_root/home" TMPDIR="$run_root/tmp"
  D007D_CANDIDATE_ROOT="$materialized" D007D_COORDINATOR="$coordinator"
  D007D_TMUX_SOURCE_ARCHIVE="$source_archive"
  D007D_GATE_TOOLCHAIN_ROOT="$toolchain"
  D007D_GATEWAY_NODE_MODULES="$node_modules"
  D007D_DOCKER_BIN="$docker_bin"
  D007D_CONTAINER_LABEL_VALUE="$container_label"
  D007D_EVIDENCE_FD=3)
mkdir -m 0700 -- "$run_root/home" "$run_root/tmp"
if test "$mode" = bootstrap-probe; then
  "${common_env[@]}" "$wrapper" --bootstrap-probe 3>&3
else
  "${common_env[@]}" "$toolchain/bin/python3" \
    "$materialized/scripts/ci_gate.py" --repo-root "$materialized" --validate-only >&3
fi
case "$mode" in
bootstrap-probe) ;;
cp1-unit-teardown|cp1-unit-stream|cp2-unit|cp3-unit-cancel|cp3-unit-ordering|cp3-unit-matrix)
  "${common_env[@]}" "$wrapper" "--$mode" 3>&3
  ;;
docker-integration|focused|full-ci)
  set -o noclobber; exec 4>"$run_root/tmux-3.6a-agents.1"; \
    exec 5>"$run_root/build.stderr"; set +o noclobber
  chmod 0700 -- "$run_root/tmux-3.6a-agents.1"
  "${common_env[@]}" "$wrapper" --stream-build 1>&4 2>&5 3>&3
  exec 4>&-; exec 5>&-
  test "$(grep -Ec $'^D007D_BUILD_V1\tbytes=[0-9]+\tsha256=[0-9a-f]{64}$' \
    "$run_root/build.stderr")" -eq 1
  status_line=$(tail -n 1 -- "$run_root/build.stderr")
  [[ "$status_line" =~ ^D007D_BUILD_V1$'\t'bytes=([0-9]+)$'\t'sha256=([0-9a-f]{64})$ ]]
  expected_bytes=${BASH_REMATCH[1]}; expected_hash=${BASH_REMATCH[2]}
  verify_binary() {
    test "$(stat -c %s -- "$run_root/tmux-3.6a-agents.1")" = "$expected_bytes"
    test "$(sha256sum -- "$run_root/tmux-3.6a-agents.1" | cut -d ' ' -f1)" = "$expected_hash"
    test "$(stat -c '%F:%a:%u:%h' -- "$run_root/tmux-3.6a-agents.1")" = \
      "regular file:700:$(id -u):1"
    test "$(od -An -tx1 -N4 -- "$run_root/tmux-3.6a-agents.1" | tr -d ' \n')" = 7f454c46
    test "$(od -An -tu1 -j4 -N2 -- "$run_root/tmux-3.6a-agents.1" | xargs)" = '2 1'
    test "$(od -An -tx1 -j18 -N2 -- "$run_root/tmux-3.6a-agents.1" | tr -d ' \n')" = 3e00
    test "$("$run_root/tmux-3.6a-agents.1" -V)" = 'tmux 3.6a-agents.1'
    "${common_env[@]}" "$wrapper" --verify-extension \
      "$run_root/tmux-3.6a-agents.1" 3>&3
  }
  verify_binary
  if test "$mode" != docker-integration; then
    "${common_env[@]}" D007D_BUILT_TMUX="$run_root/tmux-3.6a-agents.1" \
      "$wrapper" "--$mode" 3>&3
    verify_binary
  fi
  ;;
esac
test "$(git -C "$materialized" rev-parse HEAD)" = "$candidate"
test "$(git -C "$materialized" rev-parse 'HEAD^{tree}')" = "$expected_tree"
test -z "$(git -C "$materialized" status --porcelain=v1 --untracked-files=all)"
for repo_path in \
  tests/gateway/run_process_supervisor_session_port_real_host.sh \
  tests/gateway/process_supervisor_session_port_fixture_owner.js \
  gateway/vendor/tmux-agents/manifest.json \
  gateway/vendor/tmux-agents/tmux-3.6a-agents.1.patch \
  gateway/vendor/tmux-agents/cmd-agents-capture.c \
  gateway/vendor/tmux-agents/build-offline.sh scripts/ci_gate.py scripts/ci.sh; do
  test "$(git -C "$materialized" hash-object "$materialized/$repo_path")" = \
    "$(git -C "$materialized" rev-parse "$candidate:$repo_path")"
done
printf 'D007D_LAUNCHER_DONE_V1\tmode=%s\n' "$mode" >&3
sync -d -- "$evidence"
```

<!-- D007D_REVIEW_LAUNCHER_V1_END -->

For immutable Trial 3 reproduction only, the block is preserved byte-for-byte (LF endings,
including its final LF). Mechanical extraction has historical expected digest
`sha256:789b74670a9313120ce4e1e1f29ee44b25476cce4d086103e4e51c43b7a01263`, repeated in the
Trial 3 request; the independent reviewer must recompute it rather than trust the author value.
The apparent `env` array is Bash argv
construction, never `eval`, `source`, a shell command string, or candidate-selected execution.
Every repository executable or parser is addressed by an absolute path below the authenticated,
read-only materialization. The external Git/stat/hash/core utilities, explicit toolchain,
dependency tree, Docker client, source archive, evidence parent, and tmpfs parent are
reviewer-owned prerequisites and are recorded; none may be supplied by candidate output.
For every `--cp*-unit-*` mode, the absolute candidate wrapper maps that mode to the one frozen
Node argv printed in its checkpoint below, uses the absolute toolchain Node binary, sets the
authenticated materialization as cwd, and parses that one child. A RED run materializes the
exact committed RED SHA/tree; its GREEN rerun materializes the exact checkpoint candidate.
An expected assertion RED exits exactly `1` after appending its exact TAP totals and has no
launcher done record; its GREEN exits `0` with the frozen totals and one done record. Syntax,
import, missing-test, signal, timeout, skip, or cancellation cannot stand in for exit `1`.
Neither run may execute a test from the implementation worktree. For `--stream-build`, the
absolute candidate wrapper must directly execute the absolute
`D007D_COORDINATOR` with the absolute toolchain Node binary and a fixed `--stream-build` argv;
it may not source or evaluate either file. For `--focused` and `--full-ci`, only that same
absolute wrapper may start the same absolute coordinator and the absolute materialized test,
validator, or `scripts/ci.sh` paths. Relative repository execution and ambient `$PWD` lookup
are hard failures.

The rejected launcher opened fd 3 before the first candidate instruction, wrote the provenance/blob
preamble itself, and alone opens/replaces the durable evidence pathname. Candidate code may
append schema-checked test/build records only through inherited fd 3; it cannot overwrite the
launcher records or claim their authority. For the build, the launcher also opens fd 4 as the
single regular binary destination and fd 5 as build stderr before executing the absolute
candidate wrapper. It compares the final one-and-only build-status record to the captured byte
count/hash, so the stdout protocol is deliberately unframed: byte zero is ELF byte zero and EOF
is binary EOF. A missing/duplicate status record, any prefix/header, delimiter, second payload,
or trailing byte changes the recorded size/hash or ELF magic and fails. The final clean/tree/blob
recheck and `D007D_LAUNCHER_DONE_V1` are launcher-owned. Nonzero child status, signal, timeout,
parse error, identity drift, residue, or inability to prove these conditions exits nonzero and
cannot emit the done record.

Trial 3 proposed that each checkpoint request record the exact RED and GREEN/candidate blobs for the wrapper,
coordinator,
manifest, patch, extension, frozen recipe, `scripts/ci_gate.py`, `scripts/ci.sh`, and every

### Candidate-bootstrap substitution RED

In a disposable reviewer worktree at the candidate commit, the reviewer first creates the
read-only bare object database, then replaces the ambient worktree wrapper and coordinator with
two canaries that append their own absolute path to a reviewer-owned sentinel and exit `97`.
Running the exact launcher from that poisoned worktree in `bootstrap-probe` mode must exit `0`,
leave the sentinel absent, record the candidate wrapper/coordinator blobs in its launcher-owned
preamble, and record the authenticated wrapper's probe token. A one-line reviewer-only mutant
that changes either absolute materialized path to `$PWD/tests/gateway/...` is the RED: it exits
`97` and creates the sentinel. Restoring the exact launcher bytes is GREEN. The ambient canaries
are never committed and the reviewer discards only this disposable worktree. The identical
unmutated bootstrap is mandatory before `docker-integration`, `focused`, and `full-ci`; no
candidate self-hash or clean clone created after execution can substitute for it.

<!-- D007D_CONTAINER_PROGRAM_V2_BEGIN -->

```sh
exec 3>&1
exec 1>&2
umask 077
test "$(id -u)" = "$D007D_SELECTED_UID"
test "$(id -g)" = "$D007D_SELECTED_GID"
test "$(stat -c '%u:%g:%a:%F' /build)" = "$D007D_SELECTED_UID:$D007D_SELECTED_GID:700:directory"
test "$(stat -c '%u:%g:%a:%F' /tmp)" = "$D007D_SELECTED_UID:$D007D_SELECTED_GID:700:directory"
test "$(awk '$5 == "/build" { count += 1 } END { print count + 0 }' /proc/self/mountinfo)" -eq 1
test "$(awk '$5 == "/tmp" { count += 1 } END { print count + 0 }' /proc/self/mountinfo)" -eq 1
build_mount=$(awk '$5 == "/build" { print $6 }' /proc/self/mountinfo)
tmp_mount=$(awk '$5 == "/tmp" { print $6 }' /proc/self/mountinfo)
case ",$build_mount," in *,rw,*) ;; *) exit 71 ;; esac
case ",$build_mount," in *,nosuid,*) ;; *) exit 71 ;; esac
case ",$build_mount," in *,nodev,*) ;; *) exit 71 ;; esac
case ",$build_mount," in *,noexec,*) exit 71 ;; esac
case ",$tmp_mount," in *,rw,*) ;; *) exit 71 ;; esac
case ",$tmp_mount," in *,nosuid,*) ;; *) exit 71 ;; esac
case ",$tmp_mount," in *,nodev,*) ;; *) exit 71 ;; esac
case ",$tmp_mount," in *,noexec,*) exit 71 ;; esac
printf 'D007D_MOUNTS_V2\tuid=%s\tgid=%s\tbuild=%s\ttmp=%s\n' \
  "$D007D_SELECTED_UID" "$D007D_SELECTED_GID" "$build_mount" "$tmp_mount" >&2
printf '#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n' > /build/.d007d-write-exec-probe
chmod 0700 /build/.d007d-write-exec-probe
/build/.d007d-write-exec-probe
printf '#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n' > /tmp/.d007d-write-exec-probe
chmod 0700 /tmp/.d007d-write-exec-probe
/tmp/.d007d-write-exec-probe
mkdir -p /build/input /build/source
cat > /build/input.tar
printf '%s\n' tmux-3.6a.tar.gz tmux-3.6a-agents.1.patch cmd-agents-capture.c > /build/expected
tar -tf /build/input.tar > /build/observed
cmp /build/expected /build/observed
tar -xf /build/input.tar -C /build/input --no-same-owner --no-same-permissions
test "$(find /build/input -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type f -printf '%f\n' | LC_ALL=C sort)" = "$(LC_ALL=C sort /build/expected)"
printf '%s  %s\n' "$SOURCE_SHA256" /build/input/tmux-3.6a.tar.gz | sha256sum -c -
printf '%s  %s\n' "$PATCH_SHA256" /build/input/tmux-3.6a-agents.1.patch | sha256sum -c -
printf '%s  %s\n' "$EXTENSION_SHA256" /build/input/cmd-agents-capture.c | sha256sum -c -
tar -xzf /build/input/tmux-3.6a.tar.gz -C /build/source
cp /build/input/cmd-agents-capture.c /build/source/tmux-3.6a/
patch --fuzz=0 -d /build/source/tmux-3.6a -p1 < /build/input/tmux-3.6a-agents.1.patch
cd /build/source/tmux-3.6a
YACC=true ./configure --disable-static
parser_before=$(sha256sum cmd-parse.c | cut -d ' ' -f1)
make -j1
parser_after=$(sha256sum cmd-parse.c | cut -d ' ' -f1)
test "$parser_before" = "$parser_after"
test "$(./tmux -V)" = 'tmux 3.6a-agents.1'
install -m 0755 ./tmux /build/tmux-3.6a-agents.1
bytes=$(stat -c %s /build/tmux-3.6a-agents.1)
hash=$(sha256sum /build/tmux-3.6a-agents.1 | cut -d ' ' -f1)
printf 'D007D_BUILD_V2\tbytes=%s\tsha256=%s\n' "$bytes" "$hash" >&2
cat /build/tmux-3.6a-agents.1 >&3
```

## Normative sequential checkpoint contracts

The checkpoints are serial candidate/review units, not parallel work packets. Let `C1`, `C2`,
and `C3` denote their implementation commits and `V1`, `V2`, and `V3` their independently
committed OK results. Checkpoint 2 starts only from a tree containing `V1` and the exact `C1`
tree authenticated by it; checkpoint 3 starts only from a tree containing `V2` and the exact
`C2` tree authenticated by it. No fixture, test, wrapper, or production edit for a later
checkpoint may be prepared, staged, or committed before the prior verdict is OK. A KO is
immutable and uses the next trial number for that same checkpoint.

Every checkpoint request freezes candidate commit/tree/parent, exact candidate pathset, RED and
GREEN evidence, gate totals/skips, and request parent. Its result is written by a fresh reviewer
session; the coder cannot issue it. `V1`, `V2`, and `V3` are intermediate review evidence only:
none is final `D_0_7D`, none satisfies this leaf, and none unblocks `D_0_1_SPLICE`.

suite then covers every row below with one exact result and records cause ordinal, interval, accepted-byte
knowledge, sideband response, port state, completion, descriptor closure, cleanup ledger, and
survivors. No production change beyond the recorded snapshot-cancel correction is initial;
another adapter change requires its own exact behavioral RED.

Before authoring the request, run authoritative `--focused` against `C3`. Its strict checkpoint-
1 parser emits canonical JSON. The request copies the full execution-order `names` array,
concrete integer `tests`, names SHA-256, TAP SHA-256, candidate SHA/tree, and zero fail/skip/
cancel counts with no placeholder or truncation. The author independently recomputes the hash
from each two-byte big-endian UTF-8 name length followed by the name bytes and checks
`names.length === tests === pass + fail + skipped + cancelled`; mismatch
blocks request creation. This committed handoff freezes the authoritative focused TAP name set
and exact total. The independent reviewer repeats the derivation from the frozen candidate.

**Acceptance checklist.**

- [ ] Every parent race row has one exact named result; the three mandatory tests and zero/
      partial/full-write-before-response cases are GREEN.
- [ ] Snapshot cancellation after `B` but before `R` returns exact `SESSION_PORT_CANCELLED`,
      discards the held payload, and does not alter the write or post-`R` rules. The authoritative
      real-host companion records the actual relay-barrier acknowledgement before cancellation;
      the construction seam is only the deterministic parent RED.
- [ ] Every product target is terminal `RETIRED` or `PRESERVED`, all retained fds close exactly
      once, and completion waits for the ledger rather than path absence.
- [ ] Relay namespace, replacements, exact process/pane/session outcomes, shared infrastructure,
      sibling session, and permitted survivors match the frozen parent.
- [ ] Outer teardown remains zero-path-mutation, including the final-entry race; only exact pidfd
      signalling and eventual namespace disposal occur.
- [ ] The focused manifest contains the full concrete names and exact total, reconciles with TAP,
      and has zero failure/skip/cancellation.
- [ ] The serial fresh full-CI replay passes with only canonical declared infrastructure skips
      and no D/0/07d skip.
- [ ] One-shot, Darwin seams, public services/adapters, and exact transaction remain unchanged
      unless a named RED proves otherwise.

**Verification commands.** The displayed cancellation and three-ordering-test commands are the
fixed inner argv. Run `cp3-unit-cancel` against committed `<C3-red>` for exact `1/0/1/0/0`, then
against `<C3>` for exact `1/1/0/0/0`; run `cp3-unit-ordering` against `<C3>` for exact
`3/3/0/0/0`. The complete nonempty ordered root-name set selected by the frozen
`^d007d race matrix: [A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9 _./:()=-]*$` pattern and its exact total are frozen in
the handoff and run through `cp3-unit-matrix` in one fresh authenticated serial invocation before
the final gate. Then run:

```text
env -i LC_ALL=C LANG=C TZ=UTC PATH=/usr/bin:/bin PYTHON AUTHORITY_PARENT run cp3-unit-cancel <red-odb> <C3-red> <C3-red-tree> <source> <toolchain> <node-modules> <docker> <checkpoint-3-red-expectation-ledger> <fresh-red-review-base>
env -i LC_ALL=C LANG=C TZ=UTC PATH=/usr/bin:/bin PYTHON AUTHORITY_PARENT run cp3-unit-cancel <green-odb> <C3> <C3-tree> <source> <toolchain> <node-modules> <docker> <checkpoint-3-cancel-green-expectation-ledger> <fresh-green-review-base>
env -i LC_ALL=C LANG=C TZ=UTC PATH=/usr/bin:/bin PYTHON AUTHORITY_PARENT run cp3-unit-ordering <fresh-odb> <C3> <C3-tree> <source> <toolchain> <node-modules> <docker> <checkpoint-3-ordering-expectation-ledger> <fresh-ordering-review-base>
env -i LC_ALL=C LANG=C TZ=UTC PATH=/usr/bin:/bin PYTHON AUTHORITY_PARENT run cp3-unit-matrix <fresh-odb> <C3> <C3-tree> <source> <toolchain> <node-modules> <docker> <checkpoint-3-matrix-expectation-ledger> <fresh-matrix-review-base>
env -i LC_ALL=C LANG=C TZ=UTC PATH=/usr/bin:/bin PYTHON AUTHORITY_PARENT run focused <fresh-odb> <C3> <C3-tree> <source> <toolchain> <node-modules> <docker> <checkpoint-3-focused-expectation-ledger> <fresh-focused-review-base>
env -i LC_ALL=C LANG=C TZ=UTC PATH=/usr/bin:/bin PYTHON AUTHORITY_PARENT run full-ci <fresh-odb> <C3> <C3-tree> <source> <toolchain> <node-modules> <docker> <checkpoint-3-full-ci-expectation-ledger> <fresh-full-ci-review-base>
git diff --check
```

Focused and full-CI are serial independent fresh instances; every prerequisite and skip rule
fails closed as in checkpoint 1. The checkpoint-3 handoff repeats the custody/parser digests and
complete candidate-blob table before freezing the final TAP names/count/hashes.

**Commit and independent review boundary.** Commit `C3` with exact subject
`test(gateway): complete D/0/07d real-host race gate (V5 D/0/07d CP3)`. Then use request subject
`docs(review): request D/0/07d checkpoint 3 review (V5 D/0/07d CP3 Trial 1)` and immutable
artifacts `plan/reviews/PROJECT_V5/D_0_7D_CHECKPOINT_3-1_to_review.md` and
`plan/reviews/PROJECT_V5/D_0_7D_CHECKPOINT_3-1_result.md`. A fresh independent reviewer verifies
the reviewed `C1 -> C2 -> C3` ancestry and final TAP manifest. Its OK remains checkpoint
evidence: it is not final `D_0_7D` and does not unblock `D_0_1_SPLICE`.

After checkpoint 3 is independently OK, create the separate complete-leaf request
`plan/reviews/PROJECT_V5/D_0_7D-1_to_review.md`. Only its fresh independent result at
`plan/reviews/PROJECT_V5/D_0_7D-1_result.md` may return final `D_0_7D` OK. That reviewer
authenticates all three reviewed candidate trees, replays the authoritative focused manifest and
serial full gate, and explicitly decides whether `D_0_1_SPLICE` may consume the gate. No
checkpoint verdict substitutes for it.

## Required real-host race matrix

The acceptance suite covers every mutually exclusive parent-table row and records the first
linearized cause, interval, bytes known accepted, sideband response state, public result, port
state, ordinary completion, and cleanup inventory. At minimum it proves:

- cancel before dispatch, cancel after dispatch with zero accepted bytes, cancel after a
  positive prefix, and cancel after the validated result;
- identity/foreground/terminal change before the first byte and after a positive prefix;
- PTY/relay close before the first byte and after a positive prefix;
- helper/control loss after zero, partial, and full terminal writes but before a valid response;
- cancel before snapshot public commit both before and after the relay barrier;
- terminal/relay/tmux loss, resize/identity failure, capture/decode failure, and helper/control
  loss before snapshot commit;
- valid snapshot commit before later cancel/loss;
- concurrent FIFO writes/snapshots and queued operations at revocation.

No trial may accept `may return`, multiple possible errors, or a skipped real-host lane.

The same real-host suite must make cleanup outcomes observable without converting preservation
into failure or deleting evidence:

- on an ordinary accepted generation, assert all retained descriptors closed, every cleanup
  target terminal, relay key `RETIRED`, and relay socket pathname/runtime directory
  `PRESERVED`;
- substitute same-name key/socket/directory entries before their cleanup points and assert zero
  post-`V` product `unlink`/`rmdir`, replacement survival, and `PRESERVED`;
- exercise the permitted process-survival and retained-tmux-connection-loss branches and assert
  unresolved exact targets `PRESERVED`, while exact retired targets are `RETIRED`;
- keep an unrelated sibling session on the shared tmux server and assert the sibling, server,
  and shared socket survive.

These three cases retain the exact parent names and are mandatory real-host composition tests:

| Named test | Required public result |
|---|---|
| `returns SESSION_PORT_WRITE_ABORTED when cancel is requested first and its proof response is lost` | exactly `SESSION_PORT_WRITE_ABORTED`; the post-`D` cancel request never received an ordinal |
| `returns SESSION_PORT_WRITE_ABORTED when the helper dies before F` | exactly `SESSION_PORT_WRITE_ABORTED`; helper/control loss outranks its teardown-induced unreadable identity |
| `returns SESSION_PORT_TERMINAL_CLOSED when the relay disappears during a snapshot` | exactly `SESSION_PORT_TERMINAL_CLOSED`; known relay close outranks terminal-identity and barrier-failure symptoms |

## Acceptance criteria

- [ ] All eleven CP1 authority rows use the request-frozen bare `fixedMode` under D7C2-v3;
      archive emits `[4,EOF]`, every reached test phase emits `[3 x N,1,2,EOF]`, and all dashed
      mode, missing TAP, summary-only, ledger-only, reordered, and expectation-exposure mutants
      reject.
- [ ] The frozen bwrap vector supplies exactly one
      `D007D_CANDIDATE_SHA=<bindings.candidateSha>` pair after `--clearenv`; D7C2 validates and
      frames only that value without any Git/`.git`/ODB lookup, while protocol-unset legacy
      behavior remains explicit and unreachable as fallback.
- [ ] CP1 implements distinct handlers for all eleven bare modes, including the frozen CP2 and
      CP3 patterns, and the RED 4 fake-runner oracle observes each handler frame its own mode.
      CP2/CP3 never edit the reviewed wrapper.
- [ ] The TAP coordinator treats stdin as bytes, rejects invalid/non-round-tripping UTF-8 and
      byte 2,097,153 before output, emits at most 64 codepoint-safe 32,768-byte chunks, preserves
      exact original bytes/hash, and rejects bailout, duplicate/inconsistent root records,
      nonterminal plan/summary, and every trailing TAP record or byte with zero evidence output.
- [ ] The CP1 request contains literal concrete Python/authority/ODB/source/toolchain/
      `node_modules`/Docker identities, eleven exact canonical no-LF mode-`0400` expectation
      ledgers with hashes, eleven distinct empty reviewer-owned mode-`0700` review bases, and
      eleven executable argv arrays with no unresolved operand; the candidate receives none of
      the expectation artifacts.
- [ ] The replacement-identity teardown test is observably RED against the pathname-based
      harness and GREEN with `PRESERVED`, zero outer-harness signal, zero outer-harness
      `kill-session`, zero outer-harness `kill-server`, zero outer-harness mutating tmux command,
      zero outer-harness unlink/unlinkat/rmdir/rename/recursive removal, and exact
      workspace/socket/server replacement survival after complete setup, startup failure, and
      teardown failure; the last-observation-to-effect race has the same zero-mutation oracle;
      RED uses recording operations and harms no real process or replacement.
- [ ] The GREEN fixture owner and outer real-host teardown never invoke a mutating tmux command
      or any path-deletion primitive on any branch. Dirfds are evidence-only; the directly
      spawned server is signalled only through its pidfd; retained fds close; and kernel disposal
      of transient tmpfs/mount namespace is the sole path reclamation after evidence. Frozen
      product cleanup may still retire an exactly sealed port-owned pane/session through its
      retained connection.
- [ ] The named real-host authorized transaction consolidates the complementary baseline
      characterizations with the exact write, 24-byte snapshot, observation, execution keys,
      configured absolute interpreter/repository-fixture argv, and literal metacharacter
      argument, without requiring a product change.
- [ ] The default persistent factory still composes only the independently reviewed `07a–c`
      mechanisms; no rule is reimplemented or weakened. A production path changes only for a
      separately recorded observable behavioral RED.
- [ ] Every write and snapshot race has one result/error from the mutually exclusive parent
      intervals and ranked physical-event classifier, including the three exact named cases
      above and partial/full-write-before-response helper/control loss.
- [ ] All capability, codec/tag, identity/foreground, relay handshake, exact `-N -T` capture,
      real tmux row-end-space, canonicalization, boundary, ANSI/UTF-8, cap, and truncation
      mutation guards pass together.
- [ ] No shell or tmux `send-keys` exists on the port path; tmux launches only the relay and the
      provider remains direct helper `execve`.
- [ ] Raw prompt/snapshot/ANSI/provider bytes never reach lifecycle JSON, transcript, readiness,
      errors, logs, audit, observation, completion, or tombstones.
- [ ] Cancellation/revocation closes all component-owned retained descriptors exactly once and
      moves every sealed cleanup target to terminal `RETIRED` or `PRESERVED` before
      exactly-once ordinary settlement; settlement does not require an empty namespace.
- [ ] The ordinary accepted path records the pre-`ACCEPT` consumed relay key `RETIRED`, while
      absent external removal the relay socket pathname and runtime directory persist through
      settlement as terminal `PRESERVED` entries; product/session-port cleanup makes zero
      post-`V` `unlink`/`rmdir` calls.
- [ ] Same-name replacement key/socket/directory entries survive as `PRESERVED`. Exact process
      and owned pane/session targets are each `RETIRED` or `PRESERVED`, every permitted survivor
      is recorded, and no arbitrary/later PID, PGID, public tmux target, or replacement is
      selected.
- [ ] The shared tmux server/socket and unrelated sibling sessions always survive. Isolated
      namespace disposal starts only after preservation evidence and retained-fd closure, is
      outside product/session-port cleanup, cannot satisfy acceptance, and grants no production
      janitor, retention, reuse, or deletion policy.
- [ ] The authoritative real-host parent accepts the source archive through a reviewer-held
      no-follow fd, exposes it to the candidate only as a kernel-read-only mount, and validates
      the returned exact three-member USTAR from the anonymous sealed parent-held archive memfd with the independent
      byte parser before Docker. Every malformed/trailing/duplicate/order mutation is rejected.
- [ ] Before any source, build, test, parser, validator, or evidence operation, the independently
      anchored custody parent creates and authenticates the no-hardlink detached candidate,
      holds all prerequisite identities, authenticates the exact unit peer/MainPID/cgroup and
      bwrap pidfd/argv before the sole fd-3 release, mounts output only by held fd 4, and executes
      the frozen fd-seal/close-range shim before candidate code. Candidate records and archive
      bytes cross exact closed-schema bounded sockets; evidence/output fds and paths stay
      parent-only. Parent timeout/kill/EOF/unit-absence/residue and post-run held-fd
      reauthentication gate `D007D_REVIEW_DONE_V3`.
- [ ] Every Docker CLI child crosses the one injected owner/runner/sink seam with no generic
      `subprocess.run` fallback or ambient environment. Its exact argv, seven-key private
      environment, monotonic interval, held live pidfd and one start token, terminal status,
      stdout/stderr count/hash, and normalized result are owner-observed; no numeric PID is later
      re-resolved and no caller summary can satisfy the observation.
- [ ] The parent uses the exact stream-native Docker probe/build argvs and image digest with
      parent-owned names/labels/held cidfiles, fixed memory/CPU/PID/ulimit/stop flags,
      `--pull never`, `--network none`, read-only root, exact non-root `1000:1000`, dropped capabilities,
      `no-new-privileges`, private empty Docker config, and container-private tmpfs only. Both
      tmpfs roots freeze `uid=1000`, `gid=1000`, and `mode=0700`; first write/exec succeeds and
      exact `65534:65534` access is denied. Normal success records exactly 20 ordered Docker
      operations; every failure uses bounded inspect/wait/TERM/KILL/remove reconciliation and
      two empty scans. It has
      no bind mount or host staging path, never invokes `build-offline.sh`, validates all three
      manifest hashes in-container, sends diagnostics only to stderr, and sends exactly raw
      verified binary bytes to stdout. The parent captures stdout through its held inode,
      rejects framing/trailing bytes, and validates/hashes/executes that fd rather than a
      replaceable pathname before and after the test.
- [ ] The test starts with an empty environment and uses private `HOME`, `TMPDIR`,
      `TMUX_TMPDIR`, tmux configuration, PID/mount/IPC/UTS/network namespaces, and resource
      limits.
- [ ] The authoritative real-host claim is Linux/amd64 only. Darwin remains a deterministic
      seam regression and this leaf makes no Darwin real-host claim.
- [ ] The authoritative lane runs with test concurrency one, no provider credentials, no
      network, no inherited/shared tmux socket or service, and no host-root `/fixture` or ambient
      `/tmp/never`. Missing isolation or custom-tmux prerequisites fail the lane rather than
      recording a skip.
- [ ] The real-host gate mounts the fresh verified custom binary as `/inputs/tmux` and jointly
      sets the absolute directory `D007C_TEST_TMUX_PATH=/inputs`,
      `D007C_RUN_REAL_TMUX_PROBE=1`, and
      `D007C_TMUX_SOCKET_NAME=d007c-control-probe`. The authenticated Checkpoint 1 RED derives
      and freezes the complete ordered root-TAP test-name manifest, count, and canonical digest;
      GREEN must reproduce that same manifest and count with every test passing and zero
      skip/cancel. This design inherits no numeric `80/80` expectation. A run missing only the
      socket-name variable fails at `join-pane` and is recorded as false setup RED, never product
      evidence.
- [ ] One-shot execution and all existing supervisor/Darwin suites remain unchanged.
- [ ] No service/catalog/public-contract, dependency/package/release-manifest, workflow,
      lockfile, suite-contract, or CI-script change enters the candidate. The only CI metadata
      change is checkpoint 1's exact `ci/suites.json:test.gateway.inventorySha256` refresh from
      `f6bf…922e` (126 paths) to `1dd0…431d` (127 paths); `lint.gateway` remains
      `854b…e7cd4` and no other suite value changes. An adapter enters only when its
      checkpoint handoff records the mandatory focused behavioral RED requiring the smallest
      production correction.
- [ ] The independent review records the exact candidate tree and explicitly states whether
      `D_0_1_SPLICE` may consume the reviewed real-host acceptance gate.

## Verifiable local gate

Record the injected-operation replacement test RED on the declared baseline before changing the
harness. Also record the two complementary GREEN baseline characterizations—the exact 24-byte
render and the combined write-sequence-1/snapshot-sequence-2 path—without claiming that the
single exact authorized transaction already exists or relabelling its fixture assembly as a
product RED.

Every evidence-bearing RED and GREEN starts through the independently anchored custody parent; the
displayed Node commands in the checkpoint sections are the exact inner argv selected by its
fixed unit modes, never ambient commands that can count as review evidence. The custody parent and
candidate wrapper then perform these operations in this order:

1. before candidate execution or evidence creation, the parent authenticates explicit commit/
   tree and every prerequisite, creates the no-hardlink detached materialization, opens root/
   critical-file no-follow fds, proves clean blobs, then exclusive-creates parent-only evidence
   and output inodes; neither pathname nor fd enters candidate argv/env/mounts;
2. the parent starts the exact no-collect/remain-after-exit transient unit shim, authenticates
   direct-child/MainPID/peer/cgroup/bwrap pidfds and argv while bwrap is blocked on fd 3, sends
   only the exact SCM_RIGHTS allowlist, binds the held output with `--ro-bind-fd 4`, then sends
   the exact release command so the guarded shim writes the sole release byte. The final frozen shim sets stdin to `/dev/null`, executes
   `close_range(3,UINT_MAX,CLOSE_RANGE_UNSHARE)`, and only then execs candidate code;
3. the candidate wrapper emits exact D7C2/3 `[4,EOF]` archive or
   `[3 x N,1,2,EOF]` test records. The parent enforces canonical schemas, expectation-ledger
   relationships, bounded EOF, and independently validates every USTAR header/payload/padding/end
   byte from its anonymous held archive memfd before Docker and rejects candidate/parser
   disagreement;
4. the parent alone performs the exact named/cidfile-bound other-identity tmpfs probe and build,
   each with fixed resource/ulimit/stop flags and the exact inspect/wait/remove/two-scan lifecycle.
   It streams the accepted held archive and captures raw output into its held output inode. It
   rejects absent/duplicate/non-final status, size/hash mismatch, non-ELF/non-Linux/amd64 output,
   wrong held type/mode/owner/link count/version/extension, or container/host-staging residue.
   The pathname recipe is never spawned, sourced, or changed;
5. dependencies are only the explicit read-only `D007D_GATE_TOOLCHAIN_ROOT` (Python, Node, npm,
   ruff, pytest, `agent-run`, and runtime libraries) and
   `D007D_GATEWAY_NODE_MODULES` matching the candidate lockfile. Their realpaths, ownership,
   modes, versions, lockfile hashes, and deterministic dependency inventory are recorded;
   missing, writable, root-escaping, network-fetched, or candidate-produced dependencies fail;
6. build-bearing modes launch Linux/amd64 through the exact reviewer-only seqpacket unit shim,
   `systemd-run --user --wait --remain-after-exit` without `--collect`, and `bwrap` private
   mount/PID/network/IPC/UTS namespaces, `--block-fd 3`, `--die-with-parent`, a new session, explicit `/proc`, private
   `HOME`/`TMPDIR`/`TMUX_TMPDIR`, no tmux configuration, and only the frozen empty-environment
   inputs. No candidate process receives a Docker client, helper, user bus, or socket;
7. the wrapper invokes the materialized validator/test/`scripts/ci.sh` by absolute path, mounts
   the fresh verified binary as `/inputs/tmux`, sets its containing absolute directory as
   `D007C_TEST_TMUX_PATH`, and parses only its one serial
   child. `--focused` owns one exact `node --test --test-concurrency=1` invocation containing
   the process-supervisor session-port, PTY, and relay suites. `--full-ci` runs canonical
   `bash scripts/ci.sh` in a separate fresh instance with only the authorized inventory digest
   refresh. Every real-host inner gate jointly sets
   `D007C_TEST_TMUX_PATH=/inputs`,
   `D007C_RUN_REAL_TMUX_PROBE=1`, and
   `D007C_TMUX_SOCKET_NAME=d007c-control-probe`; all three values are root-verified together and
   Checkpoint 1 freezes the independently parsed RED root-TAP ordered name manifest, count, and
   digest. GREEN must have the same names/count/digest basis, all passing, zero skipped, and zero
   cancelled; no pre-Checkpoint numeric total is authoritative. Omitting only the socket-name
   variable is a false RED at tmux `join-pane`, not admissible behavioral RED evidence;
8. every mode enforces the reviewer-owned 900-second wall timeout, domain TERM/KILL and residue
   protocol; every build-bearing mode also enforces a 600-second CPU limit, 2-GiB
   address-space limit, 256-MiB file-size limit, 256-descriptor limit, 1-GiB writable tmpfs,
   `TasksMax=128`, and `MemoryMax=2G`. Missing user-manager/cgroup/bwrap/Docker/image/source/
   toolchain/dependency support, ineffective limits, timeout, or a skip is a hard failure. The
   candidate-and-nonce unit and parent-owned container names/labels/cidfiles must have zero residue; and
9. after exact EOF and zero-domain proof, the parent validates D7C2 records, re-reads every held
   prerequisite/candidate fd, re-resolves clean Git state, validates/hashes/executes the held
   output inode, unlinks only the exact reviewer-runtime allowlist and removes `REVIEW_ROOT`,
   then appends `D007D_REVIEW_DONE_V3` to the unlinked held evidence inode, `fdatasync`s,
   self-parses, obtains the exact artifact receipt, closes held fds, and proves root absence twice.
   Every failure, skip, cancellation, retained fd/
   child, timeout, identity drift, pathname replacement, or residue suppresses that record.

For each checkpoint, substitute its frozen SHA/tree into separate fresh invocations of the
exact V4 `run` argv. The final candidate gate is exactly:

```text
env -i LC_ALL=C LANG=C TZ=UTC PATH=/usr/bin:/bin PYTHON AUTHORITY_PARENT run bootstrap-probe <odb> <candidate> <tree> <source> <toolchain> <node-modules> <docker> <bootstrap-expectation-ledger> <bootstrap-review-base>
env -i LC_ALL=C LANG=C TZ=UTC PATH=/usr/bin:/bin PYTHON AUTHORITY_PARENT run docker-integration <fresh-odb> <candidate> <tree> <source> <toolchain> <node-modules> <docker> <docker-expectation-ledger> <docker-review-base>
env -i LC_ALL=C LANG=C TZ=UTC PATH=/usr/bin:/bin PYTHON AUTHORITY_PARENT run focused <fresh-odb> <candidate> <tree> <source> <toolchain> <node-modules> <docker> <focused-expectation-ledger> <focused-review-base>
env -i LC_ALL=C LANG=C TZ=UTC PATH=/usr/bin:/bin PYTHON AUTHORITY_PARENT run full-ci <fresh-odb> <candidate> <tree> <source> <toolchain> <node-modules> <docker> <full-ci-expectation-ledger> <full-ci-review-base>
git diff --check
```

The intentional full-CI replay proves the focused candidate under the complete repository gate.
The absolute materialized validator is a hard preflight inside every non-bootstrap mode.
No mode runs a repository byte or test against ambient worktree resources. A local coder may
observe an earlier non-authoritative unit failure while drafting, but only the custody-parent-owned
RED-commit execution named in the checkpoint request is admissible RED evidence.

Evidence names the exact candidate tree, configured Python, custom-tmux input/output hashes and
version, namespace, resource limits, and every failure/skip. It must show all component-owned
retained descriptors closed; every cleanup target terminal `RETIRED` or `PRESERVED`; the
ordinary accepted ledger with relay key `RETIRED` and relay socket pathname/runtime directory
`PRESERVED` absent external removal; zero post-`V` product `unlink`/`rmdir`; same-name
replacement survival; explicit `PRESERVED` outcomes for permitted process/owned-tmux survivors;
and survival of the isolated tmux server/socket and sibling session. Preservation/replacement
evidence is recorded before retained-fd closure; the owner's closure confirmation is durably
appended before namespace disposal. The fixture owner performs no path cleanup; kernel disposal
of the private tmpfs/mount namespace is the sole reclamation and remains outside product/session-
port cleanup. It cannot satisfy acceptance and creates no production janitor, retention, reuse,
or deletion policy. Only an independent
`D_0_7D` OK unblocks `D_0_1_SPLICE`; this plan does not claim that OK in advance.

The focused wrapper starts no Redis, PostgreSQL, provider, Gateway, or shared service. The
independent `--full-ci` replay preserves the repository's existing declared infrastructure skip
contract, starts no shared service, and never connects to a shared host endpoint; no D/0/07d
test may skip. Hosted workflow provisioning remains outside this leaf, so this is local
candidate-verification evidence, not integration, promotion, remote-CI, release, or production
cleanup-policy evidence.
